This assumption is overly coarse for broad and complex target concepts, since a concept often contains multiple latent semantic prototypes involving different objects, scenes, or relations, and requires different local erasure directions. A single LoRA update averages these heterogeneous erasure demands, leading to under-erasure on difficult prototypes and over-editing of nearby benign semantics. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Core LoRA, a distribution-aware low-rank adaptation framework. It fits a Gaussian mixture model in the prompt feature space to estimate latent semantic prototypes within the target concept. During inference, each input prompt is projected into this feature space to compute its Gaussian posterior responsibilities, which condition the core generator to produce a prompt-specific, norm-bounded residual reconfiguration of the shared LoRA rank space. This enables prototype-adaptive erasure with a single lightweight adapter. Compared with the strongest baseline on each metric, Gaussian Core LoRA reduces average Attack Success Rate (ASR) by 7.95%, lowers COCO Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) by 14.72%, and improves CLIP Score by 4.98%. Further experiments show robustness to adversarial prompts, scalability to multi-identity and multi-style erasure, and compatibility with SDXL and FLUX.

</details>


### [104] [RadMatch: Auditable Radiology Report Evaluation via Finding-Level Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01470)
*Charles Corbière,Léo Machado,Aubin Charley,Baptiste Callard,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RadMatch，一种多阶段、基于LLM的放射学报告评估指标，将报告比较分解为结构化发现级匹配，并通过7个临床属性维度进行显著性加权评分和错误表征，输出可解释、可审计的可操作错误计数，在两个专家基准上达成了与放射科医生间一致性最佳匹配的临床对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的放射学报告评估指标虽然与放射科医生判断的相关性最佳，但仅输出单一不透明分数，临床医生和模型构建者均难以解释和审计其评估结果。

Method: RadMatch采用多阶段LLM评估框架：将报告比较分解为结构化发现级匹配，并进行显著性感知评分；在七个临床属性维度（状态、位置、严重程度、形态、确定性、纵向比较和测量）上进行错误表征；主要评分为可解释且可审计的可操作错误计数；候选发现分为正确、部分或错误，未匹配发现计为遗漏或幻觉；同时提供分诊、可操作安全召回率/精确率及子集视图作为补充。

Result: 在两个专家基准上，RadMatch是临床对齐度最高的指标：在ReXVal上与放射科医生间一致性持平，在更难的RadEvalExpert上比最佳先前指标的提升超过一倍。

Conclusion: RadMatch仅依赖少样本提示即可实现高度临床对齐的评估，具有可解释性和可审计性，且设计上可扩展到其他模态和解剖部位；代码将开源并配套交互式仪表盘。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly used to draft radiology reports, reliably evaluating their clinical quality remains a critical challenge. Large language model (LLM)-based metrics are now the best-correlated with radiologist judgment, yet they output a single opaque score that neither a clinician nor a model builder can easily interpret or audit. We introduce RadMatch, a multi-stage, LLM-based metric that decomposes report comparison into a structured finding-level matching with significance-aware scoring and error characterization across seven clinical attribute dimensions (status, location, severity, morphology, certainty, longitudinal comparison, and measurement). The main score is the actionable-error count, both interpretable and auditable. Candidate findings are graded correct, partial, or incorrect, and unmatched findings are counted as missed or hallucinated. Triage and actionable safety recall/precision and per-subset views add complementary, deployment-oriented lenses. Across two expert benchmarks, RadMatch is the most clinically aligned metric, matching inter-radiologist agreement on ReXVal and more than doubling the best prior metric on the harder RadEvalExpert. Relying only on few-shot prompting, it is designed to extend to other modalities and anatomies. We will release RadMatch as open-source code with an interactive dashboard for inspecting results.

</details>


### [105] [CameraEditor: Camera-Controlled Image Editing via Video-Prior Sequential Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01479)
*Xin Shen,Chengyou Jia,Keshuo Xing,Zifeng Zhu,Changliang Xia,Bowen Ping,Zhuohang Dang,Hangwei Qian,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CameraEditor框架，将相机控制的图像编辑问题从空间维度重构为时序序列预测任务，利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性实现精确的相机参数控制编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型擅长语义和风格操作，但在显式相机参数控制方面表现不佳。面对大视角变化时，指令驱动模型要么产生结构撕裂，要么生成忽略几何指令的保守输出。

Method: 1) 将相机控制编辑问题重构为时间序列预测任务；2) 利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性；3) 集成显式几何感知模块与动态参考路由机制；4) 通过动态全景裁剪构建几何严谨的视觉参考对；5) 插入中间过渡帧以分解大视角变化，提供时间缓冲以保持内容身份和空间一致性。

Result: 构建了5,760个训练实例数据集，并独立贡献了CamEditor-Bench评估套件（462个测试用例）。实验证明CameraEditor在相机控制精度和源身份保持方面达到最先进水平，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CameraEditor通过将空间问题转译为时间序列预测，有效解决了相机控制编辑中的结构撕裂与几何指令忽略问题，实现了精确的相机控制编辑与良好的内容保真。

Abstract: Beyond semantic content, camera parameters play a pivotal role in dictating the geometric perspective and appearance of any given image. While recent image editing models excel at semantic and stylistic manipulation, they struggle with explicit camera parameter control. When handling large perspective shifts, instruction-driven models face a dilemma: they either suffer from structural tearing or generate conservative outputs that ignore geometric instructions. To address this, we introduce CameraEditor, a framework that reformulates camera-controlled editing from a spatial problem into a temporal sequence prediction task. By leveraging the temporal coherence of video diffusion models, our approach integrates an explicit geometric perception module with a dynamic reference routing mechanism. This allows us to construct geometrically rigorous visual reference pairs via dynamic panorama cropping, overcoming the ambiguity of text-based instructions. Furthermore, CameraEditor strategically inserts intermediate transition frames to decompose large perspective shifts, providing a robust temporal buffer that preserves content identity and spatial coherence. We construct a training dataset of 5,760 instances. As an independent contribution, we introduce CamEditor-Bench, a model-agnostic evaluation suite of 462 test cases. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CameraEditor achieves state-of-the-art camera control precision and source identity preservation, outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [106] [Benchmarking Spatial, Spectral, and Self-Supervised Cues for Face Forgery Detection under Realistic Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01511)
*Lucas Cunha,Lucas Sotomaior,Lucas Gasperin,Beatriz Caldas,Eduardo Pianovski,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于MFFI数据集的人脸伪造检测标准化基准，评估六个模型族在干净和退化测试集上的表现。结果表明干净集性能不能预测退化鲁棒性，冻结的DINOv3主干在线性分类头下取得最佳退化集性能（0.726 ROC-AUC），而Xception在干净集最佳（0.884）。傅里叶域线索需与RGB结合才有效，自监督视觉表征是鲁棒人脸伪造检测的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸伪造检测器在受控基准上表现优异，但在真实图像退化（压缩、缩放、模糊）条件下的可靠性有限，缺乏系统的标准化评估框架。

Method: 使用MFFI数据集构建标准化基准，分别评估干净和退化测试分区。比较六个模型族（卷积网络、Transformer、冻结DINOv3自监督骨干），在空间、频谱和混合输入表征上全面验证。

Result: Xception+RGB在干净集优于0.884 ROC-AUC但退化严重；冻结DINOv3在退化集最优（0.726 ROC-AUC）且仅训练线性头。纯频谱输入一致低于空间表征，傅里叶线索与RGB结合最有效。注意力图显示卷积关注局部伪影而DINOv3利用更广的面部结构。

Conclusion: 干净集性能不能作为鲁棒性的可靠指标，需要设计退化评估协议；自监督视觉表征是推动鲁棒人脸伪造检测的重要方向。

Abstract: Face forgery detectors often achieve strong results on controlled benchmarks, but their reliability under realistic image degradations remains limited. This paper presents a standardized benchmark for face forgery detection using the Multi-Dimensional Face Forgery Image (MFFI) dataset and evaluates performance on both clean and degraded test partitions. We compare six model families, including convolutional networks, transformer-based models, and a frozen self-supervised DINOv3 backbone, across spatial, spectral, and hybrid input representations. The results show that clean-set performance is not a reliable indicator of robustness under compression, resizing, and blurring. Xception with RGB obtains the best clean performance, reaching 0.884 mean ROC-AUC, but degrades substantially on the harder partition. In contrast, frozen DINOv3 achieves the strongest degraded-set result, with 0.726 mean ROC-AUC, while training only a linear classification head. The representation analysis indicates that Fourier-domain cues are most useful when combined with RGB information, whereas purely spectral inputs consistently underperform spatial representations. Qualitative attribution maps further suggest that convolutional detectors focus on localized artifacts, while DINOv3 relies on broader facial structure. These findings reinforce the need for degraded evaluation protocols and highlight self-supervised visual representations as a promising direction for robust face forgery detection. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/lucasdocunha/FaceForgery-Benchmark/.

</details>


### [107] [TempCloze: Can Video-LLMs Identify the Missing Middle?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01515)
*Wenqi Pei,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Yilai Liu,Jiahao Meng,Han Chen,Ziyu Wang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出TempCloze，一个视频完形填空基准，用于评估Video-LLMs的视觉时间推理能力，通过隐藏视频中间片段并要求模型从四个候选中选出正确的中间内容，消除了语言捷径的影响。实验发现时间对齐是模型的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时间推理基准往往依赖语言中介，模型可以通过选项措辞、答案相关性或语言先验等捷径来作答，无法真正评估模型的视觉时间推理能力。

Method: 构建TempCloze基准：给定视频首尾片段，模型需从四个候选中选出真实缺失的中间片段。包含1,521个精心筛选的视频（主要来自长镜头和第一人称视频），从语义（应该发生什么）、对齐（何时发生）和进展（如何展开）三个维度构建同源干扰项，并共享场景与物体以减少外观线索。

Result: 对10个专有和21个开源Video-LLMs的评估表明，对齐维度是主要瓶颈：模型通常能识别合理的语义内容和局部事件进展，但在时间对齐上表现困难。此外对四个代表模型进行了错误模式和敏感性分析（候选顺序、上下文方向、可见跨度、帧密度和测试时缩放的影响）。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前Video-LLMs在视觉时间推理中时间对齐能力的不足，为未来模型在细粒度时间理解方面的改进提供了诊断性基准和分析。

Abstract: Temporal reasoning benchmarks for Video-LLMs are often mediated by language, leaving room for linguistic shortcuts from option wording, answer correlations, or language priors. To reduce such shortcuts, we introduce TempCloze, a video cloze benchmark for evaluating visual temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs. Given the beginning and ending clips of a video, models must identify the true missing middle from four candidates. TempCloze contains 1,521 carefully filtered videos from seven sources, mainly long-take and egocentric videos. We construct same-source distractors along three dimensions: Semantic asks what event should happen, Alignment probes when it should occur, and Progression tests how it should unfold, while shared scenes and objects reduce appearance cues. Our evaluation of 10 proprietary and 21 open-source Video-LLMs reveals Alignment as the primary bottleneck: models often recognize plausible semantic content and local event progression but struggle with temporal alignment. We further conduct error pattern and behavioral sensitivity analyses on TempCloze-Mixed and TempCloze-Hard with four representative models to examine where errors arise and how candidate order, context direction, visible span, frame density, and test-time scaling influence model choices.

</details>


### [108] [DualDiff3D: Dual Structure-Appearance Diffusion Priors for Reliability-Enhanced 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01516)
*Qian Wang,Yu Wang,Weiqi Li,Xinhua Cheng,Xiandong Meng,Ronggang Wang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DualDiff，一种利用双扩散先验和结构-外观注意力(SAA)模块来引导细化低质量新视角渲染的方法，并构建了集成Render-Refine-Optimize(RRO)循环的DualDiff3D重建框架，在输入视图受限场景下显著提升3DGS重建质量和新视角合成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在输入视图有限时重建质量差且渲染存在伪影。现有方法利用扩散先验时将渲染视图与参考视图沿额外维度拼接送入单一网络，忽视了不同视图应保持外观相似但结构因视角变化而不同的内在特性，导致属性冲突产生模糊。

Method: 提出双扩散分支架构：(1)一个分支专注从低质量新视角中提取结构信息；(2)另一个分支确保与参考视图的外观一致性。两者通过Structure-Appearance Attention (SAA)模块进行交互。同时构建DualDiff3D框架，集成可靠性增强的Render-Refine-Optimize (RRO)循环，逐步稳健地将细化后的新视角引入3DGS优化。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在仅推理(inference-only)设置下即优于现有最先进方法，通过训练还能获得进一步性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将结构提取与外观一致性解耦到不同扩散分支，有效解决了单网络处理时结构-外观属性冲突导致的模糊问题，为稀疏视角3DGS重建提供了更优方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis, scenarios with limited input views often lead to poor reconstruction quality and artifacts in rendered novel views. Recent efforts attempt to utilize powerful diffusion priors, yet they typically process rendered and reference views concatenated along an additional dimension in a single network. These methods overlook an inherent nature that different views should maintain appearance similarity but differ in structure due to view shifts, leading to blur caused by conflicts between the two properties. In this paper, we propose DualDiff, a novel pipeline that leverages dual diffusion priors with a Structure-Appearance Attention (SAA) module to introduce reference guidance for refining low-quality novel views rendered from flawed 3D representations. Specifically, we retain one diffusion branch to focus on extracting structural information from the low-quality novel views, while introducing another branch to ensure appearance consistency with reference views. Furthermore, we present a 3D reconstruction framework named DualDiff3D, which integrates a reliability-enhanced Render-Refine-Optimize (RRO) loop to progressively and robustly incorporate the refined novel views, yielding more accurate 3DGS. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods even in the inference-only setting, with further performance gains achievable through training. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/Akaneqwq/DualDiff3D.

</details>


### [109] [Revisiting Cross-View Completion: Self-Supervised Pre-Training via Reconstruction Error Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01530)
*Thibaut Loiseau,Guillaume Bourmaud,Vincent Lepetit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gekko是一种自监督预训练网络，通过联合执行跨视角补全、掩码自编码以及预测二者的相对误差改善（作为共视性代理），将非共视区域的单目训练信号转化为额外双目信号，在零样本对应估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归等3D视觉任务上一致优于CroCo，且在ETH3D上端点误差降低22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨视角补全自监督预训练（如CroCo）依赖图像对中共视区域来学习3D特征，但对于非共视区域的补全，参考视角提供的信息极少，本质上退化为单目训练信号，这些区域的潜力未被充分利用。

Method: Gekko通过计算跨视角补全误差与掩码自编码误差的相对改善量，将其作为共视性的自监督代理指标。网络联合训练三个任务：跨视角补全、掩码自编码，以及对每个像素预测该相对改善量，从而为所有掩码区域提供额外的双目监督信号，全程无需3D真值标注。此外还支持从原始视频以简单的步长课程训练，替代繁琐的3D预处理。

Result: 在相同架构和训练数据下，Gekko在零样本对应估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归上一致优于CroCo，最严格相对位姿阈值下准确率提升达6倍，ETH3D上端点误差降低22%。其学习到的额外通道在未见场景上本身就是一个强共视检测器，冻结特征优于同等或更大规模的已发布跨视角骨干网络。

Conclusion: Gekko将跨视角补全的局限性（非共视区域的单目信号）转化为有价值的双目标注，有效提升了3D视觉任务的预训练特征质量，且能直接从原始视频训练，减少了对3D预处理的依赖，为自监督3D预训练提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training via cross-view completion learns strong features for 3D vision from co-visible regions of image pairs. However, the reference view provides little information for reconstructing non-co-visible patches, implicitly yielding a monocular training signal in these regions. We introduce Gekko, which turns this limitation into a useful signal. The relative improvement of the cross-view reconstruction error over a masked-autoencoder error is a self-supervised proxy for co-visibility: large improvements indicate co-visible regions, negligible ones non-co-visible areas. Gekko is a network, trained from scratch, that jointly performs cross-view completion, masked autoencoding, and per-pixel prediction of this relative improvement, providing an additional binocular signal for all masked regions without any ground-truth 3D annotation. Under identical architectures and training data, Gekko consistently outperforms CroCo on zero-shot correspondence estimation, relative pose estimation, and pointmap regression, with up to 6 times higher accuracy at the strictest relative-pose threshold and a 22% drop in end-point error on ETH3D. The extra channel it learns is itself a strong co-visibility detector on unseen scenes, and Gekko's frozen features outperform released cross-view backbones of comparable or larger size. It can also be trained directly from raw videos with a simple stride-based curriculum, removing the cumbersome 3D preprocessing prior methods require while matching models trained on curated data. Code and pre-trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [110] [What, Where, and How: Probing Spatiotemporal Representations in Video Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01551)
*Sharon S. Musa,Fereshteh Forghani,Harrish Thasarathan,Sonia Joseph,Matthew Kowal,Konstantinos G. Derpanis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过逐层系统分析V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2两种视频基础模型，揭示其编码的时空表征内容、各层出现的视觉概念及几何组织结构。研究发现模型擅长编码相机运动（60-70%深度层达到>90% ROC AUC），中等异常检测能力（>60% AUC），但对直觉物理理解接近随机水平。进一步发现单个视频的时间特征形成平滑低维轨迹，并据此提出基于样条插值的几何感知操作方法来生成更连贯的运动插值视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视频基础模型虽然学习到丰富的时空表征，但这些表征具体编码了哪些视觉概念、这些概念在transformers各层中如何分布、以及它们在几何空间中如何组织，这些问题仍然不清楚。理解这些问题有助于更好地解释和改进视频模型。

Method: 对V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2进行逐层分析，使用轻量级探针发现三类时间属性：相机运动理解、直觉物理和异常检测。同时分析视频个体时间特征在表征空间中形成的轨迹结构，并基于此提出几何感知的样条插值方法操纵潜在表征以插值相机运动。

Result: 两种模型都在60-70%网络深度处最佳地编码相机运动（>90% ROC AUC），异常检测达到中等性能（>60% ROC AUC），但在直觉物理任务上接近随机水平。时间特征形成平滑的低维轨迹，相机运动可线性解码且几何上有组织。提出的样条插值方法生成的视频比线性插值具有更平滑的轨迹和更连贯的时间进展。

Conclusion: 视频基础模型在中间层最能编码低层运动属性如相机运动，但缺乏深层物理推理能力；时间表征在几何空间中以低维轨迹形式结构化组织，利用该几何结构可以进行更有效的表征编辑和视频插值操作。

Abstract: Self-supervised video foundation models learn rich spatiotemporal representations, yet it remains unclear what visual concepts these representations encode, where they emerge across transformer layers, and how they are geometrically organized. In this work, we tackle these three questions through a systematic layer-wise analysis of V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAE-v2. We leverage lightweight probes trained to discover three temporally grounded properties: (i) camera motion understanding, (ii) intuitive physics, and (iii) anomaly detection. Both models encode camera motion, with best results ($>90$ ROC AUC) emerging at 60-70% of network depth, and achieve moderate anomaly detection performance ($>60$ ROC AUC), but remain near chance on intuitive-physics tasks, suggesting a limited encoding of deeper physical reasoning. Beyond classification, we find that temporal features from individual videos form smooth low-dimensional trajectories in representation space, suggesting that camera motion is not only linearly decodable but also geometrically organized. Based on these results, we apply geometry-aware spline-based steering in the model's latent representations to interpolate camera motion, yielding steered videos with smoother trajectories and more coherent temporal progression than linear interpolation.

</details>


### [111] [BS: Take the Hint - Interactive Multitracer PET/CT Lesion Segmentation with a Scribble-Conditioned ResEnc U-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01554)
*Marven Sherif,Amgad Elmasry,Youssef Ghazal,Ayman Elghotni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带涂鸦条件（scribble-conditioned）的残差编码器U-Net用于全身PET/CT中的交互式病灶分割。网络以autoPET-III获胜权重初始化，扩展为四输入通道（CT、PET、前景涂鸦和背景涂鸦），PET强度通过CT分割得到的主动脉血池参考进行归一化以消除示踪剂和中心差异。推理时对五个折模型进行滑动窗口软最大输出平均集成。五折验证中，无涂鸦平均Dice为0.554，经过五轮修正后升至0.751；约85%的增益来自第一笔涂鸦，且交互大幅缩小了不同折模型间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT中的自动化病灶分割因生理性示踪剂摄取模式的多样性和不同示踪剂下病灶外观的差异而复杂化。autoPET/CT V挑战通过引入交互式分割（用户提供前景和背景涂鸦作为条件输入）来应对这一挑战，本文旨在探索如何有效利用这些涂鸦信息提升分割性能。

Method: 提出一种涂鸦条件的残差编码器U-Net，输入四通道：CT、PET、前景和背景稀疏涂鸦图。网络从autoPET-III获胜权重初始化并扩展输入通道（涂鸦通道零初始化以保持预训练表示）。每个折的模型从对应autoPET-III折检查点微调。PET强度根据CT分割导出的主动脉血池参考进行每扫描归一化。推理时对五个折模型的软最大输出按滑动窗口块进行平均集成，再进行高斯加权拼接。

Result: 在挑战的五折划分上（每折在自己的验证集上评估），无涂鸦时平均Dice为0.554，病灶级F1为0.528；进行五轮修正后分别提升至0.751和0.733。约85%的增益来自第一笔涂鸦，且各折模型间的性能差距在交互轮次中缩小了五倍，表明交互在很大程度上弥补了模型无辅助分割能力的差异。

Conclusion: 交互式涂鸦线索能够显著提升全身PET/CT病灶分割的准确性，且交互的收益具有"早期集中"特点——首笔涂鸦贡献了绝大部分增益。同时，交互机制有效缓解了不同模型个体性能差异带来的不确定性，使分割结果更加稳健。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT is complicated by the variety of physiological tracer uptake patterns and by the differing appearance of lesions across tracers. The autoPET/CT V challenge addresses this by making segmentation interactive: user scribbles marking foreground and background are supplied alongside the image, and the algorithm is expected to exploit them. We present our submission, a scribble-conditioned residual encoder U-Net operating on four input channels: CT, PET, and a sparse scribble map for each of foreground and background. The network is initialised from the autoPET-III winning weights and extended from two to four input channels, with the two scribble channels zero-initialised so that the pretrained representation is preserved exactly at initialisation. Every model is fine-tuned per fold from the corresponding autoPET-III fold checkpoint, so that no validation case is seen during pretraining. PET intensities are normalised against a per-scan aorta blood-pool reference derived from a CT segmentation, which removes tracer- and centre-specific scaling without requiring lesion labels. At inference the five fold models are ensembled by averaging their softmax outputs per sliding-window patch, before Gaussian-weighted stitching. On the challenge's five-fold split, with each fold evaluated on its own validation cases, mean Dice is 0.554 and mean lesion-level F1 is 0.528 without scribbles, rising to 0.751 and 0.733 after five correction rounds. About 85% of that gain follows the first scribble, and the spread between fold models narrows five-fold over the same rounds, so interaction largely compensates for how well or badly a given model segments unaided.

</details>


### [112] [H3-World: Turning Language Understanding into World Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01560)
*Danze Chen,Zeqing Wang,Ziyue Lin,Xingyi Yang,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H3-World 是一个高效框架，将33B参数的MiniMax-H3视频生成器转化为交互式世界模型。它利用自然语言作为控制接口，将粗粒度的语言指令转化为精确、时间对齐的世界控制，仅需8000个游戏样本、10000步LoRA优化和0.199%的可训练参数即可实现有效的角色与相机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频生成器本身已具备通过自然语言指令进行零样本行为与镜头控制的能力，但这些控制是粗粒度的，缺乏时间上的精确性。作者希望将这种新兴的语言控制能力高效转化为精确、时间可对齐的交互式世界控制，而无需引入专门的动作模块。

Method: 1) 将每个动作表示为角色指令与相机指令的结构化组合，并与相应的时序视频潜变量对齐；2) 引入时间注意力路由（temporal attention routing）机制，限制每条指令作用于其预期的时间区间，减少不同动作间的控制泄漏；3) 直接复用大规模视频预训练中学到的语义表征，仅需轻量级适应（LoRA微调）。

Result: 仅使用8,000个游戏样本、10,000步LoRA优化和0.199%的可训练参数，H3-World实现了有效的角色与相机控制，同时保持良好的生成质量，并能泛化到未见过的场景。

Conclusion: 研究证明，大型视频生成器中涌现的控制能力可以被有效且高效地转化为交互式世界控制，语言正成为视频生成器自然、可扩展的控制接口。

Abstract: We present H3-World, an efficient framework that turns the 33B MiniMax-H3 video generator into an interactive world model. Our key finding is that, as large video generators become more capable, language is emerging as a natural interface for control. MiniMax-H3, for example, already supports zero-shot control of character behavior and camera motion through natural-language instructions. Building on this, H3-World turns this coarse language interface into precise, temporally grounded world control, without introducing dedicated action modules. Specifically, we represent each action as a structured combination of character and camera instructions, and align them with the corresponding temporal video latents. To make the control temporally precise, we further introduce temporal attention routing, which restricts each instruction to its intended time interval and reduces control leakage across actions. Importantly, H3-World directly reuses the semantic representations learned during large-scale video pretraining and requires only lightweight adaptation. With only 8,000 gameplay samples, 10,000 LoRA optimization steps, and 0.199% trainable parameters, H3-World achieves effective character and camera control while preserving strong generation quality. It also generalizes to unseen scenarios. These results show that the control capabilities emerging in large video generators can be efficiently transformed into interactive world control.

</details>


### [113] [SpatialGuard: Harness-Guided Verifiable Spatial Reasoning for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01582)
*Ziyun Qian,Zizhi Chen,Yizhou Liu,Mingyang Sun,Dingkang Yang,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialGuard，一个面向复杂3D空间文本到图像生成的布局引导框架。它通过Spatial Layout Architect将提示解析为3D布局，经Visual Realizer生成视觉条件和图像，并用Visual Alignment Critic验证提示、布局和图像之间的一致性。同时引入Layout Harness来组织规则约束、工具调用、共享知识和反馈循环，将空间生成转变为可验证的规划-实现-验证-修复流程，在复杂3D空间布局生成上达到最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示驱动或布局条件方法虽提升了可控性，但缺乏在视觉采样前可优化、可验证的空间中间表征，导致在多轮生成中对象关系、遮挡、可见性和相机约束难以维持稳定。

Method: 提出SpatialGuard框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) Spatial Layout Architect将提示解析为面向图像合成的3D布局；(2) Visual Realizer将布局实现为视觉条件和候选图像；(3) Visual Alignment Critic验证提示、布局和图像的一致性；(4) Layout Harness围绕可编辑布局状态组织规则约束、工具调用、共享知识和反馈循环，确保迭代过程中约束稳定。

Result: 综合实验表明，SpatialGuard在复杂3D空间布局生成上达到最先进的性能，并在空间忠实度上优于现有文本到图像和布局控制基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架将复杂的空间文本到图像生成从隐式的提示遵循转变为可验证的规划-实现-验证-修复闭环过程，有效提升了复杂3D空间生成的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Complex 3D spatial text to image generation requires models to convert natural language into stable visual geometry, not merely semantic appearance. Existing prompt-driven or layout-conditioned methods improve controllability, but often lack an optimizable and verifiable spatial intermediary before visual sampling. As a result, object relations, occlusion, visibility, and camera constraints can decay during multi-round generation. This paper presents SpatialGuard, a structured layout-guided framework for complex 3D spatial text-to-image generation. SpatialGuard parses prompts into image synthesis-oriented 3D layouts through a Spatial Layout Architect, realizes them as visual conditions and candidate images through a Visual Realizer, and uses a Visual Alignment Critic to validate consistency among prompt, layout, and image. To keep constraints stable across iterations, SpatialGuard introduces a Layout Harness that organizes rule constraints, tool invocation, shared knowledge, and feedback loops around the editable layout state. This design turns complex spatial generation from implicit prompt following into a verifiable process of planning, realization, validation, and repair. Comprehensive experiments show that SpatialGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance in complex 3D spatial layout generation and improves spatial faithfulness over existing text-to-image and layout control baselines.

</details>


### [114] [A Benchmark for Vehicle Attribute Classification in Cross-Domain Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01584)
*Sergio M. Silva,Otavio T. Remer,Gabriel E. Lima,Lucas Wojcik,Rayson Laroca,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UVIB基准，用于评估无约束场景下的车辆属性分析（朝向、VMMR适用性、颜色清晰度），包含84,835张来自七个巴西数据集的图像，实验发现领域偏移对模型性能的影响大于架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控条件下训练的车辆属性分析模型在真实监控场景中性能严重退化，现有数据集缺乏统一的标注，无法系统评估模型在不同领域和数据集之间的泛化能力，需要专门衡量运营稳健性的基准。

Method: 构建UVIB基准，整合七个公开巴西数据集共84,835张图像，划分为监控和通用采集两个域，统一三类二元标注（前后朝向、VMMR遮挡适用性、颜色清晰度）；在混合域、跨域和跨数据集三种协议下评估四种代表性架构（EfficientNetV2-S、ResNet-50、ViT/B-16、YOLO11s-cls）。

Result: 领域偏移对性能的影响大于架构选择；跨域设置下性能大幅下降，尤其对VMMR适用性和颜色清晰度；朝向泛化更可靠，VMMR适用性受类别不平衡和模糊遮挡影响，颜色清晰度对光照和传感器模态高度敏感。

Conclusion: 现有车辆属性分析需要超越标准域内精度的基准和评估协议来显式度量运营稳健性，UVIB为此提供了公开可用的评估平台。

Abstract: Vehicle attribute analysis is a key component of Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), supporting applications such as vehicle identification, traffic monitoring, and forensic investigation. However, models trained under controlled conditions often degrade in real surveillance scenarios due to changes in viewpoint, occlusion, illumination, and sensor characteristics. This paper introduces Unconstrained Vehicle Identification Benchmark (UVIB), a benchmark for evaluating three operational vehicle-analysis tasks: front/rear orientation, occlusion-related suitability for Vehicle Make and Model Recognition (VMMR), and color clarity. The benchmark contains 84,835 vehicle images from seven public Brazilian datasets, grouped into surveillance and general acquisition domains, with unified binary annotations that were not jointly available in the original sources. Four representative architectures, EfficientNetV2-S, ResNet-50, ViT/B-16, and YOLO11s-cls, are evaluated under mixed-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset protocols. The results show that domain shift has a stronger impact than architecture choice, with substantial degradation in cross-domain settings, especially for VMMR suitability and color clarity. While orientation generalizes more reliably, VMMR suitability remains affected by class imbalance and ambiguous occlusions, and color clarity is highly sensitive to illumination and sensor modality. These findings highlight the need for benchmarks and evaluation protocols that explicitly measure operational robustness beyond standard in-domain accuracy. The proposed benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/uvib-vehicle-attributes/.

</details>


### [115] [UI-VISA: U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01598)
*Asees Kaur,Suzanne S. Sindi,Erica M. Rutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UI-VISA混合架构，结合U-Net与CNN引导的区域生长算法，用于数字减影血管造影图像的血管分割。该方法利用U-Net前景预测作为种子点，通过区域生长迭代细化分割，在26张DSA图像上5折交叉验证中取得了最高的Dice和clDice分数，其中clDice的改善具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级深度学习方法（如U-Net）在细血管区域容易产生碎片化或断裂的预测结果，因为它们没有显式地强制结构连通性；而区域生长算法虽然能保持拓扑连续性，但对种子点初始化高度敏感且计算成本高。两种方法各有局限，需要一种能够互补二者优势的方案。

Method: 提出UI-VISA混合管道，首先使用U-Net的前景预测作为信息化的种子点，输入到CNN引导的区域生长算法中，然后通过迭代细化分割，强制局部连通性并恢复U-Net容易遗漏或过度预测的精细血管细节。

Result: 在26张DSA图像上的5折交叉验证中，UI-VISA在平均Dice和clDice分数上均高于单独U-Net和先前基于区域生长的方法VISA。配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验表明clDice改善具有统计显著性（p=0.023），而Dice改善未达到显著水平（p=0.104）。

Conclusion: UI-VISA通过结合深度学习和区域生长的互补优势，有效提升了血管分割的连通性和精细结构恢复能力，其结果与设计目标一致，即保持血管连通性（clDice显著提升），但在整体像素级准确度（Dice）上提升有限。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of vascular structures in digital subtraction angiography (DSA) images remains challenging due to the thin, elongated, and branching nature of blood vessels. Pixel-wise deep learning approaches such as U-Net achieve strong general-purpose segmentation performance but often produce fragmented or discontinuous predictions in fine vascular regions, since they do not explicitly enforce structural connectivity. Region growing algorithms preserve spatial context and topological continuity, but are highly sensitive to seed point initialization and can be computationally expensive. We propose UI-VISA (U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture), a hybrid pipeline that combines the complementary strengths of both approaches. UI-VISA uses U-Net's foreground predictions as informed seed points for a CNN-guided region growing algorithm, which then iteratively refines the segmentation by enforcing local connectivity and recovering fine vessel details that U-Net alone tends to miss or over-predict. We evaluate UI-VISA against standalone U-Net and a prior region-growing-based method (VISA) using 5-fold cross-validation on 26 DSA images. UI-VISA achieves the highest mean Dice and clDice scores across folds, and a paired Wilcoxon signed-rank test shows the improvement in clDice is statistically significant ($p=0.023$), consistent with the method's design goal of preserving vascular connectivity, while the improvement in Dice does not reach significance ($p=0.104$).

</details>


### [116] [Uncovering Understanding-Generation Synergy in Native Unified Multimodal Models: From Representation, Task to System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01607)
*Penghao Wu,Haiwen Diao,Weichen Fan,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了统一多模态模型（UMMs）中视觉理解与生成两个目标之间的关系，从表示层、任务层和系统层三个层面进行了受控实验分析。研究发现：两目标在表示层相互提供有用信号，但在同一计算路径下会导致一方主导的退化；任务解耦架构可避免这种非对称退化；理解与生成任务依赖共享知识时可产生正向双向迁移；端到端UMM在复杂任务上优于规划-执行流水线。综合表明UMMs的价值不仅在于统一接口，还在于适当的专业化、共享任务知识和端到端优化可将共存转化为协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管统一多模态模型（UMMs）能在一个模型中同时进行视觉理解和生成，但功能上的统一并不保证学习上的协同效应——两个目标可能相互强化、竞争容量或仅仅共存。作者希望深入理解这种关系，以指导UMMs的架构设计和训练策略。

Method: 在无预训练视觉先验的受控、结构原生设置下，从三个层面进行系统研究：表示层（分析各目标对视觉特征的贡献及共享计算路径的影响，比较任务解耦架构）、任务层（通过三个案例研究考察不同任务间的双向迁移）、系统层（对比端到端UMM与匹配的规划-执行流水线在复杂任务上的表现）。

Result: 表示层：生成强化了理解所需的视觉特征，理解也加强了生成的视觉-语言对齐，但共享同一计算路径时会出现一方主导的非对称退化；任务解耦架构可避免该问题。任务层：理解与生成任务依赖共享知识时存在正向双向迁移。系统层：端到端UMM在需要同时理解与生成的复杂任务上优于规划-执行流水线。

Conclusion: UMMs的价值不仅在于提供一个统一接口，更在于通过适当的专业化分工、共享任务知识和端到端优化，能够将两个目标的简单共存转化为真正的学习协同效应。

Abstract: While unified multimodal models (UMMs) jointly perform visual understanding and generation within a single model, functional unification does not guarantee learning synergy: the two objectives may reinforce each other, compete for capacity, or merely coexist. We investigate their relationship at the representation, task, and system levels in a controlled, structurally native setting without pretrained vision priors. At the representation level, we find that each objective provides useful signal to the other: generation enriches the visual features learned for understanding, while understanding strengthens vision--language alignment for generation. However, when both objectives are forced through the same computation path, one tends to dominate. A task-decoupled architecture that specializes conflicting visual computation while preserving semantic interaction avoids this asymmetric degradation. At the task level, through three case studies, we find positive bidirectional transfer when understanding and generation tasks rely on shared knowledge. At the system level, we show that an end-to-end UMM outperforms a matched planner--executor pipeline on complex tasks that explicitly require both image understanding and generation. Together, these results show that the value of UMMs extends beyond a unified interface: appropriate specialization, shared task knowledge, and end-to-end optimization can turn coexistence into synergy.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TPGC，一种双先验提示初始化方法，通过任务先验注入模块和结构先验注入模块对预训练图模型进行提示初始化，在少样本场景下优于现有基线方法，且参数量更少、运行时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务图预训练框架使用随机初始化提示，导致提示空间与预训练目标及图结构特征之间对齐不良，削弱了提示表示的任务相关性、结构感知性和可迁移性。

Method: TPGC包含两个核心模块：(1) Task-Prior Injection Module：在辅助图上进行短期的同源多任务预训练，使提示初始化继承多任务预训练目标的优化偏好；(2) Structure-Prior Injection Module：基于任务感知表示，提取辅助图中可迁移的全局结构上下文，通过聚合结构信息丰富的节点嵌入生成层级提示向量。

Result: 在覆盖节点分类和图分类的6个主流基准上，TPGC在少样本设置下持续优于最先进的基线方法，同时拥有更少的下游可调参数和更低的运行时间。

Conclusion: TPGC通过显式建模任务先验与结构先验的协同作用，有效解决了随机提示初始化带来的对齐问题，为低资源场景下图提示学习提供了更高效、可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [118] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REAL-Q，一种新型后训练量化范式，通过实时端到端对齐的损失替身和细粒度动态块级梯度下降，解决传统PTQ中信息错位问题，在LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3等模型上显著降低端到端KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PTQ方法为保持解析可处理性，对全局损失进行大量近似（丢弃跨通道耦合、合并输出行分组），并冻结整个层的Hessian矩阵，无法随损失景观逐列变化而刷新，导致信息错位问题。

Method: REAL-Q针对端到端对齐的全局损失替身进行优化，通过每128列应用细粒度动态块级梯度下降进行修正，并结合滑动窗口机制实现平滑的跨层过渡，缓解误差在网络中的传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）及Qwen3（0.6B-32B）的W4A16配置下，相对最先进的全局引导方法，端到端KL散度最高降低约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过打破"为解析可处理性而稀释目标"的传统取舍，展示了动态、细粒度的端到端对齐量化方案的有效性，为LLM后训练量化提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [119] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于AOC-poset的Relational Concept Analysis（RCA）迭代过程的收敛性问题，指出在AOC-poset设定下RCA的收敛性保障会丢失，分析了导致收敛性失败的原因，提出了确保收敛的条件、数据转换方法及一种保留AOC-poset结构的收敛变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCA在形式概念分析基础上扩展以处理多关系数据，其在概念格设定下迭代收敛有保障，但当使用AOC-poset这一更小更高效的概念格子结构时，收敛性保证丢失，需要深入分析解决这一理论困境。

Method: 论文通过理论分析探讨AOC-poset设定下RCA收敛性丢失的本质原因，识别可确保收敛的条件，提出数据集转换策略以恢复收敛性，并设计一种收敛变体——一旦创建的关系属性永不移除，从而保证收敛但可能保留指向最终结构中不存在概念的属性。

Result: 论文证明了AOC-poset设定下RCA在一般情况下不再保证收敛，给出了可确保收敛的特定条件，提出了数据转换方法和收敛变体方案（关系属性创建后不会被移除）以保证收敛性。

Conclusion: 虽然AOC-poset上的RCA在一般情况下失去收敛性保证，但通过识别特定条件、数据转换或采用属性永不移除的收敛变体策略，可以在保持AOC-poset结构优势的同时恢复收敛性。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [120] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISTAL框架，用于无结构信息的材料性能预测。该框架结合自监督成分预训练和结构感知知识蒸馏，在训练时利用结构先验而推理时无需结构输入，在39个基准任务中的37个上超越了参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性能预测在低数据场景下面临挑战：最准确的模型依赖于晶体结构信息，但早期筛选阶段往往缺乏结构数据，限制了这些模型的实际应用。

Method: DISTAL采用双先验框架：1) 利用145个成分衍生描述符在大规模虚拟成分空间上进行自监督成分预训练；2) 将预训练ALIGNN教师模型的结构知识蒸馏到成分条件化的学生模型中。最终将显式成分描述符、预训练潜在特征和蒸馏结构特征整合到统一预测流程中。

Result: 在39个基准任务中，最佳多模态配置结合了全部三种信号，在37个任务上超越了参考基准，在所有特征组合中取得最强综合性能。

Conclusion: 成分预训练与结构蒸馏提供了互补的先验知识，为小数据材料信息学中稳健的纯成分预测提供了实用路径，且推理时无需结构输入。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [121] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNFT方法，通过内部概率质量重校准来修复扩散生成器在奖励后训练中出现的模式坍缩问题，无需依赖外部信号或接口，在保持98.9%和99.0%奖励的同时分别将多样性指标提升58.8%和55.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励后训练会使扩散生成器将概率质量集中到少数受奖励偏好的模式上，导致模式坍缩并丧失提示内多样性。现有缓解方法依赖外部信号或接口（如感知目标、参考正则化调整、文本编码器修改等），但均无法在保留已获取奖励的同时修复已经坍缩的适配器。

Method: ReNFT的核心洞察是：在线后训练主要是在预训练继承的能力之间重新分配概率质量，而非学习新视觉内容，因此坍缩是抑制而非删除。方法包括三个步骤：(1) 使用无条件探针优先选择"反中心"提示，暴露提示无关偏见；(2) 通过两条策略主导的混合路线从同一提示和初始噪声生成配对反事实提案，一条探测冻结基础方向以寻找被抑制的替代方案，另一条暴露后训练的无条件倾向；(3) 利用带自适应翻转守卫的奖励排名分配推拉角色，执行联合配对的NFT更新实现修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT分别保留了NFT奖励的98.9%和99.0%，同时将DreamSim-Div多样性分别提升了58.8%和55.0%，证明了在不借助外部干预的情况下有效修复坍缩适配器的能力。

Conclusion: ReNFT证明了坍缩适配器可以从内部修复，为奖励后训练中的模式坍缩问题提供了一种对外部干预的互补性替代方案。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [122] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将注意力层级的ICL代理指标作为训练目标是否可信。作者提出了两个指标：ICS（上下文敏感度）和ICL-GAP（行为准确率差距），并在Llama-2-7B上通过四臂消融实验发现，最大化ICS的正则化器虽然能大幅提升ICS指标至几何上限的99.5%以内，但行为层面的ICL-GAP几乎为零且MMLU准确率从0.371下降到0.279，出现了Goodhart效应——代理指标被优化后不再反映真实行为。最终结论是注意力层级的ICL代理指标必须经过行为差距验证后才能作为训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用注意力变化来诊断in-context learning能力是否被微调侵蚀，但这种注意力层级的代理指标在被优化后是否仍然可信缺乏验证。本文旨在检验这一代理指标在上限优化情境下的可靠性。

Method: 形式化定义两个指标：ICS（匹配与不匹配演示前缀下最后token注意力的平均行距离）和ICL-GAP（同一前缀间的行为准确率差距）。在Llama-2-7B上进行四臂消融实验，采用ICS最大化正则化器，并结合端点统计分析、随机标签协议以及行为门控等构造性实验进行验证。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器将ICS驱动至1.413（接近几何上限的99.5%），但ICL-GAP保持接近零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279。端点分析表明注意力变得尖锐且在各前缀间近乎不相交，但主要路由到格式和演示正文token而非标签。随机标签协议确认行为探针家族在同一检查点保持了动态范围。行为门控可部分缓解，而锚定预训练计算的目标能保持高MMLU和中等ICS区域。

Conclusion: 主要教训是诊断性的：注意力层级的ICL代理指标只有在针对行为差距进行验证之后，才能作为训练目标使用。否则会产生Goodhart效应，代理指标被优化后与真实ICL行为解耦。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [123] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案，通过单一模型token在网络中顺序遍历并利用本地目标顺序更新，取代了中心化聚合和gossip式去中心化方法，消除了全局同步需求，大幅降低通信与计算成本并避免聚合误差，在非凸目标下提供收敛保证，实验证明通信计算开销远小于gossip式LoRA且性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LoRA方法（如中心化聚合和gossip式去中心化）存在显著的通信开销问题，且因同时聚合多个模型更新而产生误差，亟需一种低通信、无聚合误差的去中心化微调方案。

Method: 提出随机游走式LoRA微调方案，不维护多个模型副本，而是让单个模型token在网络中遍历，依次通过本地微调目标顺序更新模型参数，从而消除全局同步，降低通信和计算开销并避免聚合误差。

Result: 在多个NLP任务和不同图拓扑上的实验表明，该方法在保持与gossip式LoRA相当的模型性能的同时，通信和计算开销显著更低。

Conclusion: 基于随机游走的LoRA微调是一种高效可行的去中心化方案，兼具理论收敛保证与实证性能，为分布式大模型微调提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [124] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型的高效递归公式，用于计算包含聚类结构的编码向量的随机概率（最短编码长度），将时间复杂度从多项式降至线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于最小描述长度（MDL）原理的数据聚类中，需要计算NML模型下包含聚类结构向量的最短编码长度，以估计最佳聚类数和聚类结构。然而直接计算需要多项式时间，效率低下，因此需要更高效的算法。

Method: 作者引入了一个递归公式来计算NML模型中的归一化常数，从而高效地计算包含聚类结构向量的最短编码长度，将时间复杂度从多项式级降低到线性级。

Result: 新公式的时间复杂度为线性，相对于此前多项式时间复杂度（相对于向量大小和聚类数）有显著提升，证明了该计算问题是可解的（tractable）。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于MDL原则的聚类分析提供了高效的计算工具，使得从理论和实践角度估计最佳聚类数及最佳聚类结构成为可能。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [125] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了五个基础模型家族的九个零样本模型与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型，在德国、波兰和西班牙三个市场进行评估。结果显示只有TabPFN模型在统计上持续显著优于基准，但其统计优势并不能直接转化为经济优势，在低风险容忍度场景下，基准模型更有利可图。基础模型无法普遍替代市场专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型号称无需特定任务训练即可实现准确预测，但其能否替代专门为电价预测设计的模型仍不清楚，这是一个亟待验证的关键问题。

Method: 选取五个基础模型家族的九个变体在零样本模式下运行，与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型在德国、波兰和西班牙三个市场（2021-2025）进行比较，从点预测精度、概率预测精度以及电池储能套利的经济价值三个维度进行评估。

Result: 仅TabPFN模型在所有三个市场及所有统计指标上一致且显著优于基准模型，但其统计优势未直接转化为经济优势。TabPFN在无限竞价和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而基准Distributional Deep Neural Network模型在风险容忍度较低时更有利可图。

Conclusion: 基础模型不能普遍替代针对特定市场的专用模型，其价值取决于模型架构和具体决策问题。统计性能优势与经济价值之间不存在必然的对应关系。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [126] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将特征重要性方法（FIMs）嵌入基于证据权重（Weight of Evidence, WoE）的假设检验框架中，用于从对比性、基于证据的视角评估LIME和SHAP等可解释性方法的解释质量，并建立了WoE与归因方差之间的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的特征重要性方法虽广泛用于可解释AI，但仅依赖归因分数难以提供对模型底层推理过程的深入理解，缺乏一种原则性、可量化评估FIMs与先验知识或基准对齐程度及其变异性的方法。

Method: 作者提出一种新颖视角，将FIMs嵌入基于证据权重的假设检验框架中，量化观察到的证据对任何给定特征重要性假设的支持强度。参考假设可来源于领域知识、真实标签或FIM本身，从而实现对FIMs的原则性评估；同时提供了将WoE与归因方差关联的理论结果。

Result: 通过在不同参考假设设置下分析LIME和SHAP解释的实证结果，展示了该方法在评估FIMs方面的适用性和灵活性，验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估特征重要性方法提供了一种互补性工具，通过对比性、基于证据的视角，能够同时捕捉FIMs与先验知识的一致性及其变异性，补充现有评估方法。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [127] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safin-1基础模型家族，通过"Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH)"架构实现"由内而外的安全"（Safety from Within），将安全作为模型自身原生计算中的状态能力而非外部约束，通过记忆路由与状态演化实现长时程任务中的安全适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程复杂任务要求基础模型在长时间交互中积累信息并保持内部状态，但传统安全策略依赖外部防护或后置对齐（如监督微调），安全并未成为模型的内在属性。作者认为安全应内生于模型自身计算机制，而非外部附加的行为约束。

Method: 提出Safin-1模型家族，基于MARCH架构：通过Memory-Anchor Routing维护结构化记忆状态，利用内容条件路由选择性检索历史信息；支持在测试时对持久能力状态进行适配，无需反复修改主干网络；通过Safety State接口实现对下游安全任务的状态化适配。该路由状态接口将上下文记忆与持久能力适配统一于模型原生计算中。

Result: 在下游安全任务上验证了Safety State的适配效果，显示出实质性的安全改进；在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率等基准上的评估进一步验证了Safin-1的综合性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将记忆从被动的历史记录重定位为主动维护和演化模型行为的基板，为将安全构建为原生、可自适应维护的模型状态提供了路径。作者同时指出这仅是"由内而外安全"构想的初步架构探索，距离完整愿景仍需大量后续工作。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [128] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部参考几何（LRG）模块，用于诊断和修复不平衡时间序列分类中特征空间的局部几何失效问题——即少数类样本在特征空间中处于稀疏、被多数类主导或混合的近邻区域。LRG作为固定特征提取器与分类头之间的轻量级后处理特征增强模块，利用训练特征计算局部暴露度和类别混合风险，并通过标准化有符号位移和LDA投影残差摘要增强原始特征，在UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡时序分类方法主要通过改变训练分布、目标函数、logits或最终阈值来干预模型，但未衡量表示层面的问题：当少数类支持减少时，学习到的特征空间在少数类局部区域是否仍然可靠。作者揭示了"训练局部几何失效"问题——即使表示保留有用的全局类别结构，少数类仍可能位于稀疏、被多数类主导或混合的特征空间邻域中。

Method: 提出LRG（Local Reference Geometry）模块：(1) 仅使用训练特征，测量每个样本的局部暴露度和类别混合风险；(2) 用标准化有符号位移（从附近训练几何中得到的位移）增强每个固定特征；(3) 附加LDA投影的残差摘要。该模块作为后处理特征增强层，插入固定特征提取器与分类头之间。

Result: 在UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上，成对raw-versus-LRG对比表明该方法对学习型、预训练型和固定表示均有效，且可与训练层干预和后编码器分类器修正结合。消融实验证明增益来源于附加到原始特征上的有符号局部残差，而非通用原型距离、亲和特征、标量统计或VLAD类编码。进一步分析支持假设：不平衡下少数类邻域越发暴露于多数类，训练局部风险可识别易错区域，LRG增益集中在该类高风险区域。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从不平衡数据"局部几何可靠性"视角诊断时序分类问题，提出了轻量有效的LRG修复模块，并通过系统的实验和消融验证了失效假设与修复机制。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [129] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FFD是一种硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过全融合内核和top-delta策略打破长上下文解码中的内存墙瓶颈，实现最高11.6倍内核级加速，并支持扩展到256K上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型的解码过程受限于内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制的二次复杂度，现有基于元数据度量的方法存在内存开销，而自适应选择策略则计算效率低下，需要一种能够同时解决这两个问题的方法。

Method: FFD采用硬件算法协同设计，将选择器和计算器整合为全融合内核，用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引；同时提出top-delta策略，无需全局同步即可动态过滤块，实现分布自适应稀疏性；此外还实现了扫描结果的重用以避免重复计算。

Result: FFD在无训练和即插即用的条件下实现最高11.6倍内核级加速，支持256K上下文长度并带来2.37倍端到端吞吐量提升；在RULER和LongBench基准上验证了高比例稀疏性下的模型精度保持能力。

Conclusion: FFD通过硬件-算法协同设计有效解决了长上下文解码中的内存墙问题，在保持模型精度的同时实现了高稀疏性和显著加速，为长上下文LLM的高效推理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [130] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向腐蚀知识合成的领域自适应检索增强生成框架，在镁合金腐蚀场景下微调三种开源语言模型，结合混合稠密-词法检索管线，通过Reason Map图谱框架检测因果倒置和推理跳跃，实现可解释、机制可辩护的AI辅助知识合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠预测至关重要。传统机器学习虽能预测腐蚀速率但无法解释潜在机制，而安全关键材料工程需要既准确又可机制辩护的推理，现有事实性指标无法评估这一点。

Method: 1) 微调Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B和Mistral-7B三种开源模型，使用来自840篇同行评审论文的3,309个专家验证问答对；2) 构建混合稠密-词法检索管线；3) 提出Reason Map命题图谱框架，独立构建生成答案与检索文献的有向证据图，系统检测因果方向反转和无根据的推理跳跃。

Result: 检索增强带来Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度达0.964，上下文召回率0.988。在新发表文献和内部电化学数据上的盲外部验证确认了趋势级泛化能力，Reason Map能发现传统事实性指标无法暴露的因果倒置和推理缺陷。

Conclusion: 模块化架构可跨领域应用，为可信赖的AI辅助知识合成提供了通用蓝图，不仅适用于腐蚀领域，也可推广至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [131] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECCBbench基准和评测协议，从效率（FLOPs）、压缩（可压缩输入的记忆效果）和校准（不确定性与弃权行为）三个维度衡量VLM/LLM的记忆能力，发现预训练VLM在文本上可压缩记忆但在视频上不行，且校准能力普遍较差；非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准仅通过长文本或长视频的准确性来评估模型记忆，但准确性无法反映真实长时序任务中记忆的关键属性（如计算效率、压缩能力和不确定性校准），因此需要更全面的评测体系。

Method: 设计ECCBbench基准，提出"EC C"三轴评测协议：efficiency（以FLOPs衡量回答记忆问题所需计算量）、compression（检验可压缩输入是否被更准确或高效地记住）、calibration（评估系统是否根据自身不确定性和错误成本进行弃权）；对预训练VLM及多种记忆骨干架构进行系统评测。

Result: 预训练VLM在文本上表现出记忆压缩能力，但在视频上不具备该能力，且两者上的校准都较差；在更广泛的记忆骨干架构中，多个非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。

Conclusion: 现有VLM记忆能力在容量之外的维度（效率、压缩、校准）存在明显不足，非Transformer架构有望成为长时序智能体记忆组件的更优选择，ECCBench为记忆评估提供了超越准确率的新范式。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [132] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"天生缺陷，通过进化完美"（Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution)的机制，通过让AI/ML模型群中的个体模型系数故意偏离最优值（引入突变），在动态变化的环境中作为统计对冲手段，可靠且持续地提升总体性能。研究通过四个定理证明了在一般条件下悔恨（regret）减少的保证，并在合成线性回归任务上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML模型在面对真实世界中常见的非平稳（概念漂移）问题时性能会下降。虽然AI/ML技术早已纳入自然选择思想，但优化过程天然趋向收敛，导致难以维持模型多样性，因此需要一种能够在变化环境中持续保持适应性的方法。

Method: 提出"Flawed in Nature"机制：对一组AI/ML模型进行系统性突变（故意使模型系数偏离最优点），使模型群作为统计对冲应对环境非平稳性。研究者给出了四个定理证明该方法在一般条件下的悔恨减少保证，并通过自适应控制器调节突变漂移率以匹配环境漂移率，此外设计了一种推理合成（inference synthesis）方法将个体优势转化为群体优势。

Result: 在合成线性回归任务中，经过突变的模型群在约80%的环境变化场景中能提供最佳模型；推理合成成功将个体优势转化为集体优势。该机制在突变漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 该机制与生物进化有高度类比关系，可能是有机发现更接近生物智能的AI形式所缺失的关键要素；作为一种可泛化的设计原则，为构建在动态不确定环境中保持长期性能的AI/ML系统提供了理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [133] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT)的统一分子生成优化器，通过奖励引导的高分分子"精英选择"配合模型自身的预训练损失进行更新，绕过了传统策略梯度强化学习对生成轨迹对数概率的依赖，可跨多种生成架构（自回归、掩码扩散、离散流）和多种设计任务（从头生成、基序延伸、连接子设计）统一适用，并在3D形状对齐和2D相似性等多个oracle基准上表现优于对应的原生优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标导向分子优化通常采用策略梯度强化学习，但其更新依赖于与模型架构和生成过程耦合的轨迹对数概率，导致优化器难以在不同架构和条件生成设计之间复用；而监督微调虽然架构无关，但其更新由固定数据集驱动，奖励不参与更新过程，缺乏奖励引导能力。

Method: EW-SFT通过奖励引导地从生成分子中选择高分"精英"子集，然后使用模型自身的预训练损失在该子集上进行微调更新。核心设计是奖励信息主要通过精英选择传递，而非在选定集合内做连续加权。该更新规则仅需已评分的分子和模型原生损失，因此可统一适用于自回归、掩码扩散和离散流等不同生成器。

Result: 在固定3D形状对齐oracle调用预算下的两个激酶参考化合物任务中，EW-SFT一致优于对应的原生优化器；在4个保留参考化合物的2D相似性oracle任务中进一步提升了目标导向优化性能；在无轨迹级RL公式的样本效率基准上达到可比性能。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一种统一且有效的分子生成优化器，可跨不同分子生成架构、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle类型广泛适用，兼具监督微调的架构通用性和奖励驱动的优化能力。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [134] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WHALE（Weight-Harness交替学习）框架，通过交替更新模型权重和执行代码（harness）来联合优化智能体性能，在两个阶段间使用固定或自适应的切换策略，在多个领域显著优于仅优化单一组件的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的性能同时取决于模型参数和执行代码（harness），但现有方法要么只优化权重和文本提示词（固定harness），要么只优化权重，忽略了二者的联合适应性。实际上两者相互依赖：权重更新会改变有效harness，harness更新也会暴露新的模型能力，孤立优化会让系统受限于另一冻结部分。

Method: 提出WHALE交替学习框架，包含两个阶段：1）在当前harness下进行在线拒绝采样微调更新模型权重；2）使用Meta-Harness在当前更新后的模型下搜索更优的harness。切换时机通过固定阶段时长或基于训练信号的自适应耐心规则来控制，以区分真实改进与噪声，避免过度优化。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体上，覆盖搜索问答、数学推理和象棋谜题三个领域，WHALE在最佳mean@8精度上比仅权重优化、仅harness优化和Fast-Slow Training高出4.15-24.38个百分点。研究表明任一组件都可能成为瓶颈，且小的交错更新在精度和rollout成本上优于分阶段式的先权重后harness优化。

Conclusion: 权重与harness的联合交替优化能够有效克服单一组件优化的瓶颈限制，WHALE框架验证了这种交替学习策略在各领域的通用有效性，并表明灵活的自适应切换机制对实际收敛具有重要价值。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [135] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向大型语言模型的sub-2-bit权重后训练量化（PTQ）框架，将权重量化为三元值，并用具有1:4半结构化稀疏性的显著权重列作为残差补偿器来保持硬件效率；通过列级缩放精炼和误差衰减机制减少重建误差，在Qwen3-14B上实现1.7 bits/weight的有效压缩，相比最强三元PTQ基线平均精度提升16.7%，并在Llama3-8B上也获得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的权重后训练量化方法在跨模型泛化方面表现不佳，尤其是在2 bits以下精度损失严重。一些方法引入非结构化稀疏性来缓解精度损失，但牺牲了计算规律性和GPU友好性，如何在sub-2-bit下同时保持精度和硬件效率是一个挑战。

Method: 1) 将权重量化为三元值，使用显著权重作为残差误差补偿器；2) 残差被分配到选定的列上，并采用1:4半结构化稀疏性以维持硬件效率；3) 在GPTQ风格逐列量化中加入列级缩放精炼，交替更新每列缩放因子和三元分配以减少重建误差；4) 识别GPTQ中依赖顺序的误差传播问题，引入误差衰减机制来缓解后期误差积累；5) 开发基于查找表的GPU内核加速生成。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上，QTEA将所有权重压缩至1.7 bits/weight，平均精度相比最强三元PTQ基线提升16.7%，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低1.40倍和2.61倍；在Llama3-8B上获得6.6%精度提升及1.34倍/1.95倍的困惑度改善；基于查找表的内核相比FP16基线实现7.2倍的每token生成加速。

Conclusion: QTEA通过结合三元量化、半结构化稀疏残差补偿以及针对GPTQ的算法改进，成功实现了高精度、高硬件效率的sub-2-bit权重量化，且在不同模型规模上均展现良好泛化性，为LLM高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [136] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoLAMP，一种基于几何感知变分自回归生成模型的PDE求解方法，专门针对微米尺度复杂弯曲几何结构中的多物理场问题。该模型采用双编码器图表示架构联合捕捉全局拓扑和细尺度几何特征，并在潜空间中使用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer实现稳定的逐块自回归时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学计算中，复杂微米尺度弯曲几何结构内求解多物理场PDE仍是一大挑战，尤其是能源和化学工程中高度不规则的曲折结构，传统数值方法难以高效处理，亟需开发能够感知并适应复杂几何的AI求解方法。

Method: GeoLAMP包含以下核心组件：(1) 双编码器架构在图上联合捕捉全局拓扑与细尺度几何特征，实现真实空间场到紧凑潜表示的转换；(2) 引入带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer在潜空间建模时间动态，实现稳定且可扩展的逐块自回归预测；(3) 弹性解码器在任意点重建高分辨率物理场。

Result: 作者建立了三个多物理场基准数据集（涵盖反应流、热对流和弹性问题），GeoLAMP在所有数据集上均实现最稳定的自回归性能，在整个轨迹展开中保持低误差。

Conclusion: GeoLAMP系统性地研究了微米尺度复杂几何中PDE的几何感知学习，并为基于流匹配框架的潜自回归PDE建模的逐块时间推进提供了新见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction.
