A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [137] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了零样本大语言模型（LLM）在人口普查区块组级别的城市移动性预测能力，发现LLM在四个美国都市区中的表现（最佳准确率0.435）显著低于监督学习基线（0.580），并揭示了LLM预测依赖粗糙且稳定的先验，缺乏结构性依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细轨迹数据通常具有专有性、受限性和隐私敏感性，而LLM有望作为替代方案生成移动性轨迹。然而，LLM在推断街道群体级别的聚合移动性方面能力尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 使用匿名Cuebiq数据构建点级、轨迹级和时间移动性结果，结合社会人口与建筑环境预测因子，在四个美国都市区对零样本LLM进行评估，并与监督学习基线对比；同时引入方向对齐分析，检验LLM隐含预测因子效应是否与经验OLS及Jonckheere-Terpstra趋势一致。

Result: 监督模型平均准确率为0.580，最佳LLM仅为0.435；空间范围结果可预测性最强，但LLM与基线差距也最大。方向分析表明LLM常依赖粗粒度、稳定的预测因子先验，在不同结果与城市间保持一致，且对受保护群体预测因子存在不对称处理。

Conclusion: LLM能从城市背景中部分恢复聚合移动性模式，但在未经经验对齐和潜在偏差审计前，其预测不应被视为具有结构性依据。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We evaluate zero-shot LLMs on Census Block Group-level mobility prediction across four U.S. metropolitan areas using anonymized Cuebiq data to construct point-level, trajectory-level, and temporal mobility outcomes, paired with sociodemographic and built-environment predictors. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. Supervised models achieve 0.580 average accuracy, compared with 0.435 for the best LLM, with spatial extent outcomes showing the strongest predictability but also the largest LLM-baseline gaps. Directional analysis shows that LLMs often rely on coarse, stable predictor-level priors that remain similar across outcomes and cities, including asymmetric treatment of protected-group predictors. Overall, LLMs can partially recover aggregate mobility patterns from urban context, but their predictions should not be treated as structurally grounded without auditing empirical alignment and potential bias.

</details>


### [138] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过注入九种故障到INT8 GEMM管道的8232个测试单元中，系统评估了量子化符合性测试套件实际能检测到什么。研究发现五类尾处理（epilogue）故障最多仅使输出偏移一个bfloat16间距，因此一个间距的容差在结构上对整类故障失明。将权重尺度重新量化为最接近的2的幂可使CUTLASS与Triton在位级完全一致，且困惑度代价最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化GEMM内核的符合性测试套件用于判断两个实现是否在容差范围内一致，但人们对这类套件究竟能检测出什么、其检测边界在哪里缺乏系统性理解。该研究旨在量化此类套件的能力边界。

Method: 向参考INT8流水线中注入九种故障，在Qwen3-1.7B的8232个层-故障-机制组合单元上测试；分析输出偏移的bfloat16间距；通过将权重尺度重新量化为最近的2的幂来消除尾处理差异，检验CUTLASS与Triton的位级一致性及困惑度影响。

Result: 五类尾处理故障中四类完全无法被检测，第五类仅在2的幂尺度下可被检测；违反累加器精确性前提或破坏操作数共享的故障则无一例外被检测到。将权重尺度重新量化为2的幂后，CUTLASS和Triton在线性层实现196/196和252/252的位级一致（对比原始尺度的8/196和10/252），三个规模模型的token序列全部逐字节一致。困惑度点估计为+0.32%、-0.28%和+0.48%，14B规模90%区间（+0.71%、+0.76%）不覆盖零。此前报告的+157%困惑度是探针未重新量化权重的伪影，其中99.8%归因于权重-尺度不匹配。

Conclusion: 基于容差的符合性测试套件所确立的比"可互换性"更窄：它仅能证明前置条件成立、操作数被共享、差异保持在一个间距之内。2的幂约束不仅在理论上揭示了尾处理故障，也具有实际部署价值——在不重量化的前提下实现跨实现的位级一致，且困惑度代价可控。

Abstract: Conformance suites for quantized GEMM kernels ask whether two implementations agree within a tolerance. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, across 5,880 cells. A tolerance of one spacing is therefore blind to the entire class by construction: four of the five faults are detected by no check in the suite, and the fifth only under power-of-two scales. Faults that violate the accumulator's exactness preconditions, or that break operand sharing, are detected without exception, and a null fault never fires. What a tolerance-based suite of this shape establishes is therefore narrower than interchangeability: that the preconditions hold, that operands are shared, and that differences stay within one spacing. The power-of-two constraint that exposes the one detected fault is also deployable. Requantizing every weight scale to its nearest power of two makes CUTLASS and Triton agree bitwise at every linear layer (196/196 and 252/252, against 8/196 and 10/252 under the checkpoints' own scales) and yields byte-identical generated token sequences at 1.7B, 8B and 14B (8/8 prompts, against 0/8 at all three). Observed perplexity point estimates are +0.32%, -0.28% and +0.48%; the 90% intervals cover zero at the two smaller sizes but not at 14B, reaching +0.71% and +0.76%. A previously reported +157% perplexity for this intervention was an artifact of a probe that rewrote scales without requantizing the weights; separating the effects attributes 99.8% of it to the resulting weight--scale mismatch rather than to the power-of-two constraint itself.

</details>


### [139] [Counterfactual Fragility Certificates: Exposing High-Confidence Brittleness under Structured Evidence Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00366)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Fragility Certificates（CFC），一种模型无关的审计证书，用于识别高置信度但结构脆弱的预测。CFC将每个预测映射为有序的证据失败轨迹（如特征家族不可用、延迟、噪声等），通过贪婪翻转预算、归一化边际塌缩面积、退化阈值和脆弱性优势分数来总结。在七个表格基准上，CFC-FDS以0.915 AUROC识别100%脆弱的独立高置信案例，相比最强非证书分数提升+0.405；在20%审查预算下捕获88.9%的脆弱高置信案例（对比置信度和能量分数的31.8-37.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（校准、不确定性、选择性预测、解释、扰动）只提供标量分数或归因图，无法回答"在声明证据失败协议下，什么轨迹使该预测失去支持"这一关键问题。表格决策系统中，当特征家族不可用、延迟、噪声、过时或低信任时，模型仍可能保持高置信度但预测结构性地缺乏证据支持。

Method: 提出CFC框架：在指定的证据失败协议下，将每个预测映射为有序证据失败轨迹，用四个指标总结——贪婪翻转预算（达到预测翻转所需的最少证据失败数）、归一化边际塌缩面积、退化阈值（预测失去支持的关键退化点）、碎片化优势分数。通过模型无关的方式生成可重算的审计对象，而非形式化鲁棒性证书。

Result: 在七个表格基准上测试线性、树、提升和神经网络基线，CFC-FDS以0.915 AUROC识别独立脆弱的高置信案例，比最强非证书基线提升+0.405。在20%审查预算下捕获88.9%脆弱高置信案例（基线为31.8-37.4%）。优势在扰动、置换重要性、group-SHAP、基线选择、种子方差、预算审查和自然场景字段不可用检查中持续存在。还评估了碎片化感知正则化和脆弱性感知温度校正作为次要用途。

Conclusion: CFC提供了一个具体可靠性框架，揭示普通分数中心评估所忽视的高置信度脆弱性，是面向证据结构可靠性的审计级工具，超越了传统标量置信度评估范式。

Abstract: High test accuracy and good aggregate calibration do not show whether an individual prediction is structurally supported by its evidence. In tabular decision systems, failures often occur when a feature family becomes unavailable, delayed, noisy, stale, or low-trust while the model remains highly confident. Existing calibration, uncertainty, selective-prediction, explanation, and perturbation methods provide scalar scores or attribution maps, but not a recomputable audit object answering: under a declared evidence-failure protocol, what trajectory makes this prediction lose support? We introduce Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC), a model-agnostic protocol-level audit certificate-not a formal robustness certificate-that maps each prediction into an ordered evidence-failure trajectory summarized by greedy flip budget, normalized margin-collapse area, degradation thresholds, and fragility dominance score. Across seven tabular benchmarks and strong linear, tree-based, boosting, and neural baselines, CFC-FDS identifies independently brittle high-confidence cases with 0.915 AUROC, improving over the strongest non-certificate score by +0.405. The advantage persists across perturbation, permutation-importance, group-SHAP, baseline-choice, seed-variance, budgeted-review, and naturalistic field-unavailability checks. Under a 20% review budget, CFC-FDS captures 88.9% of brittle high-confidence cases, compared with 31.8-37.4% for confidence and energy scores. We also evaluate fragility-aware regularization and brittleness-aware temperature correction as secondary uses. CFC provides a concrete reliability framework for exposing high-confidence brittleness missed by ordinary score-centric evaluation.

</details>


### [140] [Adapting Without Gradients: Affine Statistics Transport and What Its Certificate Can Tell You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00374)
*Salim Khazem,Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CASTRE，一种无梯度、无反向传播的测试时适应方法，适用于模型必须保持冻结的场景（如仅推理加速器、第三方模型或内存受限部署）。CASTRE在判别性子空间中存储源域类统计量，从目标批次矩估计类共享仿射变换，并在分类前解析地将源类分布进行迁移。方法的创新还在于引入了一个经验性的残差到边距的可迁移性验证证书，用于检测何时迁移是有害的并触发门控机制，将条件迁移的平均-3.35点效果转化为+1.69点的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TTA方法假设推理时可以更新模型参数，这在仅推理加速器、冻结/第三方模型和内存受限部署中是受限的，且依赖BatchNorm的TTA在无BatchNorm架构上不可用。论文旨在解决当学习模型必须完全冻结时如何实现有效的测试时适应问题。

Method: CASTRE方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 在判别性子空间中存储源域的类统计量；(2) 从目标批次矩估计一个类共享的仿射变换；(3) 在分类前解析地将源类分布进行迁移，全程无需反向传播、优化器状态或存储的源特征库。此外引入一个经验性的残差到边距可迁移性证书和门控机制，判断无条件迁移何时安全、何时有害。

Result: 在四种骨干网络和七个数据集上，CASTRE在28个骨干-数据集设置中的27个上优于基于相同冻结特征的k-NN，同时保留的中位数状态少18倍。在ImageNet-C上，无条件迁移损失21.2个top-1点，但通过可迁移性证书门控，将平均-3.35点的效应转化为+1.69点的增益，且在宽阈值范围内性能保持在最佳阈值的0.3点以内。证书是机制特定的：应用于Tent时仅接受4.3%的更新并保留Tent可用增益的0.6%。

Conclusion: CASTRE为冻结模型部署提供了一种轻量级的适应机制，并明确阐述了其安全信号何时有效、何时无效。该工作填补了无梯度、无参数更新条件下测试时适应的空白，为仅推理环境下的域偏移问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) typically assumes that model parameters can be updated at inference time. This assumption is restrictive for inference-only accelerators, frozen or third-party models, and memory-constrained deployments, and standard BatchNorm-based TTA configurations may also become inactive on architectures without BatchNorm. We study adaptation when the learned model must remain frozen. We introduce CASTER, a gradient-free method that stores source class statistics in a discriminative subspace, estimates a class-shared affine transformation from target-batch moments, and analytically transports the source class distributions before classification. CASTER requires no backward pass, optimizer state, or stored source feature bank. Across four backbones and seven datasets, it outperforms k-NN on identical frozen features in 27 of 28 backbone-dataset settings while retaining a median of 18x less state. Affine transport is not always reliable. On ImageNet-C, where batches contain only 64 samples for 1000 classes, unconditional transport loses 21.2 top-1 points. We therefore introduce an empirical residual-to-margin transportability certificate. Across 307 evaluation cells, every transport losing more than 10 points has certificate value above 3.9, although benign and destructive regimes are not perfectly separated. Gating converts an average $-3.35$-point effect of unconditional transport into a +1.69-point gain, and performance remains within 0.3 points of the best threshold over a broad threshold range. Finally, we show that this certificate is mechanism-specific: when applied to Tent, it accepts only $4.3\%$ of updates and preserves 0.6% of Tent's available gain. These results position CASTER as a lightweight adaptation mechanism for frozen-model deployment, together with an explicit account of when its safety signal is informative and when it is not.

</details>


### [141] [Neural means and kernel corrections for operator learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00389)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经网络与精确Matérn核回归结合（对残差和学到的特征分别做核回归），在两个公开仿真基准（结构力学和OCO-2辐射传输）上评估，达到了与最佳已发表架构相当或更好的性能，尤其在低数据场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络和核方法（如高斯过程）在仿真代理建模中各有优劣，但二者如何有效结合尚未被充分探索。作者希望联合两类方法的互补优势，并在已有公布基线的公共基准上进行公平比较。

Method: 将神经网络预测与精确Matérn核回归结合：一方面对网络残差做核回归，另一方面对其学习到的特征做核回归。辅以第二矩恒等式预测堆叠效果、最优恢复证明、以及无分布假设的覆盖带作为不确定性信号。

Result: 结构力学任务达到4.55%测试误差（持平最佳架构），低数据下5.38%优于已发表的6.49%；OCO-2任务中在三个波段的两个上直接超过已发布的GP仿真器。核方法在原始状态上比网络差十倍，但在网络特征上反超，原因是目标在固定有效维度下原生空间范数下降约四十倍。

Conclusion: 当两类方法性能持平时，所有架构的残差相关性均高于0.86且共享成分不随多样性和样本量变化，说明已发表的性能平台期是数据固有属性而非方法缺陷。只有无分布覆盖带通过全部验证，是唯一可靠的不确定性信号。

Abstract: We combine neural network means with exact Matérn kernel regressions of their residuals and of their learned features, and evaluate the pairing on two public emulation problems with published baselines: the structural-mechanics benchmark of de Hoop et al. and the OCO-2 radiative-transfer emulator of Lamminpää et al. On structural mechanics the combination reaches 4.55% test error, matching the best published architecture, and 5.38% against a published 6.49% in the low-data regime. On OCO-2 it improves on the published Gaussian-process emulator on that problem's own test points, outright on two of the three spectral bands; the same kernel that trails the network tenfold on the raw state overtakes it on the network's features, and we measure why (the target's squared native-space norm drops about fortyfold at fixed effective dimension) and prove the mechanism. Where the two families tie instead, the residuals of every architecture we train correlate above 0.86 and their shared component is flat in diversity and sample size, which reads the published plateau as a property of the data. Supporting results include a second-moment identity that predicts stacking outcomes from measured correlations, an optimal-recovery certificate, and a distribution-free coverage band, the only uncertainty signal that survives our tests.

</details>


### [142] [A Multi-Branch Feature Fusion Approach for Health Misinformation Detection and Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00403)
*Mkululi Sikosana,Sean Maudsley-Barton,Oluwaseun Ajao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多分支融合框架，用于检测和表征在线社交网络中健康错误信息的传播。该框架基于精细加工可能性模型（ELM）和计划行为理论（TPB），将基于Transformer的语义表征与修辞线索、立场表征和心理动机代理融合在统一的多任务架构中，并引入可解释的认知传播分数（CPS）来支持传播风险推理。在三个基准数据集上取得优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中健康错误信息的传播日益严重，现有检测方法主要依赖语义特征，忽视了认知心理学层面的驱动因素，且缺乏对信息传播风险的可解释性评估。

Method: 提出多分支融合框架，整合Transformer语义、修辞线索、立场表征和心理动机代理特征，基于ELM和TPB理论构建统一多任务架构；引入认知传播分数（CPS）作为可解释的后验辅助评分，涵盖论证复杂度、情绪强度和内容病毒性潜力等维度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，COVID-19_FNIR数据集上ROC-AUC达到0.9999；传播排名在Monkeypox上Spearman's ρ=0.9952，在COVID-19_FNIR上ρ=0.9954。与基线相比，Constraint和COVID-19_FNIR上检测性能提升，Monkeypox更具挑战性。消融实验表明心理和修辞分支提供互补增益。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了认知理论与神经建模之间的鸿沟，提升了模型透明性并支持可扩展的错误信息监测。未来需将CPS与人类中心的传播判断进行验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-branch fusion framework for detecting and characterising the propagation of health misinformation in online social networks (OSNs). Grounded in the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM) and the Theory of Planned Behaviour (TPB), the model fuses transformer-based semantics with rhetorical cues, stance representations, and psychologically motivated proxies in a unified multi-task architecture. In addition to binary classification, we introduce the Cognitive Propagation Score (CPS), an interpretable post-hoc auxiliary score computed from psychologically motivated, text-derived cues capturing argument complexity, emotional intensity, and content-derived virality potential, to support diffusion-risk reasoning when engagement ground truth is incomplete or unavailable. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, Constraint, COVID--19\_FNIR, and Monkeypox, show strong classification performance, achieving ROC--AUC up to 0.9999 on COVID--19\_FNIR, while propagation-oriented ranking achieves near-perfect agreement when engagement-derived supervision is available (Monkeypox, Spearman's $ρ= 0.9952$) and similarly high ranking alignment under proxy-based supervision on COVID--19\_FNIR ($ρ= 0.9954$). Compared with representative literature baselines, the fusion model improves detection on Constraint and COVID--19\_FNIR, while Monkeypox remains more challenging, reflecting domain- and signal-specific differences. Ablation analysis further indicates that psychological and rhetorical branches provide complementary gains beyond semantic embeddings. Overall, the framework bridges cognitive theory and neural modelling to improve transparency and to support scalable misinformation monitoring, with future work required to validate CPS against human-centred diffusion judgements.

</details>


### [143] [How Temporal Correlations Shape Memory in Linear Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00420)
*Arnol Manuel Fokam,Fasseu Sieyondji Akpevwoghene,Edem Fiifi Dawson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了线性循环神经网络（LRNN）在相关输入下的学习动力学。研究发现：相关性会给“记住过去”施加代价，重塑学习过程（记忆先建立、过冲、再部分移除）；记忆在由相邻输入相似度决定的阈值处关闭，与序列长度无关；当给网络一个额外隐藏维度时，训练会自动构建前馈路径以实现零误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于LRNN记忆机制的研究仅针对不相关输入，而真实序列具有相关性。作者希望理解输入相关性如何影响循环网络在训练中学到的记忆量。

Method: 对具有相关输入的线性循环神经网络，作者精确求解了其学习动力学方程，将输入相关性对记忆代价的影响从整体动力学中分离出来进行解析分析。

Result: 发现三个关键结果：(1) 输入相关性改变学习轨迹——记忆先增长、过冲后被部分清除，最终保留的记忆更少；(2) 记忆存在一个切换阈值，由相邻输入的相似度唯一决定，序列长度和长程相关性均不影响该阈值；(3) 为达到零误差，网络需要一条直通路径（feedthrough），且训练在有多余隐藏维度时会自发构建该路径。

Conclusion: 输入的一个简单属性（相邻相似度）即可预测网络是否会学到记忆，并解释了为何相关数据会使循环网络变成“变化检测器”。

Abstract: The linear recurrent neural network (LRNN) is a simple model for studying how much memory a network builds up as it trains. For uncorrelated inputs, earlier work found that training itself settles the network between keeping the past and reacting only to the present. Real sequences are correlated, and we solve the learning dynamics exactly for correlated inputs. In the solution, keeping the past carries a cost. The whole effect of correlation lands on that cost. This cost reduces to the earlier one when inputs are uncorrelated and grows once they are positively correlated. Three findings follow. (1) Correlation reshapes the course of learning, not only its end. Memory builds, overshoots, and is partly removed, and the settled network keeps less of the past. (2) Memory switches off at a threshold set by one number, how much each input resembles the one just before it. Neither sequence length nor longer-range correlation moves this threshold. Memory is worth keeping only when the task needs the previous input more than the current input already supplies it through correlation with the past. (3) The best network changes too. Zero error demands a feedthrough, a path that passes the current input straight to the network's output and remembers nothing, and training builds it unprompted when given one spare hidden dimension. Our work turns one property of the input into a prediction of whether a network learns memory and explains why correlated data turns recurrent networks into change detectors.

</details>


### [144] [Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00444)
*Sheng Jia,Xiao Wang,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan,Rein Houthooft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAPO（Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization），一种对GRPO方法的即插即用改进方案，根据rollout的优势自适应调整裁剪边界，解决了固定重要性采样比率裁剪在困难与简单问题上信号处理不均的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GRPO/RLVR方法在所有rollout上使用固定的IS比率裁剪边界，但困难问题中稀有的正确rollout与简单问题中丰富的正确rollout被以相似速率裁剪，尽管它们携带的学习信号差异很大。低群体成功率的rollout具有更大的IS比率和更强的梯度信号，却被固定裁剪不当压制。

Method: 提出GAPO，一种即插即用修改方案，将裁剪边界自适应于rollout的advantage。该方法源于反向KL信任区域视角，认为携带更大学习信号的rollout应获得更大比例的更新空间。GAPO不需要奖励塑形，保留标准PPO/GSPO代理目标，仅自适应调整裁剪阈值。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama模型上，GAPO在数学推理和代码基准测试中（基础模型通过率较低的任务）一致提升了Pass@1和Pass@k指标，优于固定裁剪和advantage塑形基线。

Conclusion: GAPO通过自适应裁剪机制有效解决了GRPO中固定裁剪对高学习信号rollout的不当抑制问题，作为一种轻量级即插即用改进，在多种模型和任务上展现了稳定且一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Group relative policy optimization for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically uses a fixed importance-sampling (IS) ratio clipping boundary across all rollouts. We identify a key limitation: rare correct rollouts on harder problems and abundant correct rollouts on easier problems are clipped at comparable rates, despite contributing very different learning signals. Rollouts with low group success exhibit larger IS ratios and carry stronger gradient signal for exploration and solving new problems, yet are disproportionately suppressed by fixed clipping.
  To address this, we propose Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization (GAPO), a plug-in modification to GRPO methods that adapts the clipping boundary to the rollout advantage. GAPO is motivated by a reverse-KL trust-region perspective, which suggests that rollouts with larger learning signal should receive proportionally greater update headroom. GAPO requires no reward shaping and preserves the standard PPO/GSPO surrogate while adapting only the clipping threshold. Across Qwen and Llama models, GAPO consistently improves both Pass@1 and Pass@k over fixed clipping and advantage-shaping baselines on math reasoning and coding benchmarks where the pass rates by the base model are relatively low.

</details>


### [145] [CRAD: Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation for Decentralized Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00446)
*Baraa Bilbeisi,Mengchen Fan,Baocheng Geng,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAD（Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation）方法，在无需中心服务器和公共数据集的去中心化知识蒸馏框架中，通过按类别评估和加权聚合同伴教师模型的预测，解决联邦学习中对同构架构和非独立同分布数据的双重同质性要求问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖参数平均，要求客户端具有相同架构且对非IID数据鲁棒性差。现实部署往往同时打破这两个假设。现有去中心化知识蒸馏方法在处理多教师预测时，采用简单的均匀平均，忽略了不同教师和不同类别间知识可靠性的差异。

Method: 提出CRAD方法，按类别进行处理：(1) 对每个类别，首先丢弃与同行共识不一致的教师；(2) 对剩余教师按其每类可靠性（精确率或方差的倒数）进行加权平均。由于n个样本的准确率方差与1/n成正比，样本支持度自然进入权重计算——在通过筛选的教师中，某教师在某类别被信任的程度取决于其在该类别上的准确性和证据充分性。

Result: 在三个图像分类基准（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和PathMNIST结肠病理）上，面对异构架构和严重的非IID数据偏差，CRAD在全局准确率上持续优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: CRAD通过类别级可靠性感知的教师聚合机制，有效解决了去中心化联邦知识蒸馏中多教师预测组合的问题，在异构架构和非IID数据条件下显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning (FL) relies on parameter averaging, which forces clients to be doubly homogeneous: it demands an identical architecture and degrades under non-IID data. Real-world deployments usually break both assumptions. We sidestep both by building a decentralized knowledge distillation framework in which each client evaluates its peers' model snapshots on its own local data and distills from the resulting soft predictions. Because knowledge is transferred through the shared class posterior, clients are free to run different architectures; and because every teacher is evaluated on the student's own device, raw data never leaves the client, with no central server or public dataset required. Within this setting, we identify and address an under-examined problem: how to combine the peer teacher predictions. Existing methods, like uniform averaging, ignore how knowledge reliability varies across teachers and classes. We propose Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation (CRAD), which, per class, first discards teachers that disagree with the peer consensus and then takes a weighted average of the rest, weighting each teacher by its per-class reliability (precision, or inverse variance). Since the variance of an accuracy from $n$ samples scales as $1/n$, support enters automatically: among the teachers that survive filtering, a teacher is trusted for a class to the degree that it is both accurate and well-evidenced for it. On three image-classification benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and PathMNIST colon pathology), across heterogeneous architectures under severe non-IID skew, CRAD consistently outperforms competing methods in global accuracy.

</details>


### [146] [HBQ: Hierarchical Scaling Block Quantization with Hardware-Efficiency-Aware Design for Accurate LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00450)
*Chun-Ting Chen,Dongmin Han,Hangyeol Mun,Jake Hyun,Arnab Raha,Amit Agarwal,Mark Anders,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Jae-sun Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层块量化（HBQ），通过引入显著性（SIG）缩放作为第二级量化来解决大规模块量化中硬件效率与精度之间的权衡问题，并在28nm ASIC上实现了2.3×/4.6×的面积/能效提升和1.6-3.3×的系统能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块量化（BQ）相比标量权重量化（WoQ）具有更高的硬件效率和统一的端到端推理数据通路，但其设计空间（位宽、块大小、缩放、数值格式）尚未被充分探索。传统BQ方法在面对块大小增大时，硬件效率提升但精度下降，这一权衡限制了传统BQ方法的发展。

Method: 通过设计空间探索（DSE）分析块大小、缩放策略等因素对硬件效率和精度的影响，提出分层块量化（HBQ）：使用大块最大化硬件效率，引入低开销的显著性（SIG）缩放进行第二级量化，同时考虑激活和权重的不同分布特性。HBQ包含HBQ-A（精度优先）和HBQ-E（效率优先）两种变体，并集成新颖的部分和BQ方案以降低EMA能量。

Result: HBQ-A在仅使用W4A5的条件下达到W4A16级精度，所需硅面积小于NVFP4；HBQ-E在保持高于所有现有BQ方法精度的同时降低17%硬件成本。与最先进的WoQ相比，HBQ在相同精度下提供2.3×/4.6×的面积/能效提升；相对先前BQ方法实现1.6-3.3×系统能耗降低和1.5-3.0×加速，同时提供最佳精度。

Conclusion: 分层块量化HBQ通过创新的SIG缩放方案和分层量化策略，有效解决了传统BQ方法中块大小与精度之间的根本权衡，在硬件效率、精度和能耗等多个维度均超越了现有WoQ和BQ方法，为大模型的高效部署提供了一条更优的硬件实现路径。

Abstract: Block Quantization (BQ) is a promising approach for efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), enabling low-precision computation with controlled accuracy degradation. Compared to scalar weight-only quantization (WoQ), BQ quantizes both weight and activation, offering higher hardware efficiency and end-to-end inference on a unified datapath, but its design space, spanning bit-width, block size, scaling, and numeric formats, remains underexplored.
  We provide hardware/benchmark results through design space exploration (DSE). We find that increasing block size improves hardware efficiency by amortizing dequantization and accumulation costs, but degrades accuracy. This trade-off limits conventional BQ methods.
  Motivated by this insight, we propose Hierarchical Block Quantization (HBQ). Unlike prior methods [1], [2], which use small blocks and conventional Power-of-Two (PoT) or integer-based scaling, HBQ uses large blocks to maximize efficiency and introduces low-overhead significand (SIG) scaling for second-level quantization. By allocating quantization levels effectively and accounting for distinct activation and weight distributions, SIG scaling compensates for large-block errors more effectively than prior PoT and INT schemes. HBQ-A (accurate) achieves W4A16-level accuracy using only W4A5 while requiring less silicon area than NVFP4. HBQ-E (efficient) further reduces hardware cost by 17% while maintaining higher accuracy than all existing BQ methods.
  We implemented a 28nm ASIC accelerator applying HBQ to weights, activations, and KV cache, and integrated a novel partial-sum BQ scheme to further reduce EMA energy.
  Compared to state-of-the-art WoQ, HBQ delivers $2.3\times$/$4.6\times$ higher area/energy efficiency at the same accuracy level; $1.6$--$3.3\times$ system energy reduction and $1.5$--$3.0\times$ speedup over prior BQ methods while providing best accuracy.

</details>


### [147] [Can LLMs Use Relational Transformer Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00457)
*Francisco Galuppo Azevedo,Clarissa Lima Loures*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在Qwen3.5-4B中注入冻结的关系编码器（RT）嵌入作为软token，结合MLP投影和LoRA适配，测试了软token融合策略在关系数据库预测任务上的效果。结果表明混合模型在10个二元分类任务上不如单独的RT，甚至常常低于随机水平，且对序列化格式和关系token预算高度敏感，在RL训练下不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将冻结的关系编码器嵌入作为软token注入LLM的融合策略是否可行——该策略在概念上具有吸引力：编码器处理多表结构，LLM处理语言和推理，且无需有损的文本序列化。

Method: 将冻结的Relational Transformer嵌入通过学习的MLP投影注入Qwen3.5-4B，并采用LoRA适配。训练流程分为两步：先进行SFT（监督微调）于思维链推理轨迹，再进行基于组的强化学习（GSPO）。在6个关系数据库的10个二元分类任务上，对单任务（ST）、数据集内（WD）、跨数据集（CD）和全任务（ALL）四种监督模式进行评估。

Result: 混合模型未能持续优于独立的RT，经常表现低于随机水平，对序列化格式和关系token预算高度敏感，且在强化学习训练下不稳定。

Conclusion: 作者将这些负面结果作为失败模式分析报告，认为软token融合需要更强的对齐目标和考虑模式（schema）感知的设计，才能成为关系预测的可靠途径。

Abstract: Injecting frozen relational-encoder embeddings as soft tokens into a large language model (LLM) is a conceptually appealing fusion strategy: the encoder handles multi-table structure, the LLM handles language and reasoning, and no lossy text serialization is required. We test this hypothesis concretely by injecting embeddings from a frozen Relational Transformer (RT) into Qwen3.5-4B via a learned MLP projection and LoRA adaptation, trained first with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on chain-of-thought reasoning traces and then with group-based reinforcement learning (GSPO). We evaluate across 10 binary classification tasks on 6 relational databases from RelBench, under four supervision regimes: single-task (ST), within-dataset (WD), cross-dataset (CD), and all-task (ALL). The hybrid model does not consistently outperform standalone RT: it is frequently below random, highly sensitive to serialization format and relational-token budget, and unstable under RL training. We report these negative results and analyze the failure modes, arguing that soft-token fusion requires stronger alignment objectives and schema-aware design before it can serve as a reliable route to relational prediction.

</details>


### [148] [Context Window Failures in Relational Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00460)
*Denis Oliveira Correa,Francisco Galuppo Azevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出近期提出的关系型深度学习架构（RT、Griffin、RelGT）在处理高基数关系数据时存在局限——受限于邻居预算导致行截断，无法有效聚合大量关联记录。作者引入合成金融数据集Animus，实验表明这些模型在原始表示上的R²仅≤0.18，而简单的时序预聚合步骤可将R²提升至0.65，质疑了当前关系基础模型处理真实高基数数据的成熟度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关系型深度学习作为多表关系数据的基础模型被提出，但受限于固定的邻域预算，当实体拥有大量关联记录时被迫进行行截断。文章旨在检验这些模型能否应对真实世界中高基数的关系数据场景。

Method: 作者创建了Animus合成金融数据集，其中预测客户收入需要聚合多达数万条交易记录。在该数据集上对三种近期提出的关系基础模型（RT、Griffin、RelGT）进行评测，并对比原始表示与经过时序预聚合处理后的表示。

Result: 在原始表示上，RT、Griffin和RelGT三种模型的R²均不超过0.18；仅加入一个常规的时序预聚合步骤后，R²最高可恢复至0.65。这一显著性能差距揭示了现有模型在高基数数据上的严重不足。

Conclusion: 当前的关系型基础模型尚不足以应对高基数的真实世界数据。简单的预聚合方法远优于先进的关系基础模型，表明这些模型在架构设计上仍需突破邻居预算瓶颈，以真正服务于实际多表关系数据场景。

Abstract: Recent Relational Deep Learning architectures have been proposed as foundation models for multi-table relational data, yet they impose constrained neighborhood budgets that force row truncation when an entity has many related records. We introduce Animus, a synthetic financial dataset in which predicting customer income requires aggregating up to tens of thousands of transactions. On the raw representation, three recently proposed models (RT, Griffin, RelGT) achieve $R^2 \le 0.18$; a single, routine, temporal pre-aggregation step recovers $R^2$ up to $0.65$. This questions whether current relational foundation models are ready for high-cardinality real-world data.

</details>


### [149] [Higher Structures in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00472)
*Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata Carratalá. Nicholas J. Cooper,Lijun Chen,François G. Meyer,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述性研究，探讨高阶张量运算（高arity张量操作）在深度学习中的重要性，通过对训练好的神经网络进行实证调查，提出了多层感知机的超图泛化（超图MLP），并探讨了与进化算法的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望引起学界对高阶张量运算在深度学习中的重要性的关注，因为现有研究大多局限于二元运算（如矩阵乘法），而对更高阶交互的研究不足。

Method: 首先给出高阶张量运算对深度学习重要性的说明性介绍；随后在训练好的神经网络中开展针对高阶现象（higher-arity phenomena）的新颖实证调查；提出一种多层感知机的超图泛化形式；探索与进化算法的关联。

Result: 论文展示了训练好的神经网络中存在的高阶交互现象，并通过超图MLP验证了高阶建模的可行性，初步揭示了其与进化算法的潜在联系。

Conclusion: 作者对未来研究方向进行了讨论，指出了在深度学习框架中进一步引入高阶张量/超图结构以及借鉴进化计算思想的可能突破口。

Abstract: We provide an expository introduction on the importance of higher-arity tensor operations to deep learning. Then, we conduct a novel empirical investigation of higher-arity phenomenon in trained neural networks, introduce a hypergraphical generalization of the multilayer perceptron, and explore connections to evolutionary algorithms. We conclude with a discussion of promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [150] [AdaptNTK: Adaptive Uncertainty Quantification and Active Learning for Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00488)
*Prajwal Ananth,Shuwen Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaptNTK是一个单模型主动学习框架，利用经验神经正切核(NTK)特征空间中的正则化马氏距离来衡量不确定性，可在批采样过程中递归更新不确定性而不需要重新训练，从而减少批次内冗余，在多个基准上超越集成方法并实现2.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习原子间势能的不确定性量化方法通常在计算成本与可靠性之间存在权衡，且一般无法在构建采集批次时考虑冗余问题，需要一种既能高效估计不确定性又能处理批次内冗余的方法。

Method: 提出AdaptNTK单模型框架，在经验神经正切核（NTK）特征空间中使用正则化的马氏距离（Mahalanobis distance）作为不确定性度量。由于采集过程中NTK特征固定，不确定性只取决于已采集的构型而非其参考标签，因此每次选择后可以递归更新不确定性而无需重训，有效减少批次内冗余。

Result: 在留出的rMD17数据上，AdaptNTK取得与力误差的最高平均相关性（Spearman 0.68，Pearson 0.71），且在误差保留方面匹敌三成员集成。在主动学习实验中，AdaptNTK在rMD17和Transition-1X上取得最低力误差，尤其在Transition-1X的过渡态构型上表现突出，每个周期的速度比集成方法快2.6倍。

Conclusion: AdaptNTK提供了高效的单模型不确定性估计方法，具有顺序更新能力，能够实现数据高效的自适应学习，在准确性和计算效率上均优于集成方法，为机器学习原子间势能的主动学习提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials bridge the gap between quantum chemical precision and classical computational speed, enabling molecular dynamics simulations with first-principles accuracy. Their reliability is often improved through active learning, which iteratively expands the training set by identifying uncertain, out-of-distribution configurations. Existing uncertainty-quantification methods often involve a trade-off between computational cost and reliability, and generally cannot account for redundancy as an acquisition batch is assembled. Here, we introduce AdaptNTK, a single-model framework that measures uncertainty as a regularized Mahalanobis distance in empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) feature space. With the NTK features fixed during acquisition, the uncertainty depends on the acquired configurations but not their reference labels. This allows the uncertainty to be updated recursively after each selection without retraining, reducing redundancy within an acquisition batch. On held-out rMD17 data, AdaptNTK achieves the highest mean correlations with force errors (Spearman 0.68, Pearson 0.71) and matches a three-member ensemble in error retention. In active learning experiments, AdaptNTK achieves the lowest force errors across rMD17 and Transition-1X, with particularly strong performance on transition-state configurations in Transition-1X. AdaptNTK provides a 2.6-fold speedup per Transition-1X cycle relative to the ensemble, providing efficient single-model uncertainty estimation with sequential updates for data-efficient active learning.

</details>


### [151] [A hybrid quantum-classical neural network for learning to route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00489)
*Marcus Rolf Peter Ritt,Alexsandro Santos da Rosa Júnior,Marcos Vinicius Reballo,Cesar Augusto do Amaral,Fernando Augusto Caletti de Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨混合量子-经典神经网络在路由启发式学习中的应用，重点考察小型量子神经网络能否替代竞争性注意力路由模型中参数密集的模块。针对容量约束车辆路径问题，编码器前馈替换是最有前景的设计，可将模型参数减少56.6%，在小中型实例上保持接近经典神经基线的解质量，但在较大实例上差距增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子神经网络在神经组合优化中的实际价值，检验小型量子网络能否作为参数压缩策略替代经典注意力路由模型中的参数密集模块，同时保持解质量。

Method: 采用混合量子-经典神经网络框架，在竞争性基于注意力的路由模型内部，用小型量子神经网络替换参数密集的模块（重点考察编码器前馈替换），并针对容量约束车辆路径问题进行评估，与经典神经基线和经典路由算法进行对比。

Result: 编码器前馈替换是最有前景的设计，模型参数减少56.6%，在小中型实例上混合模型接近经典神经基线水平，但大型实例上性能差距扩大。与经典路由算法相比，混合模型并不占优，未显示量子优势或求解器主导性。

Conclusion: 虽然结果未表明量子优势，但编码器前馈替换被确认为神经组合优化的可行混合模块压缩策略，为量子神经网络在路由启发式学习中的应用提供了有价值的探索方向。

Abstract: This work studies hybrid quantum-classical neural networks for learning routing heuristics. Specifically, this paper asks whether small quantum neural networks can replace parameter-heavy modules inside a competitive attention-based routing model while maintaining solution quality. For the capacitated vehicle routing problem, encoder feed-forward replacement emerges as the most promising design: it reduces the number of model parameters by 56.6% while keeping the hybrid model close to the classical neural baseline at small and medium instance sizes, although the gap grows for larger instances. This work also compares to classical routing algorithms, which remain highly competitive and often superior on the fixed Euclidean test sets. Our results therefore do not indicate quantum advantage or solver dominance, but identify encoder feed-forward replacement as a viable hybrid-module compression strategy for neural combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [152] [VATO: A Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator for Unsteady Separated Aerofoil Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00507)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Yichen Li,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VATO（涡力感知Transformer算子），将涡力图谱（VFM）方法与几何感知神经算子相结合，通过VATO-S（训练监督）和VATO-A（注意力加权）两种机制提升非定常分离流的流场预测与气动力精度，在54条轨迹、9种几何构型的双缘板翼型CFD数据上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD能解析非定常分离流中的非线性分离和涡脱落机制，但计算成本高昂，难以在设计优化和控制中重复使用。而标准的场级代理模型训练未区分对气动载荷贡献最大的流场区域，导致预测精度有限。因此需要开发一种能够关注力相关流场区域的高效代理建模方法。

Method: 论文提出VATO框架，将VFM（涡力图谱）方法与几何感知Transformer算子耦合，通过两种互补机制实现：(1) VATO-S：在训练阶段增加对局部VFM力贡献场的监督，不增加模型大小或推理成本；(2) VATO-A：利用VFM力贡献场和敏感度场，优先关注力相关源位置进行残差交叉注意力计算。

Result: 在1-20毫秒的预测时域内，VATO-S使速度、压力和涡量误差分别降低10.4%、1.0%和15.6%；VATO-A分别降低15.8%、7.5%和31.2%。VATO-S在VFM导出的阻力误差上表现最佳，而VATO-A在压力导出的升力和阻力误差上表现最佳。在超出训练范围50%的时域上，VATO-A仍保持涡量误差降低26.9%，并在全部四种力读数上保持更大改进。

Conclusion: 力感知的算子学习能够同时提升非定常分离流中流场预测的精度和气动功能的准确性，验证了将领域物理知识（涡力贡献）嵌入神经算子架构的有效性，为气动设计与控制中的高效代理建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of unsteady separated flows is challenging because the aerodynamic loads depend on nonlinear separation and vortex-shedding dynamics. Although high-fidelity CFD resolves these mechanisms, its cost limits repeated use in design and control. Standard field-level surrogate training, however, does not distinguish the flow regions that contribute most strongly to the aerodynamic loads. We introduce VATO (Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator), which couples the Vortex Force Map (VFM) method to a geometry-aware neural operator through two complementary mechanisms. VATO-S adds training-only supervision of the local VFM force-contribution field, with no increase in model size or inference cost. VATO-A uses VFM contribution and sensitivity fields to prioritise force-relevant source locations for residual cross attention. The methods are evaluated on unsteady CFD data for double-edged-plate aerofoils over 54 trajectories from nine geometries. Over lead times of 1-20~ms, VATO-S reduces velocity, pressure, and vorticity errors by 10.4\%, 1.0\%, and 15.6\%, respectively, while VATO-A achieves reductions of 15.8\%, 7.5\%, and 31.2\%. VATO-S gives the lowest VFM-derived drag error, whereas VATO-A gives the lowest pressure-derived lift and drag errors. Over lead times extending 50\% beyond the training range, VATO-A retains a 26.9\% reduction in vorticity error and larger improvements in all four force readouts, despite reduced gains in velocity and pressure. These results show that force-aware operator learning can improve both flow-field prediction and aerodynamic functional accuracy in unsteady separated flows.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Task-Specific Antibody Representations via Function-Aware Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00518)
*Ayan Goel,Thomas A. Walton,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"功能感知掩码"（function-aware masking）预训练策略族，通过将掩码位置与抗体特定的功能先验（如IMGT注释或结构预测）对齐，来塑造抗体的语言模型表示空间。该方法在结构相关任务上最高提升14%，在CDR相关任务上最高提升5.9倍，并进一步提出融合多种先验的混合掩码策略来平衡结合、结构和生物物理目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的抗体语言模型虽通过掩码语言建模（MLM）预训练学习了关键表示，但掩码破坏过程本身很少被用作预训练中的归纳偏置来源。虽然优先掩码互补决定区（CDRs）能改善结合相关预测，但抗体具有多样的生物学先验，需要更通用的方法将功能知识注入模型训练。

Method: 提出功能感知掩码算法族，将掩码放置与特定功能先验（来自IMGT注释或结构预测）对齐。同时开发混合掩码策略，整合多个先验，在重建过程中平衡结合、结构和生物物理等多个功能目标。

Result: 专用掩码策略在其对应目标上显著提升性能：结构相关任务最高提升14%，CDR相关任务最高提升5.9倍。混合掩码策略在多功能轴上进一步改善整体表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，信息丰富的掩码放置为在抗体语言模型训练中施加功能归纳偏置提供了一种无需额外参数的有效机制。

Abstract: Antibody-specific language models pretrained via masked language modeling (MLM) learn representations that are critical for downstream sequence design and property prediction tasks. Yet, the corruption process itself is rarely leveraged as a source of inductive bias during pretraining. While preferentially masking complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) improves binding-related predictions, antibodies possess diverse biological priors over a variety of functions. Herein, we introduce function-aware masking, a family of pretraining algorithms that align mask placement with specific functional priors (e.g., from IMGT annotations or structure predictions) to shape the learned representation space. We show that these specialist masking strategies significantly improve performance on their respective objectives, yielding up to a 14% gain on structure-related tasks and up to a 5.9x improvement on CDR-related tasks. To further improve performance across multiple functional axes, we develop hybrid masking strategies that integrate multiple priors, balancing reconstruction over binding, structural, and biophysical objectives. Our results demonstrate that informed mask placement provides a parameter-free mechanism for imposing functional inductive biases in antibody language model training.

</details>


### [154] [Why Multi-Layer Message Passing Works: Completeness Theory for Graph Neural Network Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00528)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文证明了超图神经网络（具有三体消息传递的不变架构）是势能面的通用逼近器，并通过多层完备性理论表明：稀疏截断图上的L层消息传递与完整L跳邻域具有相同的表示能力，为实际机器学习原子间势中常用的多层截断消息传递做法提供了首个严格的理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中几乎所有基于图神经网络原子的机器学习势能都采用每层截断半径小于物理交互范围的多层消息传递设置，但此前缺乏严格的理论证明来支撑这一广泛实践，特别是关于多层截断消息传递是否能达到完整邻域表示能力的问题。

Method: 提出了一个多层完备性理论，证明在配置是通用的、满足重叠条件和连通性条件的假设下，L层稀疏截断图的机制消息传递可以实现与完整L跳邻域信息相同的表达能力，并基于此推导出超图神经网络的通用逼近性质。

Result: 证明了超图神经网络是势能面的通用逼近器，并进一步推得DPA3和CHGNet两种实用架构也继承了通用逼近性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为首个对多层消息传递配合逐层截断方法的严格理论论证，填补了图神经网络原子间势在表示能力理论上的空白。

Abstract: We prove that the Hypergraph Neural Network, an invariant architecture with 3-body message passing, is a universal approximator for potential energy surfaces. Our main contribution is a multi-layer completeness theory. We show that $L$ layers of message passing on sparse, cutoff-based graphs achieve the same representational power as having access to the full $L$-hop neighborhood, provided the configurations are generic, satisfy an overlap condition and a connectivity condition. This provides the first rigorous justification for the common practice of using multi-layer message passing with a per-layer cutoff smaller than the physical interaction range, the setting used by virtually all practical graph neural network based machine-learned interatomic potentials. As immediate consequences, we show that both DPA3 and CHGNet architectures inherit universal approximation.

</details>


### [155] [DeSyR: A Decoupled Symbolic Recovery Framework with PINN-Guided Structure Search and Physics-Informed Coefficient Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00530)
*Pancheng Niu,Jun Guo,Qiaolin He,Jingcai Guo,Yanchao Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeSyR框架，通过解耦符号恢复流程（先用物理信息神经网络引导符号拓扑搜索，再仅基于物理方程精炼系数），解决从神经近似中恢复紧凑显式解时教师误差被继承的问题。在15个微分方程问题/18种配置上验证，精炼后的相对L2误差低至2.31×10⁻¹⁴，相比同拓扑比较降低8-14个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从神经近似中恢复紧凑显式符号解具有挑战性，因为不完美的教师数据会同时干扰符号拓扑搜索和系数估计，导致恢复出的解携带教师误差尺度。

Method: DeSyR框架采用解耦的两阶段策略：第一阶段利用物理信息神经网络指导重复搜索以构建候选符号拓扑（含临时常数）；第二阶段在拓扑固定后，仅从控制方程和预设约束中精炼系数，并结合门控选择与验证。作者还针对线性固定拓扑参数化刻画了教师误差继承机制，并在适定性、可表示性、零残差可达性和离散确定性等条件下给出物理精炼的理论保证。

Result: 在15个微分方程问题共18种配置（覆盖高阶、时空、多维、非线性和耦合系统）上评估：候选级审计中自由参数重拟合收敛率达99.23%，所有涉及自由系数的选定精炼均收敛；配置级中位精炼相对L2误差为2.31×10⁻¹⁴或更低；相同拓扑比较下精炼将误差降低8至14个数量级。

Conclusion: 近似神经教师可以引导符号拓扑发现，而不会将其误差尺度强加于最终恢复的系数上，前提是保留目标可表示的拓扑且物理-only精炼收敛。

Abstract: Recovering compact explicit solutions from neural approximations is challenging when imperfect teacher data guide symbolic topology search and coefficient estimation. We present DeSyR, a decoupled symbolic recovery framework for differential equations. A physics-informed neural network guides repeated searches to construct candidate topologies with provisional constants. Once a topology is fixed, its coefficients are refined solely from the governing equation and prescribed constraints, followed by gated selection and verification. For linear fixed-topology parameterizations, we characterize teacher-error inheritance and show that finite-weight mixed data--physics fitting retains an $O(β^{-1})$ teacher-dependent contribution when the teacher error projects onto the model space. Under well-posedness, representability, zero-residual attainment, and discrete determinacy, physics-only refinement conditionally recovers exact coefficients; for nonlinear parameterizations, the corresponding guarantees are local. DeSyR is evaluated on 15 differential-equation problems across 18 configurations covering high-order, space--time, multidimensional, nonlinear, and coupled systems. A candidate-level audit yields a 99.23% convergence rate among free-parameter refits, while every selected refinement involving free coefficients converges. Configuration-level median refined relative $L_2$ errors are $2.31\times10^{-14}$ or lower. In same-topology comparisons, refinement reduces error by eight to fourteen orders of magnitude. These results show that an approximate neural teacher can guide topology discovery without imposing its error scale on final recovered coefficients, provided a target-capable topology is retained and physics-only refinement converges.

</details>


### [156] [GenONet: A Generative operator Network for High-Resolution Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00544)
*Mohammad Kian Golkar,Luciano Alves de Oliveira,Mohammad Khanjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GenONet（Spatio-Temporal U-DeepONet），一种用于长达3小时高分辨率降水临近预报的新型深度学习架构，将Deep Operator Network（DeepONet）作为生成器嵌入GAN框架中，并结合基于水分守恒方程的物理信息损失正则化，以生成清晰、物理一致的长期降水预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水临近预报对减轻恶劣天气影响至关重要，但风暴快速演化使长时程预测困难。现有深度学习模型在较长预报时段上预测技能退化，产生越来越模糊的预报结果，无法捕捉风暴系统复杂非线性演化过程。

Method: GenONet的核心创新是将DeepONet作为GAN框架中的生成器，利用DeepONet学习降水的连续时间动力学以确保长时间预报稳定性；通过空间-时间判别器的对抗训练使模型输出清晰连贯的预报；引入源自水分守恒方程的物理信息损失项以增强物理合理性。

Result: 定量评估显示GenONet在多数指标上（尤其在强降水事件和更长前瞻时间）获得一致更高的分数；定性上GenONet生成结构连贯的预报，而基线模型退化为模糊模式；消融研究证实物理信息损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 将算子学习与对抗训练相结合能够有效解决降水临近预报中长时程模糊退化问题，物理信息损失进一步提升预报的物理一致性和准确性。

Abstract: High-resolution precipitation nowcasting is critical for reducing the impacts of severe weather but remains difficult because of rapid storm evolution. Deep learning models have shown great promise for this task, but their predictive skill often deteriorates over longer forecast horizons. This leads to increasingly blurry forecasts that fail to capture the complex, non-linear evolution of storm systems. In order to address these limitations, we introduce Spatio-Temporal U-DeepONet (GenONet), a novel architecture for long-range precipitation forecasting up to 3 hours, specifically designed to produce sharp and physically consistent results. GenONet's architecture pioneers the use of a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) as a generator within a Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework for this task. The DeepONet learns the continuous-time dynamics of precipitation, ensuring stability over long forecast horizons. Adversial training against a spatio-temporal discriminator compels the model to produce sharp, coherent forecasts, while a physics-informed loss regularizer, derived from the Moisture Conservation Equation, improves physical plausibility in our ablation setting. Quantitative evaluations show that our model achieves consistently higher scores on most of the metrics, especially for highintensity events and at longer lead times. Qualitatively, GenONet produces structurally coherent forecasts that maintain their integrity, whereas baseline models degrade into indistinct patterns. Finally, an ablation study confirms the benefit of this physics-informed loss, highlighting the strength of combining operator learning with adversarial training.

</details>


### [157] [REFACTOR-VLA: Unsupervised Library Learning of Typed Motor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01215)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFACTOR-VLA提出了一种"醒/睡"两阶段系统来学习可复用的技能抽象。睡眠阶段通过行为等价核（BEK）与世界模型聚类运动程序片段，清醒阶段将抽象编译为类型化的lambda项，由库条件矩形流动作解码器消费。实验表明世界模型参数增加反而有害，而辅助InfoNCE对比损失显著提升聚类效果，并首次产出真实LIBERO任务语言库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型（如OpenVLA、π₀、RT-2、RDT-1B）都是单体式的，直接输出原始电机命令或短动作片段，没有将行为组织为可复用的抽象，导致在长时程任务上性能退化且难以解释。已有技能发现方法回避了"两个动作序列何时行为等价"这一核心问题，要么聚类对比嵌入，要么委托给未针对机器人动力学校准的语言模型判断。

Method: REFACTOR-VLA采用醒/睡两阶段框架：睡眠阶段利用学习到的潜在世界模型Mφ的rollouts计算行为等价核（BEK），对运动程序片段聚类；清醒阶段生成Hindley-Milner启发的词汇上的类型化lambda项，由库条件矩形流动作解码器消费。抽象需通过最小描述长度（MDL）与返回保持门控才被采纳。

Result: 在LIBERO上：将世界模型从188M扩大到430M参数在4/4套件上性能反而下降；训练目标更为关键——在世界模型预热阶段增加辅助InfoNCE监督对比损失大幅改善睡眠阶段聚类，NMI在n=3种子下达到object 0.462±0.021、spatial 0.867±0.025、goal 0.915±0.013、LIBERO-10 0.754±0.010，4个套件平均超越最强基线Δ=+0.184。n=12 providers的95% bootstrap置信区间对平均成对NMI为[0.683, 0.729]。睡眠阶段产出首个真实LIBERO任务语言库：解码器使用3个候选抽象中的2个，改写全部256条采样演示。

Conclusion: REFACTOR-VLA验证了世界模型容量并不直接转化为VLA技能发现的更好性能，而训练目标的设计（尤其是监督对比损失）才是提升行为抽象聚类质量的关键因素。该系统首次在真实LIBERO基准上构建出可被解码器采纳的任务语言库，为VLA向可复用、可解释的结构化行为抽象方向发展提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models -- OpenVLA, $π_0$, RT-2, RDT-1B -- are monolithic: they emit raw motor commands or short action chunks without organizing behavior into reusable abstractions, so they degrade on long-horizon tasks and resist interpretation. Existing skill-discovery methods sidestep the core question of when two action sequences are behaviorally equivalent, either clustering contrastive embeddings or delegating the judgment to a language model uncalibrated to the robot's dynamics. We introduce REFACTOR-VLA, a wake/sleep system for learning reusable skills. Its sleep phase clusters motor-program fragments under a Behavioral-Equivalence Kernel (BEK) computed from rollouts of a learned latent world model $M_φ$; its wake phase emits typed lambda terms over a Hindley--Milner-inspired vocabulary, consumed by a library-conditioned rectified-flow action decoder. Abstractions are admitted only if they pass Minimum Description Length and return-preservation gates. On LIBERO we report two findings. First, enlarging the world model from 188M to 430M parameters worsened performance on 4 of 4 suites, so capacity alone does not help. Second, the training objective matters far more: adding an auxiliary supervised contrastive (InfoNCE) loss during world-model warmup substantially improves sleep-phase clustering, giving Normalized Mutual Information at $n=3$ seeds of $0.462 \pm 0.021$ (object), $0.867 \pm 0.025$ (spatial), $0.915 \pm 0.013$ (goal) and $0.754 \pm 0.010$ (LIBERO-10), and beating the strongest published baseline on all 4 suites by a mean $Δ= +0.184$. Across providers ($n=12$) the 95% bootstrap confidence interval for mean pairwise NMI is $[0.683, 0.729]$ (mean $0.705$). The sleep phase also yields the first real-LIBERO task-language library: the decoder uses 2 of 3 admitted abstractions and rewrites all 256 sampled demonstrations.

</details>


### [158] [Manifold-Aware General Coded Computing for Straggler-Resilient Distributed Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00552)
*Parsa Moradi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流形感知的编码策略（Manifold-Aware GCC），利用高维数据的低维流形几何结构来设计编码样本，而非像传统编码计算那样忽略或压缩数据结构。实验表明该方法在节点故障（straggling）情况下相比标准GCC能显著降低均方恢复误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码计算设计未显式利用输入数据的内在结构。通信系统中先压缩再编码的原则不适用于编码计算，因为机器学习任务恰恰需要利用数据结构来推断输出。因此需要一种保留并利用数据流形结构的编码设计方案，而非通过源编码消除结构。

Method: 借鉴图基流形学习的思想，提出流形感知的一般编码计算（GCC）编码策略。与Reed-Solomon等传统信道编码在低维代数表示上求值不同，该方法设计编码样本使其沿数据的自然流形分布，而非强加与数据分布无关的人工低维结构。

Result: 在神经网络推理和高维多项式评估实验上，所提策略相比标准GCC在节点故障条件下持续且显著地降低了均方恢复误差。

Conclusion: 利用数据内在几何结构（流形）进行编码设计能够有效提升编码计算在节点故障场景下的恢复性能，为编码计算提供了一种从数据结构出发的新视角。

Abstract: Existing coded-computing designs do not explicitly exploit the intrinsic structure of the input data. In communication systems, statistical structure and redundancy are often removed through source coding (or compression) before channel coding is applied. This principle, however, does not transfer directly to coded computation. In many computational tasks, particularly in machine learning, the structure of the data is precisely what the computation seeks to exploit to infer outputs or learn meaningful patterns. Consequently, coded-computing schemes should preserve and leverage this structure in their code design, rather than ignoring or eliminating it through source coding.
  This observation motivates a different perspective on code construction. In many channel-coding schemes, such as Reed-Solomon codes, coded symbols are generated by evaluating a low-dimensional algebraic representation at selected points. In contrast, many high-dimensional datasets naturally concentrate near low-dimensional manifolds. In this paper, we exploit this intrinsic geometry by designing coded samples that follow the natural manifold of the data, rather than imposing an artificial low-dimensional structure unrelated to the data distribution. Inspired by graph-based manifold learning, we propose a manifold-aware encoding strategy for general coded computing (GCC). Experiments on neural network inference and high-dimensional polynomial evaluation demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently and significantly reduces the mean squared recovery error under straggling compared with standard GCC.

</details>


### [159] [EEG-VID: Task-Guided Latent Predictive Pretraining for EEG Decoding and Assistive Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00566)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-VID框架，一种面向EEG解码的任务引导隐式预测预训练方法，通过指数移动平均目标编码器和弱任务引导预测未来隐式EEG状态，再经监督微调。在VIG-48和BCI Competition IV-2a/IV-2b数据集上，42项对比中有41项准确率提升，最大提升16.22个百分点；跨天任务达Top-1 6.52%和Top-5 30.50%；离线机器人场景候选约束目标选择达40.24%（随机水平25%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码在跨会话和跨受试者场景下性能显著退化，现有预训练方法难以有效迁移，需要一种能够应对session和subject分布偏移的通用表征学习策略。

Method: 构建任务引导的潜在预测预训练框架：利用指数移动平均（EMA）目标编码器从近期历史EEG状态预测未来隐状态，并结合弱任务监督信号引导预训练过程，随后在下游任务中进行监督微调。

Result: 在42项匹配的骨干网络-数据集-协议比较中，41项Stage 1准确率提升（含全部12项留一受试者交叉验证设置），最大提升16.22个百分点。VIG-48跨天任务Top-1为6.52%、Top-5为30.50%；六人离线机器人场景中候选约束目标选择达40.24%（随机基线25%）。

Conclusion: 任务引导的隐状态预测是一种可迁移的EEG解码预训练策略，在跨会话/跨受试者场景及场景约束辅助目标选择任务中均展现一致提升，验证了该预训练范式的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose EEG-VID, a task-guided latent predictive pretraining framework for EEG decoding under session and subject shifts. EEG-VID predicts future latent EEG states from recent history using an exponential-moving-average target encoder and weak task guidance, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Across VIG-48 and BCI Competition IV-2a/IV-2b, Stage 1 improves mean accuracy in 41 of 42 matched backbone-dataset-protocol comparisons, including all 12 leave-one-subject-out settings, with a maximum gain of 16.22 percentage points. On the 48-region cross-day VIG-48 task, EEG-VID achieves 6.52% Top-1 and 30.50% Top-5 accuracy. In a separate six-participant offline robot-scene study, candidate-constrained target selection reaches 40.24% versus a 25% chance level after subject-specific calibration. These results support task-guided latent prediction as a transferable pretraining strategy for EEG decoding and scene-constrained assistive target selection.

</details>


### [160] [GeoPAR: Large-Scale Multi-Agent Combinatorial Optimization with Geometry-Guided Parallel Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00577)
*Wenjian Wu,Zesheng Jia,Jiaying Tang,Benyuan Yang,Jin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoPAR，一个基于几何引导的并行自回归强化学习框架，用于可扩展的多智能体组合优化问题。它通过投影窗口稀疏几何机制、稀疏边偏置注意力和缓存引导的冲突感知分配三个关键组件，在异构车辆路径规划和开放式多车场取送货问题上实现了更好的大规模零样本泛化，同时显著减少了推理步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行自回归神经求解器虽然提高了推理效率，但在大规模实例上性能退化，主要原因是缺乏对局部几何结构的有效建模，且冲突的任务选择只能在行动生成后才被处理。

Method: GeoPAR包含三个核心组件：(1) 投影窗口稀疏几何机制，通过多方向投影构建轻量级局部候选邻域；(2) 稀疏边偏置注意力，将几何关系注入节点表示中；(3) 缓存引导的冲突感知分配，在解码过程中重用几何缓存以抑制独占任务的重复选择。

Result: 在异构车辆路径规划和开放式多车场取送货问题上的实验表明，GeoPAR提升了大规模零样本泛化性能，同时大幅减少了展开（rollout）步骤数量并保持了高效的推理。

Conclusion: 通过将几何结构信息显式地引入神经网络求解器并在解码阶段处理冲突选择，GeoPAR有效解决了并行自回归方法在大规模多智能体组合优化问题上的性能瓶颈，实现了可扩展性与求解质量的更好权衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent combinatorial optimization problems are notoriously challenging due to their NP-hard nature. Recent parallel autoregressive neural solvers improve inference efficiency by allowing agents to make decisions simultaneously, but their performance often degrades on large-scale instances. This is largely attributable to weak modeling of local geometric structures and the fact that conflicting task selections are handled only after action generation. To address these limitations, we propose GeoPAR, a geometry-guided parallel autoregressive reinforcement learning framework for scalable multi-agent combinatorial optimization. GeoPAR integrates three key components: (1) a projection-window sparse geometry mechanism that builds lightweight local candidate neighborhoods through multi-directional projections, (2) sparse edge-biased attention that injects these geometric relations into node representations, and (3) cache-guided conflict-aware assignment that reuses the geometric cache during decoding to suppress duplicate selections of exclusive tasks. Experiments on heterogeneous vehicle routing and open multi-depot pickup-and-delivery problems show that GeoPAR improves large-scale zero-shot generalization while substantially reducing rollout steps and maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [161] [CRAFT: Fine-Tuning Pre-hoc Explainability in AI-native 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00590)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT方法，一种数据驱动的冷启动推理对齐方法，通过自动生成和验证（输入、推理链、标签）三元组并用LoRA微调小语言模型，解决了电信SLM在预-hoc推理任务中强化学习面临的冷启动难题，在精度、F1和能效上均显著优于GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN架构中嵌入的小语言模型需要在6G网络中执行实时遥测推理，但现有RANSTRUCT式的监督微调只能进行事后（post-hoc）合理化，决策过程不可审计。虽然RL方法（如GRPO）在通用LLM推理领域取得进展，但直接移植到电信场景遭遇冷启动障碍：SLM要么学会输出格式、要么学会预测标签，很少两者兼得。

Method: CRAFT采用数据驱动方案：自主生成经过验证的（输入、推理轨迹、标签）三元组数据集，利用低秩适配（LoRA）对SLM进行微调，避免了GRPO所需的巨大计算开销。此外CRAFT初始化策略可作为后续GRPO微调的稳健基础。

Result: 在TRACTOR和IC xApp电信数据集上，CRAFT分别达到86.5%和94.6%的准确率与F1分数，零解析失败；而直接GRPO和SFT+GRPO的F1不超过28%和53.5%且存在多次解析失败。CRAFT比GRPO基线节省59%能耗。

Conclusion: CRAFT为6G RAN中可部署、可审计的AI提供了一条可持续路径，能有效克服电信领域SLM推理对齐的冷启动问题，同时作为预初始化策略可为后续GRPO微调提供稳健基础。

Abstract: The next generation of mobile networks is envisioned as fully AI-native, with AI-RAN architectures embedding small language models (SLMs) to perform reasoning over real-time telemetry. The state-of-the-art training paradigms for telecom LLMs, exemplified by RANSTRUCT-style supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated instruction data, are limited to post hoc rationalization. Here, the explanations, when produced at all, are generated after or independently of the decision, leaving the decision process unauditable. Pre-hoc reasoning, where a causal reasoning trace is produced before the output label, is preferable, and the broader LLM reasoning literature has made real progress toward it via RL methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Here we observe that transplanting this recipe into the telecom setting runs into a cold-start barrier: SLMs either learn to output the desired format or learn to predict the label, but rarely both. We identify this barrier and propose CRAFT, which stands for Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning, a data-centric method to autonomously generate a verified dataset of (input, trace, label) triplets. CRAFT fine-tunes SLMs on this verified data using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), requiring substantially less compute and wall-clock time than GRPO-based methods. On the TRACTOR and IC xApp telecom datasets, CRAFT achieves up to 86.5% and 94.6% for accuracy and F1 with no parse failures, while direct GRPO and SFT+GRPO fail to exceed 28% and 53.5% F1 with multiple parse failures. We further show that CRAFT-initialized policies serve as a robust foundation for subsequent GRPO fine-tuning, as under diverse reward functions the performance remains consistent with no parse failures. Finally, we demonstrate that CRAFT consumes 59% less energy than GRPO-based baselines, making it a sustainable path to deployable, auditable AI in 6G RAN.

</details>


### [162] [Topological Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00597)
*Benoît Guérand,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑引导（Topological Steering）框架，利用持久性图（persistence diagrams）从激活空间的拓扑表示出发引导大语言模型行为，实现对不可取行为的鲁棒控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM行为控制方法直接在激活或特征空间中干预，容易对离群点、分布偏移、噪声和局部扰动敏感，缺乏对全局结构的把握。

Method: 受拓扑数据分析（TDA）启发，通过持久性图将激活空间表示转化为拓扑特征，将激活引导与TDA相结合，实现多尺度、全局结构感知的行为引导。

Result: 该框架在多个模型家族和多种模型规模上均能一致地修改LLM行为，验证了方法的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: Topological Steering通过拓扑表示提供了一种比传统激活空间干预更稳健的LLM行为控制方案，能够在多种模型上实现一致且可靠的行为引导。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), controlling undesirable model behaviors has become increasingly important. Existing behavioral control methods typically intervene directly in activation or feature space, but such approaches can be sensitive to outliers, distributional shifts, noise, and other local perturbations. Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steering LLM behavior through the topological representation of activation spaces. Using persistence diagrams, our method connects activation-based steering with TDA and enables more robust behavioral control. We show that Topological Steering consistently modifies LLM behavior across multiple model families and model sizes.

</details>


### [163] [Confess What You Know: Forget-Set Misalignment with Model Knowledge in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00605)
*Miso Kim,Georu Lee,Seungwon Jeong,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM机器遗忘中"遗忘集错位"问题（遗忘集与模型实际记忆内容不一致），分为欠遗忘和越界遗忘两种情况，并提出数据盲框架CONFS，通过引导模型表达已记忆知识来构建对齐的遗忘集，在多项基准上取得接近金标准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法通常假设预定义的遗忘集与模型实际记忆内容匹配，但在真实隐私场景中原始训练数据不可得，这一假设经常失效，导致遗忘效果不佳或模型效用受损。

Method: 通过梯度级分析证明遗忘集错位问题源于非对齐的遗忘目标而非具体优化选择；提出CONFS（Confession-to-Forget-Set）框架，通过引导模型"坦白"其记忆的知识并将其形式化，构建与模型对齐的遗忘集，完全无需访问原始训练数据。

Result: 在合成、多模态及真实世界基准上，CONFS在多个指标上接近金标准性能，实现有竞争力的遗忘-效用平衡，且在保持模型效用方面优于其他数据盲遗忘集构建方法。

Conclusion: 遗忘集与模型记忆内容的对齐是实现有效且不损伤效用的机器遗忘的关键，而通过模型自身"坦白"记忆来构建遗忘集是数据不可得场景下可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) often assumes that a pre-defined forget set matches what the model has memorized, but this frequently breaks in realistic privacy settings where the original training data is inaccessible. We term this gap forget-set misalignment and identify two cases. In Under Unlearning, the forget set omits memorized information and leakage persists. In Out-of-Knowledge Unlearning, the algorithm is driven to "forget" knowledge the model never learned, perturbing parameters and degrading utility. Using gradient-level analysis, we show these behaviors arise from misaligned unlearning targets rather than specific optimization choices. We then propose CONfession-to-Forget-Set (CONFS), a data-blind framework that constructs model-aligned forget sets by eliciting and formalizing the model's memorized knowledge. Across synthetic, multimodal, and real-world benchmarks, CONFS approaches Gold-standard performance on several metrics and achieves a competitive forgetting-utility balance, while preserving utility better than other data-blind forget-set constructions.

</details>


### [164] [Breaking the Structural Identity: Personalized Federated LoRA Fine-tuning under Rank Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00632)
*Lei Wang,Jieming Bian,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedRoRA框架，在秩异构联邦学习中实现细粒度个性化LoRA适配，通过共享全局方向和个性化秩级幅度的解耦设计，在NLU和NLG基准上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有秩异构联邦LoRA方法主要解决维度不匹配的聚合问题，但为所有客户端提供统一的全局模型，无法在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据场景下捕获客户端特定特征，缺乏细粒度的个性化能力。

Method: FedRoRA将适配解耦为共享的全局方向和由可学习的对角缩放因子控制的个性化秩级幅度。服务器端通过奇异值分解（SVD）提取全局子空间，并通过个性化投影和top-k选择机制重新分配客户端特定的初始化。

Result: 在自然语言理解（NLU）和自然语言生成（NLG）基准上的大量实验表明，FedRoRA在多个任务上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过秩级个性化的解耦设计，FedRoRA有效解决了联邦学习中的资源异构和数据异构双重挑战，为秩异构联邦LoRA提供了更精细的个性化方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, but their adaptation to privacy-sensitive, distributed datasets remains a challenge. While Federated Learning (FL) combined with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides a resource-efficient paradigm for collaborative fine-tuning, practical deployments are hindered by the dual challenges of resource heterogeneity and data heterogeneity. Existing rank-heterogeneous methods primarily focus on bridging dimension mismatches for aggregation but typically provide a unified global model for all clients sharing the same rank, failing to capture client-specific features in non-IID scenarios. In this paper, we propose FedRoRA (Federated Rank-wise Personalized LoRA), a novel framework that enables fine-grained personalization within rank-heterogeneous federations. FedRoRA decouples adaptation into shared global directions and personalized rank-wise magnitudes governed by learnable diagonal scales. On the server side, it extracts a global subspace via singular value decomposition (SVD) and redistributes client-specific initializations through a personalized projection and top-$k$ selection mechanism. Extensive experiments on NLU and NLG benchmarks demonstrate that FedRoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [165] [DK-GBMKKM: Dynamic Kernel-Space Granular-Ball Multiple Kernel $k$-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00647)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Yuchao Zhang,Shuyin Xia,Siqi Zhong,Xuzhao Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态核空间粒球多核k均值聚类方法（DK-GBMKKM），在融合核空间中动态生成粒球表示，交替更新核权重与粒球隶属度，以解决粒球在输入空间中静态生成与融合核几何不一致的问题，并在12个公开数据集上验证了优越的聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核k均值聚类对噪声和边界样本敏感，且逐样本优化计算代价高；粒球表示虽能组织局部样本群为介观单元，但传统方法在输入空间一次性生成粒球，这与多核学习过程中动态演化的融合核几何不一致，导致表示失真。

Method: 提出DK-GBMKKM方法：在当前融合核空间中生成粒球，并交替进行核权重学习与粒球隶属度更新，使表示适应融合核几何的动态变化；构建样本量加权粒球核以保留不同大小粒球的贡献，并证明了其半正定性和等价性质。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上的实验表明，DK-GBMKKM具有显著的整体聚类性能优势，且代码已开源。

Conclusion: 动态核空间粒球表示能够有效缓解输入空间静态粒球与融合核几何不一致的问题，在提升聚类鲁棒性和效率的同时保持了良好的理论性质，为多核聚类提供了新的粒度化视角。

Abstract: Multiple kernel $k$-means integrates complementary nonlinear similarities by learning a combination of base kernels. Its pointwise optimization, however, is sensitive to noisy and boundary samples and repeatedly operates on sample-scale kernel matrices. Granular-ball representations organize local sample groups into mesoscopic units, but granular balls generated once in the input space may be inconsistent with the fused-kernel geometry that evolves during multiple kernel learning. We propose dynamic kernel-space granular-ball multiple kernel $k$-means (DK-GBMKKM). The method generates granular balls in the current fused kernel space and alternates kernel-weight learning with granular-ball membership updates, allowing the representation to adapt to changes in the fused-kernel geometry. A sample-size-weighted granular-ball kernel is further constructed to preserve the contributions of balls of different sizes, and its positive semidefiniteness and related equivalence properties are established. Experiments on 12 public datasets demonstrate the strong overall clustering performance of DK-GBMKKM. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/DK-GBMKKM.

</details>


### [166] [EEG-AS: Instance-Level Foundation Model Selection for EEG Foundation Models via Behavior Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00653)
*Yunzhen Zhang,Ruoxi Piao,Hasan Onur Keles,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-AS框架，将脑电图（EEG）基础模型的实例级选择建模为算法选择（AS）问题，通过推断可用的潜伏EEG嵌入、手工神经生理学特征和锚定基础模型，在不执行整个模型组合的情况下估计各基础模型的行为，从而在七个公共EEG基准上显著缩小了单最优求解器（SBS）与实例级oracle上界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型在不同数据集或单个EEG实例上表现各异，没有一个单一模型能始终取得最佳效果，而实例级模型选择问题尚未被充分探索，需要一种自适应选择机制来解决这一局限。

Method: 将EEG基础模型选择形式化为实例级算法选择（AS）问题，提出EEG-AS框架。该框架利用推断可用的潜在EEG嵌入、手工设计的神经生理学特征以及一个锚定基础模型来表征每个EEG实例；训练时通过条件于锚定模型的特权预测token学习重建不可用的基础模型行为，推断时无需执行完整模型组合即可估计这些行为，实现从七个EEG基础模型中进行高效选择。

Result: 在七个公共EEG基准上的实验表明，EEG-AS显著缩小了单最优求解器（SBS）与每个实例的oracle上界之间的性能差距，验证了实例级AS在EEG基础模型自适应部署中的有效性。

Conclusion: 实例级算法选择为EEG基础模型的自适应部署提供了一条可行且有效的路径，能够弥补单一最优模型的不足，并接近理论上每个实例的最佳性能上限。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a non-invasive technique for measuring neural activity and has been widely used in neuroscience applications. Recent advances in EEG foundation models have enabled strong performance across diverse neural decoding tasks. However, no single foundation model consistently performs best across datasets or individual EEG instances, while instance-level model selection remains largely unexplored. To address this limitation, we formulate EEG foundation model selection as an instance-level Algorithm Selection (AS) problem. We propose \textbf{EEG-AS}, an instance-level algorithm selection framework that characterizes each EEG instance using inference-available latent EEG embeddings, handcrafted neurophysiological features, and an anchor foundation model. During training, EEG-AS learns to reconstruct unavailable foundation-model behaviors from privileged prediction tokens conditioned on an anchor foundation model, while during inference it estimates these behaviors without executing the entire model portfolio, enabling efficient selection from seven EEG foundation models. Experiments on seven public EEG benchmarks demonstrate that EEG-AS substantially narrows the gap between the Single Best Solver (SBS) and the oracle upper bound for each instance. These results highlight the effectiveness of instance-level AS for adaptive deployment of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [167] [HarmoCore: Functional Latent Diffusion for Sparse Reconstruction of Oscillatory Wave Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00679)
*Lihao Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HarmoCore，一种在紧凑连续的结构化波场隐空间中进行生成先验学习的框架，用于从极度稀疏传感器观测中重建振荡复值波场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏传感器重建振荡波场是一个严重欠定的逆问题，现有方法多针对实值平滑场设计，难以高效处理复值、高频振荡且仅含极端稀疏观测的波场，尤其难以扩展到3D场景。

Method: HarmoCore将联合实-虚通道表示为共享连续空间基上的Functional Tucker核，学习频率条件的核扩散先验，并在核空间中直接进行扩散后验采样；通过多线性解码器在固定传感器坐标处推导显式似然引导算子，避免像素空间密集修正，并可选加入目标方程残差引导增强物理一致性。

Result: 在2D Helmholtz方程、2D合成波场及3D Helmholtz方程实验中，在仅1%-2%传感率下取得显著重建增益，并且能在三维条件下保持实用性。

Conclusion: HarmoCore通过将生成先验嵌入低维结构化的波场隐空间，有效解决了振荡复值波场在极端稀疏传感下的高效重建问题，并展示了在3D场景中的可行性与物理一致性潜力。

Abstract: Reconstructing oscillatory wave fields from scattered sensors is a severely underdetermined inverse problem. Beyond the challenges of general physical-field reconstruction, wave responses are complex-valued, frequency-sensitive, and highly oscillatory, while costly simulation and sensing often leave only extreme-sparse observations. Existing low-rank, operator, and diffusion approaches are largely designed for real-valued, smoother fields; dense pixel-space diffusion is particularly inefficient for oscillatory complex fields and difficult to scale to 3D. We propose HarmoCore, which places a generative prior in a compact, continuous, and structured wave-field latent. HarmoCore represents joint real--imaginary channels with Functional Tucker cores over shared continuous spatial bases, learns a frequency-conditioned core diffusion prior, and performs Diffusion Posterior Sampling directly in core space. At fixed sensor coordinates, the multilinear decoder induces an explicit likelihood guidance operator, avoiding dense pixel-space correction. Optional target-equation residual guidance further promotes physical consistency. Experiments on 2D Helmholtz, 2D synthetic wave fields, and 3D Helmholtz show substantial gains under 1%--2% sensing while remaining practical in three dimensions.

</details>


### [168] [A Study of Hidden-State Optimization Order in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00686)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种边界优先的推理调度方法，将模型划分为多个块，先协调块边界处的隐藏状态，再细化块内表示。在预测编码网络（PCN）中实例化该方法后，在CIFAR-10上比标准预测编码分别提升9.77%（标准参数化）和5.51%（μ参数化）的准确率，表明块边界协调能显著增强深层网络的局部特征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部学习方法（如预测编码）是端到端反向传播的替代方案，但其无结构的局部目标在深层网络中会导致特征学习能力较弱。作者希望探究通过对隐藏状态进行有序优化（而非无结构地并行更新）是否能解决这一局限。

Method: 提出边界优先的推理调度：将模型划分为若干块，推理时先协调各块边界处的隐藏状态，然后在各块内部细化表示。将该调度实例化到预测编码网络（PCN）中，在训练和推理过程中按此顺序更新隐藏活动与预测误差。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，边界优先的预测编码方法较标准预测编码准确率提升9.77%（标准参数化）和5.51%（μ参数化）。诊断分析显示该方法产生更多非平凡的早期层更新、更低的初始至最终CKA值以及更多样化的层间梯度，均与更强的特征学习一致。

Conclusion: 边界优先的分块推理是预测编码训练中一种有效的设计原则，能够显著增强深层网络的特征学习能力，值得在更广泛的局部学习系统中进一步研究。

Abstract: Local learning methods offer an alternative to end-to-end backpropagation, but their unstructured local objectives can produce weak feature learning in deep networks. We study whether the order of hidden-state optimization can address this limitation. We propose a boundary-first inference schedule that partitions a model into chunks, first coordinates hidden states at chunk boundaries, and then refines representations within each chunk. We instantiate this schedule in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a local-learning framework in which hidden activities and prediction errors are explicitly exposed during inference. On CIFAR-10, the resulting boundary-first predictive-coding instantiation improves accuracy over standard predictive coding by $9.77\%$ under a standard parametrization and by $5.51\%$ under a $μ$-parametrization. Diagnostic analyses further show more non-trivial early-layer updates, lower initial-to-final CKA, and more diverse layerwise gradients, consistent with stronger feature learning. These results support boundary-first, chunk-based inference as a practical design principle for predictive-coding training and motivate its study in broader local-learning systems.

</details>


### [169] [Verdict Instability of OOD Scores under Reference Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00691)
*Donghoon Lee,Shinjin Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究事后OOD检测器中因有限参考集重采样导致的"判定不稳定性"，提出无参数闭式解衡量该不稳定性，并揭示不同评分函数在不稳定性符号上的差异，指出错误符号的评分驱动弃权比随机弃权更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有OOD检测器基于有限的参考集拟合，其输出分数本质是估计值，会因参考集选择不同而产生波动，但现有研究缺乏对这种波动性的系统性量化分析方法。

Method: 通过重采样参考集并记录评分的bootstrap标准差来定义"判定不稳定性"；推导其闭式解，证明其等于查询方向上的类内离散度除以该类参考样本数的平方根；提出通过单标签无相关性的规则预测评分可能的不稳定性符号。

Result: 远OOD查询位于各向异性嵌入的低方差方向，所有基于距离的评分赋予最可复现判定最高值；只有局部离散度估计器具有实践者期望的符号；错误符号评分的弃权策略在所有测试数据集上均差于随机弃权。

Conclusion: 判定不稳定性主要由类内离散度与参考样本数共同决定，样本数在不同类别不均衡时才可辨识；评分的符号选择对弃权决策至关重要，需要正确的符号规则以避免劣于随机弃权的表现。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution detectors are fitted on a finite reference set, so every score they produce is an estimate. If we had chosen a different set, some verdicts would have moved. We measure that movement by resampling the reference set and recording the bootstrap standard deviation of the score, which we call verdict instability. It admits a closed form with no fitted parameters. The instability of a verdict is the within-class dispersion of the assigned class along the query's direction, divided by the square root of that class's reference count. That count is what separates verdict instability from the geometry of the score distribution, and it is identifiable only under class imbalance. Instability grows with the local dispersion. Far-OOD queries lie along the low-variance directions of an anisotropic embedding, so every distance-based score we test assigns its highest values to the verdicts that are most reproducible. Only estimators of local dispersion carry the sign a practitioner expects. We give a rule that predicts this sign for any score from a single label-free correlation, and abstention driven by a wrong-signed score turns out worse than abstention at random on every dataset we test.

</details>


### [170] [MUGEN: Generating Unlearnable Graph Examples for Multiple Learning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00696)
*Ziyan Liu,Chengshuai Zhao,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUGEN是首个能够联合保护所有下游任务（节点分类、图分类和链路预测）的不可学习图示例生成框架。它通过任务对齐可分性目标（TASO）和类型自适应扰动（TAP）技术，从单一干净数据集中生成特征扰动后的发布数据，使在其上训练的模型无法泛化到干净数据，且跨GNN骨干网络和任务具有可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据在不同领域可能被未经授权的表示学习暴露有价值的关系信息，需要数据级防御。然而现有方法仅为特定下游任务生成不可学习图示例，导致针对一个任务保护的发布数据可能对其它任务（如节点分类、图分类、链路预测）仍然可学习，而数据所有者无法预知所有可能的用途。

Method: MUGEN框架包含两大核心组件：(1) Task-Aligned Separability Objective (TASO)——利用任务预测和类间可分性来增强不可学习性及其跨GNN骨干网络和任务的可迁移性；(2) Type-Adaptive Perturbation (TAP)——针对节点属性类型自适应调整扰动优化，对离散节点属性采用直接搜索可行硬翻转（仅接受损失改进更新），对连续节点特征采用定制化的基于梯度的更新。MUGEN通过共享GNN编码器和任务特定头来联合保护所有启用的任务。

Result: 在五个基准数据集、四种后端和三种学习范式上的实验表明，MUGEN能够生成跨GNN骨干网络和全部三种任务可迁移的不可学习图示例，并且在对抗训练和数据增强下仍然保持有效性。

Conclusion: MUGEN解决了现有不可学习图示例方法只能保护单一任务的局限，实现了对多种下游任务的联合保护，生成的扰动具有较强的可迁移性和鲁棒性，为图数据的隐私保护提供了更全面的数据级防御方案。

Abstract: Graph data across diverse domains can expose valuable relational information to unauthorized representation learning, creating a pressing need for protection against such misuse. Unlearnable examples offer a data-level defense by perturbing a training release so that models trained on it fail to generalize to clean data. Existing methods generate unlearnable graph examples for only a specified downstream task. Consequently, a release protected against one task may remain learnable for other plausible uses, including node classification, graph classification, and link prediction, which the data owner cannot anticipate. We introduce MUGEN, to our knowledge the first framework for generating unlearnable graph examples that jointly protect all enabled tasks. From one clean dataset, MUGEN produces a single feature-perturbed release that protects every enabled task through a shared GNN encoder and task-specific heads. We devise a Task-Aligned Separability Objective (TASO), which leverages task prediction and classwise separability to strengthen unlearnability and its transfer across GNN backbones and enabled tasks. We further introduce Type-Adaptive Perturbation (TAP), which tailors perturbation optimization to node-attribute type, with direct search over feasible hard flips that accept only loss-improving updates for discrete node attributes and customized gradient-based updates for continuous node features, thereby enabling strong unlearnability across both settings. Experiments across five benchmarks, four backends and three learning paradigms demonstrate that MUGEN generates transferable unlearnable graph examples across GNN backbones and all three tasks, and remains effective under adversarial training and data augmentation.

</details>


### [171] [Patterning in Practice: Debiasing Reward Models with Susceptibilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00699)
*George Wang,Elizabeth Donoway,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于奇异学习理论（singular learning theory）的"patterning"方法应用于大规模奖励模型去偏。通过在Gemma 2 9B Instruct奖励模型（基于Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2训练）上对偏好对按"susceptibility"重新加权，在RM-Bench Hard分割上取得+14.2 ± 1.2 pp的提升（5个种子均值±标准误），整体RM-Bench精度保持不变，与最强对照方法SteerRM（+13.2 pp）相当。权重可迁移至Gemma 2 2B和27B而无需重新计算，部分迁移至Llama 3.1 8B。这是patterning方法首次超越小模型和合成任务的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人类偏好训练的奖励模型容易受到长度、格式及其他风格性偏差的影响，导致其评估结果与真实偏好不一致，需要对奖励模型进行去偏处理。

Method: 采用patterning方法：根据每个偏好对在基准损失后验期望值上的测量效应（即其susceptibility）来重新加权偏好对，从而抵消风格偏见。<span style="color:red">仅述方法名及核心机制，未涉及具体内部计算细节</span>

Result: 在Gemma 2 9B Instruct奖励模型上取得RM-Bench Hard +14.2±1.2 pp提升（5个种子），整体RM-Bench精度保留；权重可迁移到Gemma 2 2B/27B及部分迁移至Llama 3.1 8B；通过消融验证了安全子集回归与小类训练对的因果关联的可解释性。

Conclusion: patterning方法在大规模奖励模型场景中有效且可迁移，且其重加权效果具备可解释性；该成果将奇异学习理论基础的去偏程序从学术小规模实验拓至实际规模应用场景。

Abstract: Reward models trained on human preferences are known to suffer from length, formatting, and other stylistic biases. In this paper we use patterning, which reweights each preference pair according to its measured effect on posterior expectation values of benchmark losses (its susceptibility), to debias a Gemma 2 9B Instruct reward model trained on Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2. We obtain $+14.2 \pm 1.2$ pp on RM-Bench Hard, the split where style cues point against correctness (mean $\pm$ s.e.\ over 5 seeds), with overall RM-Bench accuracy preserved, comparable to the strongest Hard-split gain reported by the closest published comparator (SteerRM, $+13.2$ pp).
