We demonstrate in a simple case that the reweighting is interpretable by tracing a side effect of the intervention (a regression on a safety subset of RM-Bench) to a small class of training pairs, which we confirm by ablation. The weights also transfer: those computed on Gemma 2 9B debias Gemma 2 2B and 27B with no recomputation, and transfer partially to Llama 3.1 8B. This is the first application of patterning, a program grounded in singular learning theory, beyond small models and synthetic tasks.

</details>


### [172] [Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00734)
*Haiquan Wen,Yiwei He,Bei Peng,Guangliang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线自加权微调方法（OSW-FT），通过在标准监督微调（SFT）基础上引入在线轨迹级权重，利用少量推理rollout估计模型当前成功率并自适应调整损失权重，在无需大量采样的前提下提升小到中型LLM在可验证推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT对所有专家演示施加相同的显式损失权重，忽视了模型在不同训练查询上的能力变化；而基于RL的方法虽能自适应调整更新强度，但需要大量采样且训练不稳定。作者希望找到一种简单、计算高效且稳定的替代方案。

Method: OSW-FT在SFT基础上引入在线轨迹级加权：对每个查询，用少量纯推理rollout（仅2个）估计模型当前成功率，据此重新缩放标准SFT损失。优化方向锚定在专家轨迹上，更新幅度在线自适应调整。在梯度层面连接了SFT与RL，基于方差缩减原理得到无偏估计器，并分析了收敛性。

Result: 在Qwen3系列（0.6B至4B）多个具有挑战性的基准（如AIME）上，OSW-FT一致优于标准SFT，仅需2个在线rollout即可获得有竞争力的性能与计算开销权衡。

Conclusion: OSW-FT作为一种实用的微调方法，为小到中型LLM在二元可验证推理任务上提供了良好的计算-性能权衡，相比SFT有稳定提升，且相比RL方法大幅降低了采样需求。

Abstract: Standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) assigns the same explicit loss weight to every expert demonstration, regardless of the model's changing competence over training queries. Reinforcement learning (RL) based methods adapt update strength using model-generated rollouts, but often require substantially more sampling and can be unstable on hard tasks. We propose \textbf{Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning (OSW-FT)}, a simple method that augments SFT with online, trajectory-level weighting. For each query, OSW-FT estimates the model's current success rate using a small number of inference-only rollouts and rescales the standard SFT loss accordingly. The optimization direction remains anchored to the expert trajectory, while the update magnitude adapts online. For binary-verifiable reasoning, we connect this weighting to SFT and RL at the gradient level, inspired by variance-reduction principles. The resulting estimator is unbiased for the exact OSW-FT surrogate update for any finite rollout count, and we analyze convergence with respect to the corresponding surrogate objective. Evaluated across Qwen3 series ranging from 0.6B to 4B on multiple challenging benchmarks (e.g., AIME), OSW-FT consistently improves over SFT on small-to-medium scale models. OSW-FT offers a favorable compute-performance trade-off as a practical approach for fine-tuning small-to-medium LLMs on binary-verifiable reasoning tasks with only \textbf{2 online rollouts}.

</details>


### [173] [Text Capability Loss in Vision-Language Adaptation: An Attention-Sink Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00746)
*Minsik Choi,Geewook Kim,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现将预训练LLM微调为视觉语言模型（VLM）会侵蚀骨干模型的文本能力，尤其是严格遵循输出格式的任务。作者将这一退化归因于"注意力汇损坏"（attention-sink corruption），并提出Sink Strength——一种在基础LLM上仅需几秒即可计算的标量，无需VL训练即可预测VL适应后的退化程度。同时报告了QK-RMSNorm注入和权重合并等方法无法恢复能力的负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM微调会损害LLM骨干的文本能力，特别是在需要精确输出规则的任务（如指令遵循、严格解析的CoT推理）上退化严重。作者希望理解并预测这种退化，以便在VL训练前筛选骨干模型。

Method: 1) 将退化归因于注意力汇损坏：VL微调扰动了锚定大量注意力概率的早期汇位置；2) 提出Sink Strength，一个在基础LLM上计算的单一标量，可在单GPU上数秒内完成，用于预测后VL退化；3) 测试了跨6个VLM-LLM对和多个格式敏感任务的退化跟踪；4) 探索了QK-RMSNorm注入和权重合并等干预措施。

Result: Sink Strength能够一致地跟踪6个VLM-LLM对和多个格式敏感任务中的相对退化程度。负面结果方面：后训练QK-RMSNorm注入无法复现原生QK-RMSNorm的保护效果，多种现成的权重合并设置也无法恢复VL训练后丢失的能力。

Conclusion: Sink Strength可作为VL训练前的骨干筛选工具；负面结果收窄了干预空间，表明需要向头部选择性（head-selective）训练时保护的方向探索。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a pretrained LLM into a vision-language model (VLM) can erode the backbone's text capability, with the damage concentrated on tasks that require following exact output rules, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning graded on a strictly parsed final answer, and similar evaluations with strict graders. We trace this gap to attention-sink corruption: VL fine-tuning perturbs the early sink position that anchors a large fraction of attention probability, and how well the base LLM preserves its sink tracks how much of the affected capability survives adaptation. Building on this view, we introduce Sink Strength, a single scalar computed on the base LLM in a few seconds on a single GPU that predicts post-VL degradation without any VL training. It consistently tracks relative degradation across the six VLM-LLM pairs and multiple format-sensitive tasks. Complementing this diagnostic, we find that post-pretraining QK-RMSNorm injection fails to reproduce the protection of native QK-RMSNorm, while several off-the-shelf weight-merging settings fail to recover the lost capability after VL training. These negative results underscore the value of screening backbones with Sink Strength before VL training and narrow the intervention space toward head-selective training-time protection.

</details>


### [174] [How Do Language Models Choose Between Context and Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00753)
*Benjamin Shih,John Winnicki,Arianna Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过反事实实验研究模型如何在上下文信息与参数知识冲突时选择信息来源，发现操控"权威方向"（authority directions）能部分复现模型的来源选择行为，但这种方向仅在任务内具有因果效力，跨任务迁移效果很差（仅9%），表明权威计算可能是任务依赖的而非跨任务可复用的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用激活方向来解读和操控模型在上下文与参数知识冲突时的来源选择，但缺乏对因果性的严格验证——即未编辑模型是否天然使用该方向，以及该方向是否能跨任务复用。

Method: 从一致性提示（上下文与参数知识支持相同答案）中估计权威方向；在匹配的提示对之间交换这些方向的自然坐标，这些提示分别引导模型优先上下文或参数知识。在Qwen、Llama和OLMo模型上分别进行任务内干预和跨任务迁移实验。

Result: 任务内干预可复现30-68%的权威诱导来源选择偏移，匹配对照组几乎无效果；跨任务迁移仅覆盖约9%的权威差距，而任务内局部方向可覆盖57%。

Conclusion: 研究区分了权威表示、因果使用和跨任务因果复用三个层次，表明模型中关于信息来源权威的计算可能是任务特定的，而非在不同任务间通用的可复用表征。

Abstract: When contextual information conflicts with the knowledge stored in model parameters, activation directions can be used to decode and steer which source the model follows. However, steering along a direction does not establish causality: whether the unedited model would naturally use that direction or whether the direction is reusable across tasks. We test these distinctions through counterfactual experiments in unambiguous settings. First, we estimate authority directions from agreement prompts, in which the context and parametric knowledge support the same answer. We then interchange naturally occurring coordinates along these directions between matched prompts that direct the model to prioritize either the supplied context or its parametric knowledge. Across Qwen, Llama, and OLMo models, this intervention reproduces 30-68% of the authority-induced shift in source choice, whereas matched controls reproduce almost none. To test cross-task reuse, we learn authority directions on two tasks separately and see that cross-task transferability closes only 9% of the authority gap while the local direction learned on the given task closes 57%. These results distinguish authority representation, causal use, and cross-task causal reuse, and suggest that authority computations may be task-dependent, rather than reusable across tasks.

</details>


### [175] [Frozen Cores Need Task Signal: Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00762)
*Wentao Ye,Zhanming Shen,Zhiqing Xiao,Yao Ding,Haobo Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FCCA方法，通过冻结核心（frozen-core）适配，仅优化r×r的核心矩阵而非全部低秩因子，在固定子空间内实现参数高效微调。实验表明在11个任务上，FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，比次优方法高2.3分，并以仅36.9K可训练参数接近LoRA和DoRA的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调通常只关注更新多少参数，但在严格的可训练状态预算下，系数作用于哪些子空间同样重要。现有方法中可训练因子可能修复较差的初始子空间，掩盖了子空间质量的影响，因此需要一种能直接观察和评估子空间质量的方法。

Method: FCCA通过冻结核心适配实现：首先进行校准过程固定每个权重矩阵的左右基（bases），仅微调优化r×r核心。FCCA估计带符号的输入-误差交叉协方差，用对角Fisher矩进行白化，在局部度量下截断，将选定方向映射回原空间，并施加薄QR分解以获得稳定的核心坐标。

Result: 在匹配的r²预算下，比较了8种基构造函数、11个任务、4种模型设置和3个种子。FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，比次优方法高2.3分，在全部11个任务上超过未白化的RawGrad基线。消融实验显示白化带来2.7-17.2分的提升，QR分解对稳定核心优化是必要的。FCCA与LoRA和DoRA的性能差距分别在0.32和0.23平均分以内，但仅优化36.9K参数而非约740万参数。

Conclusion: 精心选择的固定子空间可以恢复可移动低秩因子的大部分收益，同时大幅降低可训练参数和优化器状态开销，证明了子空间选择质量在参数高效微调中的关键作用。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is usually framed as a question of how many parameters to update. Under a severe trainable-state budget, however, where those coefficients act is equally consequential. We study this choice through frozen-core adaptation: a calibration pass fixes left and right bases for each weight matrix, and fine-tuning optimizes only an $r\times r$ core. This removes the ability of trainable factors to repair a poor initial span and makes subspace quality directly observable. We introduce FCCA, which estimates the signed input--error cross-covariance, whitens it with diagonal Fisher moments, truncates it in the resulting local metric, maps the selected directions back, and applies thin QR to obtain stable core coordinates. Under a matched $r^2$ budget, we compare eight basis constructors on 11 tasks, four model settings, and three seeds. On Qwen2.5-3B, FCCA reaches an 83.0 macro-average, 2.3 points above the next-best matched-budget constructor, and exceeds its unwhitened RawGrad control on all 11 tasks. It ranks first at all three Qwen scales and finishes within 0.13 points of the best method on Llama-3.2-1B. Controlled ablations show gains of 2.7--17.2 points from whitening and identify QR as necessary for stable core optimization in the tested regime. Finally, FCCA comes within 0.32 and 0.23 average points of LoRA and DoRA while optimizing 36.9K rather than roughly 7.4M parameters. These results show that a carefully selected fixed span can recover most of the benefit of movable low-rank factors at a much smaller trainable and optimizer-state cost.

</details>


### [176] [Are You Thinking What I am Thinking? : Examining Conceptual Separation in Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00764)
*Jaee Ponde,Roshni Agarwal,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过几何和分布分析方法，探究CNN和LLM内部激活是否表现出"概念分离"——即同类示例是否形成连贯表征、相关概念是否在表征空间中更接近。结果显示CNN中熟悉的ImageNet概念形成连贯且有语义顺序的表征，但在未见概念和类内域偏移下连贯性减弱；LLM中清晰领域分离良好、相关子域更接近，而模糊主题的区分在均值和协方差层面均崩溃。概念分离可作为输出准确率之外揭示模型内部表征结构的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络评估主要依赖输出层面的指标（如准确率），但这些指标无法揭示概念在模型内部是如何被表征的。论文旨在探究模型内部是否具备概念组织性结构，从而弥补仅靠输出准确率无法洞察内部表征的不足。

Method: 通过对CNN和LLM的内部激活进行几何和分布分析，检验概念的"分离度"：同类概念示例是否形成连贯的表征、相关概念在表征空间中的距离是否更近。分析涵盖熟悉的ImageNet概念、未见概念、类内域偏移场景，以及LLM中的清晰领域、相关子领域和模糊主题。

Result: CNN中，熟悉的ImageNet概念形成连贯且语义有序的表征，但这一连贯性在未见概念上减弱，并受类内域偏移影响。LLM中，清晰区分的域保持良好分离，相关子域在空间中更接近，而模糊主题之间的区分在均值和协方差两个层面都发生崩溃。

Conclusion: 概念分离可以揭示输出准确率无法单独展示的内部结构信息，可作为评估模型如何稳健地表征其被要求识别概念的有用诊断指标。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly employed to identify both well-defined and ambiguous concepts, yet output-level metrics reveal little about how those concepts are represented internally. Our study asks if these networks exhibit \textit{conceptual separation}: if examples of the same concept form coherent representations, and whether related concepts lie closer together in the representation space. We examine this conceptual organisation in Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) through geometric and distributional analysis of their internal activations. In CNNs, familiar ImageNet concepts form coherent and semantically ordered representations, while this coherence weakens for unseen concepts and suffers within-class domain shift. In LLMs, clearly distinct domains remain well separated, related subdomains move closer together, and the distinction between ambiguous topics collapses at both the mean and covariance level. These results suggest that conceptual separation can reveal structure that output accuracy alone cannot, and may serve as a useful diagnostic of how robustly a model represents the concepts it is asked to identify. Code and data available on \href{https://github.com/JaeeRoshniCapstoneProject/Are-You-Thinking-What-I-m-Thinking-Examining-Conceptual-Separation-in-Neural-Architectures}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [177] [Subspace Levenberg Marquardt Algorithms in Training Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00789)
*M. Duc Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了子空间Levenberg-Marquardt（LM）算法在神经网络回归和分类任务中的性能，并与经典LM方法以及SGD、Adam等一阶算法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LM算法虽然收敛快、鲁棒性强，但随着神经网络参数数量增加，其计算和内存成本显著上升，因此需要探索更高效的子空间LM方法来弥补这一局限。

Method: 对子空间LM算法（如KSLM和HSLM）在回归和分类任务中进行系统评估，并与经典LM方法以及SGD、Adam等一阶优化算法进行性能对比。

Result: 通过实验比较了子空间LM变体与经典LM算法及SGD、Adam等算法的性能表现，评估其在神经网络训练中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为子空间LM算法在神经网络训练中的应用提供了全面的性能评估，有助于在保持二阶算法优势的同时降低计算和内存开销。

Abstract: The Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) algorithm is a well-known second-order method for rapid convergence and strong robustness when training small- to medium-sized neural networks (NNs). However, its computational and memory costs increase significantly as the number of parameters in an NN grows. To address this limitation, subspace methods have been proposed, such as the Krylov subspace LM (KSLM) and the hybrid subspace LM (HSLM), making second-order algorithms more efficient. In this work, we evaluate the subspace Levenberg-Marquardt algorithms for regression and classification tasks in neural networks. We compare the performance of subspace LM variants with the classical LM method, as well as other popular first-order algorithms, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and Adam.

</details>


### [178] [HarnessEvolve: Learning from Reference Trajectories for Reliable Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00829)
*Wen Jiang,Mingmin Chu,Yimeng Tian,Qianxin Zhang,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Yang Liu,Tao Lv,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessEvolve，一个通过参考轨迹学习实现可靠智能体自进化的框架，将执行与进化管线解耦，通过质量门和性能门防止捷径学习与灾难性遗忘，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体在优化其工具链（提示词、技能、工具和执行逻辑）时面临三大挑战：信用分配失败（难以定位出错步骤）、捷径学习（记忆任务特定模式而非获得泛化能力）以及灾难性遗忘（更新损害已有能力）。这些挑战阻碍了智能体向可靠自主进化的方向发展。

Method: HarnessEvolve将执行智能体与进化管线解耦，分配执行、评估、优化和门控给独立模块。它通过生成参考轨迹（在给定正确答案时产生的执行路径）并将失败执行与其对齐来提取错误信号，聚类以揭示系统性失败模式。候选更新须通过质量门（过滤数据泄漏和提示膨胀）和性能门（要求当前批次有改进且不退化近期批次），并在保留集上进行epoch-end验证选择最佳快照。

Result: 在涵盖开放域和企业场景的多个基准上，使用不同模型和智能体框架进行广泛实验，HarnessEvolve在所有基准和设置中一致优于最先进的基线方法，确认了跨任务域的可靠性。

Conclusion: HarnessEvolve通过参考轨迹学习和双重门控机制，有效解决了自进化智能体的信用分配、捷径学习和灾难性遗忘三大问题，实现了可靠且泛化的智能体自进化。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents advance toward autonomy by optimizing their harness---prompts, skills, tools, and execution logic---based on environmental feedback. This paradigm, however, is hampered by three challenges: \textit{credit assignment failure}, where terminal success/failure feedback makes it ambiguous which step caused the error; \textit{shortcut learning}, where agents memorize task-specific patterns rather than acquire generalizable capabilities; and \textit{catastrophic forgetting}, where unguarded updates degrade previously acquired competence. In this paper, we introduce HarnessEvolve, a self-evolving framework that learns from reference trajectories to achieve reliable agent self-evolution. HarnessEvolve decouples the execution agent from the evolutionary pipeline, assigning execution, evaluation, optimization, and gating to independent agent modules, enabling generalizable and stable harness improvements. Specifically, HarnessEvolve overcomes credit assignment failure by generating reference trajectories (execution paths produced when given the ground-truth answers) and aligning failed executions against them to extract error signals, which are clustered to reveal systematic failure patterns. To prevent shortcut learning and catastrophic forgetting, candidate harness updates must pass two gates: a quality gate that filters data leakage and prompt bloat, and a performance gate that accepts each update if it improves on the current batch without degrading recent batches, with epoch-end validation on a held-out set selecting the best-performing accepted agent snapshot. We conduct extensive experiments on several benchmarks spanning open-domain and enterprise scenarios, using different models and agent frameworks. Results demonstrate that HarnessEvolve consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all benchmarks and settings, confirming reliability across task domains.

</details>


### [179] [Conditional Flow Matching for ML-Based Inverse Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00863)
*Juliana Felder,Milad Habibi,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将条件流匹配（CFM）方法引入工程逆向设计优化框架EngiOpt，并在结构（beams2d）和热传导（heatconduction2d）两个基准任务上与条件扩散模型和条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）进行比较。实验结果表明，CFM在累积最优性差距（COG）、最终最优性差距（FOG）、最大均值差异（MMD）和体积分数偏差等指标上均达到最优，且推理速度远快于扩散模型（约66倍吞吐量），同时网络评估次数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计常受限于偏微分方程约束的迭代求解器高昂的计算成本以及对初始化的敏感性。深度生成模型可以在推理阶段无需重跑模拟器即可生成候选设计，但现有方法存在不足：GAN虽然单次前向采样，但扩散模型需要迭代式反向积分，计算开销大。因此需要探索更高效的生成模型来为基于梯度的优化提供更好的初始解（warm start）。

Method: 将条件流匹配（CFM）方法集成到EngiOpt框架中，与条件扩散模型和cGAN在同一下游优化协议下进行比较。使用COG和FOG作为评估生成设计作为梯度优化warm start的主要指标，并在EngiBench的两个基准任务（beams2d结构和heatconduction2d热传导）上进行实验评估。

Result: 在EngiOpt实现和两个EngiBench任务上，CFM在COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差指标上均取得最低值。CFM在beams2d和heatconduction2d上的平均体积分数偏差分别为0.4%和1.0%，显著优于扩散模型的3.8%和11.2%。在Euler s=16配置下，CFM在beams2d上达到53.2 samples/s的吞吐量，约为扩散基线（1000次网络评估）的66倍，且COG为1.182±3.126，与Euler s=32时的1.173±3.100相近。

Conclusion: 跨两个任务，CFM生成的warm start相比两个基线方法具有更低的COG，且相比扩散模型使用更少的网络评估次数，表明CFM在工程逆向设计优化中是一种更具计算效率和优化性能的生成式方法。

Abstract: Engineering inverse design is often limited by the high computational cost of iterative solvers for optimization problems constrained by partial differential equations (PDEs) and by their sensitivity to initialization. Deep generative models can produce candidate designs without rerunning the simulator at inference time. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) sample in one forward pass, whereas diffusion models require iterative reverse-time integration.
  In this work, we add conditional flow matching (CFM) to EngiOpt and compare it with a conditional diffusion model and a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) on structural (beams2d) and thermal (heatconduction2d) benchmarks from EngiBench using the same downstream optimization protocol. We use cumulative optimality gap (COG) and final optimality gap (FOG) as the primary metrics for evaluating the generated designs as warm starts for gradient-based refinement. On the evaluated EngiOpt implementations and two EngiBench tasks, CFM achieves the lowest measured COG, FOG, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and volume-fraction deviation on both tasks. CFM has mean volume-fraction deviations of 0.4% and 1.0% on beams2d and heatconduction2d, respectively, compared with 3.8% and 11.2% for diffusion. At Euler s = 16, CFM achieves 53.2 samples/s on beams2d, about 66 times the measured throughput of the evaluated diffusion baseline using 1000 network evaluations under the same timing protocol, with COG 1.182 +/- 3.126, compared with 1.173 +/- 3.100 for Euler s = 32.
  Across the two tasks, CFM produces warm starts with lower measured COG than both baselines and uses fewer network evaluations than diffusion.

</details>


### [180] [MemoryWalker: Stop Training Agents on Contexts They Never Saw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00865)
*Zinco J,Xunjie Zhu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Pengjun Xie,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对智能体生产框架（如Claude Code、Qwen-Agent）在压缩上下文下的训练条件化问题，提出LogitTree和4D注意力掩码两种精确梯度等价修正方法，以及一种单反向传播变分松弛方法SDCC，在七个web搜索基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的智能体框架在rollout期间压缩上下文，但压缩训练导致条件化问题：每次驱逐都会使有效历史产生分支，学习目标从序列变为树。已有的线性化方法要么保留最右路径（导致时间穿越泄漏），要么回放深度优先遍历（导致训练-推理不匹配）。

Method: 提出三种方法：(1) LogitTree——分段K前向遍历，需要K+1次反向传播；(2) 4D注意力掩码——需要自定义内核和透明驱逐记录；(3) SDCC——单反向传播变分松弛，在每次驱逐时最小化压缩学生与停止梯度教师在重建驱逐前前缀上的前向KL散度。

Result: 在七个web搜索基准（TC-RAG、AgentFold、MemexRL、Claude Code、OpenCode）上，朴素训练显著放大训练-rollout对数概率差距（尤其在驱逐密集批次上）；精确方法保持在无压缩基线水平；SDCC大幅缩小差距，表现为更低的logit漂移和更高的rollout奖励。

Conclusion: SDCC提供了剩余每节点KL epsilon_KL的O(√epsilon_KL)训练-部署总变差差距上界，且适用于黑盒框架，是精确方法之外实用且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Production agent harnesses such as Claude Code and Qwen-Agent compress context during rollout, but training under compression creates a conditioning problem: every eviction branches the effective history, so the learning object is a tree rather than a sequence. Existing linearizations either retain the rightmost path, causing time-travel leakage, or replay a depth-first traversal, causing train-inference mismatch. We introduce two exact, gradient-equivalent corrections: LogitTree, a segmented K-forward traversal, and a packed 4D attention mask. LogitTree requires K+1 backward passes; the 4D mask requires a custom kernel and white-box eviction records. We also propose SDCC (Self-Distillation for Conditioning Consistency), a single-backward-pass variational relaxation. At each eviction, it minimizes forward KL between the compressed student and a stop-gradient teacher on the reconstructed pre-eviction prefix. A residual per-junction KL of epsilon_KL gives an O(sqrt(epsilon_KL)) bound on the train-deployment total-variation gap. SDCC also applies to black-box harnesses. On seven web-search benchmarks with TC-RAG, AgentFold, MemexRL, Claude Code, and OpenCode, naive training inflates the train-rollout log-probability gap, especially on eviction-heavy batches. The exact methods stay at the no-compression floor, and SDCC substantially closes the gap, with lower logit drift and higher rollout rewards.

</details>


### [181] [iPINN for Broadband CARS Phase Retrieval: A Framework for Function Approximation and Inverse Modeling Problems in Nonlinear Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00883)
*Ravi Teja Vulchi,Carl Messerschmidt,Mohammadsadegh Vafaeinezhad,Rajendhar Junjuri,Tobias Meyer-Zedler,Juergen Popp,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆物理信息神经网络(iPINN)，用于宽带相干反斯托克斯拉曼光谱(BCARS)中的相位恢复问题。iPINN通过变压器编码器将光谱特征映射到24个可学习的洛伦兹峰参数槽位，并利用可微分的解析前向模型重建共振磁化率，同时引入多视角一致性损失确保对不同采集条件的鲁棒性。在公开基准上达到最低误差(MAE 0.016)，并在跨溶剂和焦平面的零样本测试中表现出深度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCARS中的相位恢复是一个病态逆问题。拉曼信号编码在共振磁化率的虚部中，但与非共振背景(NRB)相干混合，且NRB随采集条件变化而变化，导致传统直接光谱回归方法在不同采集条件下的精度下降。

Method: 提出iPINN框架：1) 变压器编码器将原始BCARS光谱特征分配给24个可学习的洛伦兹峰参数槽位；2) 通过可微分的解析前向物理模型重建共振磁化率；3) 引入多视角一致性损失，强制模型在不同NRB模式、NRB强度和噪声水平下保持预测不变性。

Result: 在公开基准上，iPINN达到最低误差MAE 0.016，远优于次优基线0.046。在28个零样本光谱测试(涵盖7种溶剂和4个焦平面位置)中，7种溶剂中有5种实现了深度不变的精度。

Conclusion: 逆参数预测与可微分物理解码器的结合，比直接光谱回归方法具有更好的跨测量条件的鲁棒性，为BCARS的稳健相位恢复提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Phase retrieval in broadband coherent anti-Stokes Raman spectroscopy (BCARS) is an ill-posed inverse problem. The Raman-like signal is encoded in the imaginary part of the resonant susceptibility, which mixes coherently with a non-resonant background (NRB) that varies across acquisitions. We introduce an inverse physics-informed neural network (iPINN) that predicts Lorentzian peak parameters from raw BCARS spectra and reconstructs the resonant susceptibility through a differentiable analytical forward model. A transformer encoder assigns spectral features to 24 learnable peak slots, and a multi-view consistency loss enforces invariance across NRB pattern, NRB strength, and noise. Unlike direct spectral regression approaches, the method retains accuracy under varying acquisition conditions. On a public benchmark, iPINN achieves the lowest error among the tested baselines (MAE 0.016 vs. next-best 0.046). On 28 zero-shot test spectra acquired across seven solvents and four focal positions, accuracy is depth-invariant in five of seven solvents. These results show that inverse parametric prediction with a differentiable physical decoder supports robust phase retrieval across measurement conditions.

</details>


### [182] [Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems with Time-varying Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00896)
*Jiahao Wang,Yijun Wang,Nan Fang,Sikun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有时变转移核的Poisson-Gamma动态系统（TV-PGDS），通过三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链来捕捉计数时间序列中随时间演化的转移动态，并利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增强技术设计了全共轭的高效Gibbs采样器，在预测性能上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PGDS模型在捕捉真实世界计数时间序列中常见的时变转移动态方面存在不足，其转移核假设是固定的或静态的，无法适应底层依赖结构的异质性突变。

Method: 提出TV-PGDS模型，允许底层转移矩阵随时间演化；构造三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir、Dir-Gam-Dir、PR-Gam-Dir）来容纳依赖关系中的异质结构突变；利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增强技术开发全共轭的高效Gibbs采样器进行后验推断。

Result: 与相关模型相比，所提出的PGDS由于能够学习由时变转移矩阵捕捉的随时间演化的依赖结构，取得了更好的预测性能。

Conclusion: TV-PGDS通过引入时变转移核有效弥补了传统PGDS在捕捉真实计数时间序列动态转移方面的不足，提升了模型的表达力和预测能力。

Abstract: Bayesian methodologies for handling count-valued time series have gained prominence due to their ability to infer interpretable latent structures and to estimate uncertainties. Among these Bayesian models, Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems (PGDSs) are proven to be effective in capturing the evolving dynamics underlying observed count sequences. However, the state-of-the-art PGDS still falls short in capturing the transition dynamics that are commonly observed in real-world count time series. To mitigate this limitation, a PGDS with time-varying transition kernel (TV-PGDS), is proposed to allow the underlying transition matrices to evolve over time. Three specifically-designed Dirichlet Markov chains (Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir) are constructed to accommodate heterogeneous structural mutations within these dependencies. Leveraging Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta data augmentation techniques, a fully-conjugate and efficient Gibbs sampler is developed to perform posterior simulation. Experiments show that, in comparison with related models, the proposed PGDS achieves improved predictive performance due to its capacity to learn time-varying dependency structure captured by the time-evolving transition matrices.

</details>


### [183] [When Metropolis and Hastings Meet Bradley and Terry: Exact MCMC From Preference Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00905)
*Ariel Smogorghevski,Nir Rosenfeld,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pref-MH是一种仅利用随机二元成对比较（人类或模型判断器）进行精确条件采样的Metropolis-Hastings采样器，其关键洞察是MH的未归一化密度比与Bradley-Terry选择模型中的偏好赔率相匹配，并提出了有效的接受/拒绝规则，使马尔可夫链可证明收敛到目标分布，且在Peskun-Tierney意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从满足特定语义属性的分布中进行条件采样是现代生成建模中的新兴挑战。虽然Metropolis-Hastings提供了原则性的条件采样路径，但它需要精确的点对点目标密度评估，这在生成设置中通常不可用。相比之下，人工或模型"裁判"进行的二元成对比较易于获取且已被证明在多种应用中具有价值，因此希望利用这些比较来实现条件采样。

Method: 提出Pref-MH采样器。核心观察是MH的未归一化密度比恰好对应于Bradley-Terry选择模型中的偏好赔率。由于MH需要精确的比率计算而BT裁判只提供采样的二元反馈这一矛盾，文章开发了一个有效的接受/拒绝规则，使得生成的马尔可夫链能够收敛到目标分布，并证明了在给定提议核和预算下，该方法在该类精确可逆接受规则中具有Peskun-Tierney最优性。

Result: 在文本生成和分子设计（使用LLM裁判）以及图像生成（使用VLM裁判）上进行了实验，结果表明Pref-MH提供了一种实用且灵活的条件采样方法，特别是在比较反馈相对容易获取的场景中表现良好。

Conclusion: Pref-MH证明仅凭比较反馈就可以实现精确的条件采样，且具有理论上的最优性保证。这为在生成模型中当精确密度不可得但比较反馈容易获取时提供了一种有效的条件采样新范式。

Abstract: Sampling from distributions conditioned on desired semantic properties is an emerging challenge in modern generative modeling. Metropolis-Hastings (MH) provides a principled route to conditional sampling, but requires access to exact pointwise target-density evaluations, which are not available in generative settings. Meanwhile, pairwise comparisons by humans or model "judge" are highly accessible and have proved valuable across diverse applications. We introduce Pref-MH, a general exact MH sampler for judge-induced conditional distributions using only stochastic binary pairwise comparisons. Our key observation is that the MH unnormalized density ratio matches the preference odds of the Bradley-Terry (BT) choice model. The central challenge is that while MH requires precise ratio computation, BT judges provide only sampled binary feedback. To this end, we develop a valid accept/reject rule whose resulting Markov chain provably converges to the target distribution. We further show that, for a fixed proposal kernel and budget, Pref-MH is optimal in the Peskun-Tierney sense among this class of exact reversible acceptance rules. Experiments on text generation and molecular design with LLM judges, as well as image generation with VLM judges, demonstrate that Pref-MH provides a practical and flexible approach to conditional sampling when comparative feedback is relatively easy to obtain.

</details>


### [184] [The Multiple Timescales of Gradient Descent on the Edge of Stability: A Perturbative Derivation of the Central Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01034)
*Raphaël Berthier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个微扰框架，在该框架下Cohen等人(2025)提出的"边缘稳定性中心流"（central flow）可以被严格推导为梯度下降的极限行为。通过将损失函数分解为$f = g + \varepsilon h$并在$\varepsilon \to 0$的极限下分析，作者使用奇异摄动理论中的多尺度方法，证明了梯度下降的动力学收敛到受约束于$g$的极小值集合且尖锐度不超过$2/\eta$的$h$的梯度流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen等人(2025)的central flow虽然在经验上准确描述了深度学习在边缘稳定性下的梯度下降动力学，但其推导是启发式的、缺乏严格的理论基础。本文旨在通过提出一个微扰框架（perturbative regime），为central flow提供一个形式化的理论推导基础，弥合经验观察与理论解释之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者采用奇异摄动理论中的多尺度方法（method of multiple scales）来处理此问题。具体而言：(1) 将梯度下降视为在$\varepsilon$上的奇异摄动动力系统；(2) 识别出三个不同时间尺度：沿最尖锐方向的快速振荡、自稳定机制的中间时间尺度、沿$g$极小值集合的慢速中心流动动力学；(3) 在$\varepsilon$中展开动力学，central flow作为领头阶出现，自稳定机制作为次领头阶出现；(4) 对于单特征值处于边缘稳定性情形，计算涨落能量的慢漂移；对于多个特征值处于边缘稳定性情形，推导自稳定系统并解释涨落为何持续存在。

Result: 论文得出了以下结果：(1) 在$\varepsilon \to 0$的极限下，以学习率$\eta$运行的梯度下降动力学收敛到$h$受限于$g$极小值集合（尖锐度不超过$2/\eta$）的梯度流，即central flow；(2) 通过多尺度展开，central flow作为领头阶项出现，而自稳定机制体现在次领头阶；(3) 对单特征值情形，计算了涨落能量的慢漂移；(4) 对多特征值情形，推导出自稳定系统并解释了涨落持续存在的机制，这超越了以往的分析。

Conclusion: 本文通过奇异摄动的形式化框架为central flow提供了从梯度下降到极限行为的理论推导，确认了Cohen等人(2025)中心流模型的合理性。虽然该方法的推导是形式化的而非严格的，但它为理解深度学习在边缘稳定性区域的动力学提供了清晰的理论框架，并揭示了自稳定机制在不同特征值配置下的行为差异。

Abstract: The central flow of Cohen et al. (2025) is an empirically accurate continuous-time model of gradient descent at the edge of stability in deep learning, However, its derivation is heuristic. We propose a perturbative regime in which the central flow is the limit of gradient descent: we assume that the loss decomposes as $f = g + \varepsilon h$; in the limit $\varepsilon \to 0$, the dynamics of gradient descent with learning rate $η$ converge to the gradient flow of $h$ constrained to the minimizers of $g$ of sharpness at most $2/η$. Our approach is formal rather than rigorous; it treats gradient descent as a singularly perturbed dynamical system in $\varepsilon$. Three timescales emerge: a fast timescale of oscillations along the sharpest direction, an intermediate timescale of the self-stabilization mechanism, and a slow timescale of the dynamics along the minimizers of $g$-the central flow. Using the method of multiple scales, a classical formal method from singular perturbation theory, we derive the expansion of the dynamics in $\varepsilon$: the central flow emerges as the leading-order term in the expansion, while the self-stabilization mechanism appears in the next-order term. We study this mechanism beyond previous analyses: with a single eigenvalue at the edge of stability, we compute the slow drift of the energy of the fluctuations; with several eigenvalues at the edge of stability, we derive the self-stabilization system and explain why fluctuations persist.

</details>


### [185] [From Truncation to Commitment: Persistent Context in Uniform Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01043)
*Satoshi Hayakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种名为"提交揭示采样"(CRS)的训练免费采样方法，用于均匀状态离散扩散模型。与传统方法不同，CRS将选定的argmax token作为持久上下文存储并插入到后续模型输入中，而不是仅影响当前反向步骤。作者通过理论分析和实验证明，在相同最终化规则下，CRS无需使用top-p截断也能比固定p=0.95和p=0.9的基线在生成困惑度(GenPPL)上表现更好，并在GenPPL-熵权衡中取得更优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均匀状态离散扩散模型每次反向步骤都在并行更新所有token且可反复修订，即使top-p规则只留下一个候选，该选择也只影响当前步骤并可被后续修改。作者想探究一个关键问题：当选中的假设被转换为后续预测的持久上下文时会发生什么变化，从而提出CRS方法来利用选择token的持久性提升生成质量。

Method: 论文提出"提交揭示采样"(CRS)，这是一种无需训练的采样器，核心做法是将每次选择的argmax tokens存储起来并插入到后续模型输入中。分析方法为：(1)在精确前向过程下，证明随噪声减少，选择干净token的贝叶斯误差不会增加，为"更晚选择"提供理论依据；(2)在简单潜模式模型中，证明将选中token保持可见有助于后续并行预测达成一致的序列级选择；(3)在两个Duo蒸馏模型上进行配对实验，将持久效应与单步top-p限制和标量温度缩放分离对比。

Result: 在相同的最终化规则下，CRS无需top-p截断在8-64个NFE预算范围内均获得比固定p=0.95和p=0.9基线更低的生成困惑度(GenPPL)。在64 NFE时，在匹配的unigram熵水平比较下，CRS仍获得更低GenPPL，展现更优的GenPPL-熵权衡。Base Duo模型在描述性比较中呈现相同趋势。不过，其他的多样性和连续性指标可能对同一组操作点给出不同排序，说明这些指标从不同维度评估结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果识别出支持限制(support restriction)与持久上下文(persistent context)是对GenPPL-熵权衡起不同作用的两个独立控制因素。CRS通过将选中的token变为持久上下文实现了更优的生成质量与多样性权衡，为离散扩散模型采样提供了新的有效路径。

Abstract: Uniform-state discrete diffusion models update all tokens in parallel while keeping every position revisable. Even when the commonly used top-$p$ rule leaves only one candidate at a position, that choice affects only the current reverse step and can be revised at the next sampling step. We ask what changes when selected hypotheses instead become persistent context for later predictions. We therefore propose committed reveal sampling (CRS), a training-free sampler that stores selected argmax tokens and inserts them into subsequent model inputs. Our analysis gives a rationale for selecting later and for keeping selected tokens visible. Under the exact forward process, the Bayes error of selecting a clean token cannot increase as noise decreases, while in a simple latent-mode model, keeping the selected token visible helps later parallel predictions agree on the same sequence-level choice. Empirically, paired experiments on Duo-distilled then separate this persistent effect from single-step top-$p$ restriction and scalar temperature scaling. Under the same finalization rule, CRS without top-$p$ truncation reaches lower generative perplexity (GenPPL) than fixed $p=0.95$ and $p=0.9$ baselines across budgets of 8--64 function evaluations (NFE). At 64 NFE, the comparison at matched unigram entropy also gives lower GenPPL for CRS, yielding a more favorable GenPPL--entropy tradeoff. Base Duo shows the same direction in a descriptive comparison, while other diversity and continuation metrics can rank these operating points differently. These results identify support restriction and persistent context as distinct controls of that tradeoff.

</details>


### [186] [SAGE: Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement for Mitigating Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01051)
*Yiming Luo,Rongqiang Zhao,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架，一种两阶段生成式增强方法，在无虚假属性标签的情况下，通过聚类子标签和类别标签微调条件生成模型，生成针对性合成数据填补训练集中欠表示区域，并构建平衡验证集进行last-layer重加权，以缓解虚假相关性导致的少数群体性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集中固有的分布不平衡导致传统ERM模型依赖多数虚假属性进行分类，使少数群体性能表现差。当虚假属性不可用时问题更具挑战性，现有无组标签方法通过上采样少数群体或重复错分样本，但重复相同实例会降低有效多样性并鼓励过拟合。

Method: SAGE是两阶段生成式增强框架：首先利用聚类得到的子标签和类别标签，微调条件生成模型和文本编码器；然后生成针对性的合成数据以填补训练集中的欠表示区域，并构建平衡验证集用于最后一层重加权。

Result: 在Waterbirds、CelebA和MetaShift上分别达到89.5%、85.7%和79.1%的最差组准确率，相比最佳无组标签基线方法最高提升7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: SAGE从数据视角有效缓解了虚假相关性带来的鲁棒性问题，在无组标签的前提下通过生成式增强显著提升了少数群体的最差组准确率，验证了生成式方法在解决该问题上的潜力。

Abstract: Spurious correlations pose a significant challenge to the robustness of modern machine learning. The inherent imbalance in dataset distributions often leads traditional Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) models to rely on majority spurious attributes for classification, resulting in poor performance on minority groups. This problem becomes particularly challenging when the spurious attributes are unavailable. Existing group-label-free methods often upsample minority groups or misclassified real training examples; repeating the same instances can reduce effective diversity and encourage overfitting. To mitigate these spurious correlations from a data-centric perspective in the absence of prior knowledge, we introduce Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement (SAGE), a two-stage generative augmentation framework. Using cluster-derived sub-labels and class labels, we fine-tune a conditional generative model and text encoder, generating targeted synthetic data to fill underrepresented regions in the training set and construct a balanced validation set for last-layer reweighting. We experimentally show that SAGE achieves 89.5%, 85.7%, and 79.1% worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds, CelebA, and MetaShift, respectively, outperforming the best group-label-free baselines by up to 7.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [187] [Let Confidence Change, Not the Prediction: Prediction-Preserving Repair for Post-hoc Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01072)
*Daehwan Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CORD（Calibrator-Output Repair for Top-1 Decision Preservation），这是首个后拟合（post-fit）适配器，用于修复校准后的完整概率向量，从而强制保证校准不会改变原始模型的top-1预测。CORD仅利用原始与校准输出，无需额外训练或调参，即可达到零TPCR并在多个数据集上改善ECE、NLL和Brier等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后校准虽然能修正置信度，但多类校准器可能改变原始的top-1预测。现有评估指标（如准确率）只反映这些改变对正确性的净效应，无法度量预测改变的频率（TPCR）。校准中的预测保持是一个尚未解决的约束问题。

Method: CORD作为校准器拟合后的适配器，直接从原始与校准输出出发，确定分配给原始top-1类别的质量。它利用校准后的条件分布在其余类别间分配剩余质量，从而构造修复后的概率向量，使其argmax恢复原始预测。校准集上CORD协调修复后质量，在可行时保持校准输出的原始预测平均质量。该方法不修改校准器或其直接输出，不拟合额外监督映射，也无须用户或验证集调超参数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet-1K上，CORD按构造达到零TPCR，并在每个数据集的平均ECE、NLL和Brier指标上均优于相应的直接校准输出。在分布偏移和不同校准集规模下，成对的性能提升依然稳健。

Conclusion: CORD将预测保持约束从校准器拟合中剥离，交由事后输出修复来精确恢复原始决策，从而在不牺牲校准质量的前提下消除预测改变问题，为后续校准方法提供了通用且即插即用的后处理手段。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration corrects reported confidence, yet a multiclass calibrator can also change the associated top-1 prediction. Accuracy captures only the net effect of these changes on correctness, not how often predictions change; the Top-1 Prediction Change Rate (TPCR) instead measures this frequency. We propose Calibrator-Output Repair for Top-1 Decision Preservation (CORD), the first post-fit adapter to impose exact prediction preservation by repairing the full calibrated probability vector. From the original and calibrated outputs alone, CORD determines the mass assigned to the original top-1. The calibrated conditional distribution allocates the remaining mass over the other classes, yielding a repaired vector whose own argmax recovers the original prediction. On the calibration split, CORD coordinates the repaired masses to retain the calibrated outputs' mean mass on original predictions whenever attainable. The adapter alters neither the fitted calibrator nor its direct output, fits no additional supervised map, and requires no user- or validation-tuned hyperparameter. Across CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet-1K, CORD attains zero TPCR by construction and lowers mean ECE, NLL, and Brier relative to the corresponding direct outputs in every dataset; paired gains persist under distribution shift and across calibration-set sizes. CORD thus removes the preservation constraint from calibrator fitting and assigns exact recovery of the original decision to subsequent output repair. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/ORCU.

</details>


### [188] [Modelpedia: A Catalog of Model Findings for the Meta-Science of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01090)
*Franciszek Bernat,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Luca Longo,Jianlong Zhou,Andreas Holzinger,Riccardo Guidotti,Wojciech Samek,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Modelpedia，一个基于LLM的自动化框架，用于从已发表论文中提取关于AI模型的研究发现，并将其与相关模型、数据集、方法和概念关联，聚合成可搜索的公开目录。原型系统已在ICLR 2024和2025论文上应用，提取了超过一千条发现，并基于目录本身进行了元分析，以揭示社区研究AI模型的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AI模型的科学知识产生速度远超社区的组织能力。尽管有大量论文、博客和技术报告记录模型的行为与失败，但这些发现分散各处，实际上难以检索，缺乏一个系统化的知识整合框架。

Method: Modelpedia采用LLM辅助的自动化框架，从已发表论文中提取模型相关发现，建立发现与模型、数据集、方法、概念之间的关联链接，并将结果聚合成一个可搜索的公开目录。研究原型应用于ICLR 2024和2025年录用的论文进行验证。

Result: 在ICLR 2024和2025论文上成功提取了超过一千条模型发现，构建了可搜索的公开目录，并以该目录为研究对象进行了社区如何研究模型的元分析。

Conclusion: 作者邀请社区共同探索、贡献和建设这一开放目录，希望将"模型发现"确立为AI模型元科学的共享基础，推动该领域知识组织和研究范式的进步。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge about AI models is produced faster than the community can organize it. Every few months a new foundation model reshapes the field and hundreds of papers, blogs, and technical reports document how each behaves or fails. Yet, these findings remain scattered and effectively unretrievable. To address this gap we present Modelpedia, an automated, LLM-assisted framework that extracts findings about models from published papers, links it to the model, dataset, method, and concept it concerns, and aggregates the result into a searchable public catalog. Applying the prototype to accepted ICLR 2024 and 2025 papers, we extract over a thousand findings and, treating the catalog itself as an object of study, run a meta-analysis of how the community investigates models. Now, we invite the community to explore, contribute to, and build on the open catalog, and to help establish model findings as a shared foundation for the meta-science of AI.

</details>


### [189] [Subliminal Learning as Trait-Direction Drift: A Mechanism and Targeted Control under SFT Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01091)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuyu Fan,Xiangtian Li,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了"特质方向漂移"（trait-direction drift）作为隐性学习（subliminal learning）的机制：教师模型因系统提示偏差生成的清洁数据中存在的偏好差距，会在学生模型监督微调过程中累积为行为迁移。据此提出基于探针空间的走廊正则化方法，有效抑制隐藏特质迁移并保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究识别了模型蒸馏中隐性学习的部分环节，但信号在训练过程中如何累积并导致行为迁移的机制尚不清楚，使得针对性防御难以实现。作者旨在阐明这一机制并提出有效的定向防御方法。

Method: (1) 提出特质方向漂移机制：有偏生成在教师数据中产生可测量的偏好差距，学生模型在监督微调中识别该差距并沿特质方向产生参数更新，不断累积形成行为迁移；(2) 提出探针空间走廊正则化（probe-space corridor regularization）：在蒸馏过程中沿已校准的特质方向约束参数漂移，以抑制隐藏特质迁移。

Result: 该方法大幅降低隐藏特质迁移同时保持任务性能：恶意响应迁移率从29.55%降至6.45%，主任务准确率损失很低；在Qwen主设置下一致抑制动物偏好迁移。

Conclusion: 偏好差距、训练轨迹和干预实验三方面证据表明，隐性学习与特质方向漂移机制相关联，走廊正则化可作为蒸馏过程中针对性的可控手段。

Abstract: Beyond intended capabilities, model distillation can transfer hidden traits from a teacher. A teacher biased by a system prompt can generate semantically clean training data, such as numeric sequences, that still causes a downstream student to inherit the hidden preference, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. Prior work has identified several parts of this process. How the signal builds up during training and produces behavioral transfer remains unclear, making targeted mitigation difficult. We propose and validate trait-direction drift as a mechanism for subliminal learning: biased generation creates measurable preference gaps in teacher data, and student-recognizable gaps induce trait-aligned updates during supervised fine-tuning that accumulate into behavioral transfer. Guided by this mechanism, we propose probe-space corridor regularization, a targeted defense that constrains drift along a calibrated trait direction during distillation. The method substantially reduces hidden-trait transfer, preserving task performance: for example, it lowers malicious-response transfer from 29.55% to 6.45% with low main-task accuracy cost, and consistently suppresses animal-preference transfer across the main Qwen setting. The preference-gap, training-trajectory, and intervention evidence links subliminal learning to trait-direction drift and motivates corridor regularization as a targeted control during distillation.

</details>


### [190] [Neural Symbollic Regression Using Deep Learning and Sparse Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01102)
*Ravi Kumar U,Sumitra S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号回归（NSR）框架，将神经网络作为函数预条件器，通过解耦管道先学习平滑、抗噪的函数近似，再使用LASSO提取稀疏的闭式表达式，并集成Ray Tune和ASHA进行分布式超参数优化。在Nguyen基准测试上，该方法在RMSE、噪声鲁棒性和分布外泛化方面均优于SINDy和非调优的神经基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法（如遗传规划）面临可扩展性差和噪声敏感的问题，而稀疏回归技术（如SINDy）高度依赖预定义的特征库。现有方法难以同时兼顾可解释性、可扩展性和噪声鲁棒性，因此需要一种新的框架来弥补这些不足。

Method: 采用解耦的两阶段管道：首先使用神经网络在交互感知的非线性特征空间中学习目标函数的平滑且抗噪声的近似；然后应用LASSO提取稀疏、可解释的闭式表达式。同时集成Ray Tune与ASHA调度进行分布式超参数优化以提升预测精度和符号保真度。

Result: 在Nguyen基准套件上的实验表明，该方法在RMSE、噪声鲁棒性和分布外泛化方面一致优于SINDy和未调优的神经基线。消融研究证实了特征交互、神经深度和调优策略的重要性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个可扩展且可理解的神经-符号框架，在神经逼近与稀疏方程发现之间建立了坚实的桥梁，为科学机器学习中的符号发现提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) seeks to find succinct mathematical expressions that represent the fundamental relationships within data, providing interpretability and scientific understanding that exceeds that of black-box models. Nevertheless, traditional methods like Genetic Programming face challenges with scalability and are highly sensitive to noise, while sparse regression techniques such as SINDy rely significantly on predetermined feature libraries. In this work, we present a Neural Symbolic Regression (NSR) framework that treats neural networks as functional preconditioners for symbolic discovery. Our approach uses a decoupled pipeline: a neural network first learns a smooth, noise-robust approximation of the target function in an interaction- aware nonlinear feature space. LASSO is then applied to extract sparse, interpretable closed-form expressions. To improve predictive accuracy and symbolic fidelity by integrating distributed hyperparameter optimization with Ray Tune and ASHA scheduling. Experiments on the Nguyen benchmark suite show that our approach consistently outperforms SINDy and non-tuned neural baselines in RMSE, noise robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization. Ablation studies confirm the significance of feature interactions, neural depth, and tuning strategies. In general, this study presents a scalable and understandable neural-symbolic framework, creating a solid link between neural approximation and the discovery of sparse equations for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [191] [Replicating TRACE: A Practitioner's Guide to Its Threshold and Particle Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01108)
*Alex Chadyuk,Alicia Zhang,Roy Kucukates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了TRACE方法（用固定阈值对条件互信息估计做因果图挖掘），发现其效果高度依赖阈值与真实因果边界的吻合，实际只能恢复相邻有效因果（lag-1），对长延迟因果覆盖很差，且基准数据的偏斜误导了结论的解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并剖析TRACE因果发现方法的真实适用范围与局限，揭示其宣称的高性能背后隐藏的阈值敏感性和基准偏斜问题。

Method: 独立复现TRACE在合成数据上的实验；变化词表大小、因果时滞分布、粒子数量等条件；系统性地改变阈值选择策略（单阈值、按lag分阈值），与原子干预的对照比较，分析误差分布结构与估计器的收敛性质。

Result: 复现了论文的头部F1结果但发现：最优阈值由真值边界决定而非固定常数；TRACE单阈值主要恢复lag-1直接边（0.97-0.99），lag-2以上召回率低数十倍；默认基准中85%的真因果集中在lag-1，造成F1虚高；F1在粒子数N=2时即饱和，但估计器本身以N^-1/2收敛。

Conclusion: TRACE的实际能力是恢复相邻的有效直接因果，其在原基准上的高F1主要反映了数据偏斜而非算法优势；工程师需要注意阈值标定、时滞识别和评估基准的构造偏差，论文总结了五条实践规则。

Abstract: TRACE (Math & Lienhart, arXiv:2602.01135) reads causal graphs over event types out of a pretrained autoregressive sequence model by thresholding a per-position conditional-mutual-information estimate at a fixed tau. We independently replicate its headline synthetic result: with tau selected on a validation split, mean per-sequence F1 against exact interventional truth reaches 0.90-0.91 at vocabulary size 1000 (paper: 0.91) and 0.86-0.91 from 100 to 2000. First, the optimal threshold is pinned to the truth margin, not to any constant: at every size the errors at tau* straddle the delta = 0.05 margin defining ground truth (missed true edges lie just above it, accepted false ones just below), and the blind optimum lands near delta/2 times the estimator's calibration, confirmed out of sample at 5000. Second, at a single global threshold TRACE mostly recovers a direct, adjacent-influence graph: lag-1 true edges are recalled at 0.97-0.99, while true edges at lag 2 or more read orders of magnitude lower---the reading-scale price of randomizing mediating positions, which an exact test of direct causal effect requires when the truth is unknown. A per-lag threshold family recovers a third to a half of lag-2 truth; on lag-uniform data one validated threshold recalls every lag at 0.40-0.87, 8-26 pp below an atomic-intervention control at lags 3-6. Third, the default lag decay of the paper's synthetic benchmark concentrates about 85% of interventional truth at lag 1 and pushes the rest below the estimator's noise floor, so headline F1 there certifies lag-1 recovery only and conflates the benchmark's skew with the algorithm's own limit; a flatter decay separates the two. Fourth, F1 saturates from N = 2 particles at the selected threshold---a property of the threshold's margin over the noise floor, not of the estimator, which converges as N^(-1/2). We distill five practitioner rules.

</details>


### [192] [When Does Online Adaptation Pay on the Edge? A Leakage-Free Evaluation of Warmup, Learning-Rate Selection, and Resource Trade-offs for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01126)
*Takumi Fujimoto,Hiroaki Nishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了边缘时间序列预测中的在线自适应评估偏差问题，识别出两个额外的比较偏差来源——静态基线的预热预算和优化器学习率设置——并提出了一种仅使用验证集切片、不接触测试数据的校验程序来公平评估自适应收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自适应可以帮助边缘时间序列预测应对分布漂移，但其实际收益强烈依赖于评估方式的选择。以往工作未充分识别比较中的偏差来源，可能导致对自适应方法收益的高估或错误判断。

Method: 作者在六个公共多元流数据集（包括建筑传感器和智能电表数据）上，采用无泄漏的流式协议进行研究。他们系统分析了预热预算对静态基线的影响（发现其存在双向效应），并纠正了将SGD+m与Adam在共享默认学习率下比较所产生的混淆。他们使用留出的预漂移验证切片来同时选择预热预算和各优化器的在线学习率，不访问测试数据，并在全参数、仅头部和基于校准的自适应设置下评估准确率与适应状态内存及A100实测的逐更新延迟之间的关系。

Result: 在六个数据集-骨干网络组合中，自适应收益估计在1000到20000步预热范围内变化3.0至18.8个百分点。在仅验证程序下，Adam在360个评估单元中310个优于SGD+m，但仍有4个Adam单元低于静态基线。在PatchTST前沿设置中，多个参数高效变体在适应状态内存轴上非占优。智能电表分析还显示报告收益取决于电表选择规则。

Conclusion: 研究支持采用仅验证的调试程序来评估边缘自适应的收益，同时指出目标设备的延迟和能耗指标仍待进一步测量。

Abstract: Online adaptation can help edge time-series forecasting under distribution drift, but its measured benefit is sensitive to evaluation choices. We study six public multivariate streams, including building-sensor and smart-meter data, under a leakage-free streaming protocol. We identify two additional sources of comparison bias. First, the warmup budget of the static baseline has a two-sided effect: insufficient warmup undertrains the baseline, whereas excessive warmup can degrade its pre-drift generalization. Across six dataset-backbone settings, the estimated adaptation benefit changes by 3.0 to 18.8 percentage points (pp) over the 1,000-20,000-step warmup range. Second, comparing SGD with momentum (SGD+m) and Adam at a shared default learning rate conflates optimizer quality with rate sensitivity. We select both the warmup budget and each optimizer's online rate using a held-out pre-drift validation slice without accessing test data. Under this validation-only procedure, Adam outperforms SGD+m in 310 of 360 evaluated cells, while 4 Adam cells remain below the static baseline. We further characterize accuracy against adaptation-state memory and A100-measured per-update latency for full, head-only, and calibration-based adaptation. In the evaluated PatchTST frontier settings, several parameter-efficient variants are nondominated on the adaptation-state-memory axis. Smart-meter analyses also show that reported gains depend on meter-selection rules. These findings support a validation-only commissioning procedure, while target-device latency and energy remain to be measured. Code, data, and all reported numbers: https://github.com/keiotakmin/tsf-edge-adaptation.

</details>


### [193] [Scaled Idempotence in Transformer Attention: Paired OV Geometry and Shared-Value Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01129)
*Jiming Feng,Junliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer注意力中存在一种反复出现的代数规律：一小部分有效的OV算子T=OV^T在组合下近似封闭，即T²≈αT（缩放幂等性）。这一现象在多个大规模预训练模型中得到验证，且该性质依赖于K矩阵的特定方向（而非仅奇异值等几何因素）。在共享值向量的情况下，该性质可推广为右作用代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer注意力机制中为何特定的OV算子在训练后呈现近似幂等的代数结构，以及这种结构与算子的几何容量（几何能力是否广泛可用）和训练方向（特定的面向为何被选中）之间的关系。

Method: 1) 在14个预训练端点和9个MHA/GQA模型（共7,304个注意力头）上计算封闭对齐度P≥0.9的头部比例；2) 提出精确的主坐标分解T=Q_OKQ_V^T和T²=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^T来分离传输与读写回返几何；3) 通过打乱K的方向（保留奇异值、范数、因子跨度、主角度）来测试方向的重要性；4) 进行构造性搜索验证高封闭性是否在每层均可实现；5) 回顾性分析多个训练谱系的轨迹；6) 在共享值向量时推导T_iT_j=α_jT_i的右作用代数。

Result: 在2.8B-235B参数的6个预训练端点中，3.98%-8.00%的注意力头达到封闭对齐度P≥0.9，而匹配的层内O/V错配头均未达到。打乱K取向使中位封闭度从0.336降至1.04×10^-4（98.64%的头和所有层都更偏好训练后的方向）。构造性搜索表明每层都能实现高封闭性但通常未被达到。七个模型验证了值共享下的右作用代数法则，揭示了具有共享值定义核的不同斜投影。

Conclusion: 缩放幂等性是稀疏训练出的方向性性质，其嵌入在广泛可用的几何容量中；值共享机制将单头的关系扩展为局部算子代数，为理解Transformer注意力的代数计算结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: We identify a recurrent algebraic regularity in Transformer attention: a sparse subset of effective OV operators $T=OV^\top$ nearly closes under composition, $T^2\approxαT$. Across six pretrained endpoints spanning 2.8B--235B parameters, 3.98--8.00% of heads reach squared closure alignment $\mathcal{P}\geq0.9$, while no matched within-layer O/V mismatch does. An exact principal-coordinate factorization, $T=Q_OKQ_V^\top$ and $T^2=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^\top$, separates within-support transport from read--write return geometry. Across all 7,304 heads in nine MHA/GQA models, scrambling only the orientation of $K$ while preserving singular values, norms, factor spans, and principal angles reduces median closure from 0.336 to $1.04\times10^{-4}$; trained orientation wins for 98.64% of heads and in every layer. Constructive searches show that high closure is feasible in every surveyed layer, but usually not attained. Retrospective trajectories in three independently trained lineages further separate broadly available capacity from the orientations attained by final strong heads. Under exact value sharing, headwise closure extends to a right-action algebra, $T_iT_j=α_jT_i$. Seven-model experiments verify the approximate law and reveal distinct oblique projections with a shared value-defined kernel. These results characterize scaled idempotence as a sparse trained orientation within broadly available geometric capacity and show how value sharing extends a headwise relation into a local operator algebra.

</details>


### [194] [Superposed Latent Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01158)
*Quanling Zhao,Jiaying Yang,Tianqi Zhang,Ziyang Hao,Fatemeh Asgarinejad,Flavio Ponzina,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超位置潜在自编码器（SLAE），通过将多个宽潜在表示超位置叠加存储在单一记忆张量中，在相同存储预算下替代传统的维度瓶颈压缩方式，重建误差最高降低56%，下游分类性能最高提升16.79个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自编码器通过缩减每个潜在表示的维度来满足存储预算限制，但这会牺牲表示容量。作者提出质疑：能否改为同时存储多个更宽的潜在表示？核心动机是通过共享存储来保留高容量表示，而非单纯压缩单个表示。

Method: SLAE包含四个关键步骤：(1) 将潜在表示转换为存储友好的编码；(2) 使用随机密钥对其进行绑定；(3) 将多个编码超位置叠加到单一记忆张量中；(4) 学习在解码前恢复每个潜在表示。该方法用可抑制的结构性干扰取代不可逆的维度瓶颈。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、STL-10和Tiny ImageNet等多个数据集及多种存储预算下，SLAE在相同存储条件下重建误差最高降低56%；在下游分类任务中性能提升最高达16.79个百分点。分析表明优势来源于在相同存储预算下使更宽的表示变得可用。

Conclusion: 研究提出一种新的表示压缩原则：与其让每个潜在表示都变得更小，不如保持表示宽度并让其共享存储空间。SLAE验证了这一原则的有效性，在重建与存储的权衡以及下游任务性能上均显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Autoencoders typically meet tight latent-memory budgets by making each latent representation smaller, sacrificing representational capacity. We ask a different question: can multiple wider latents be stored together instead? We introduce the Superposed Latent Autoencoder (SLAE), which preserves high-capacity latent representations while sharing storage through learned superposition. SLAE transforms latents into storage-friendly codes, binds them with randomized keys, superposes multiple codes into a single memory tensor, and learns to recover each latent before decoding. Under the same storage budget, SLAE replaces irreversible dimensional bottlenecks with structured interference that can be suppressed. Across CIFAR-10/100, SVHN, STL-10, Tiny ImageNet, and a wide range of memory budgets, SLAE substantially improves the reconstruction--memory tradeoff, reducing reconstruction error by up to 56% over conventional autoencoders at matched storage. Further analysis shows that SLAE's advantage comes from making wider representations usable under the same storage budget. These gains also extend beyond reconstruction: the information preserved by SLAE improves downstream classification by up to 16.79 percentage points under the same memory budget. Our results suggest a new principle for representation compression: instead of making every latent smaller, keep representations wide and let them share memory.

</details>


### [195] [CopyShield: A Cross-Level Benchmark of Copyright Defenses in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01161)
*Maryam Alshehyari,Dushyant Singh Chauhan,Samuele Poppi,Martin Takac,Salem Lahlou,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopyShield是一个受控基准测试，系统比较了三种不同干预层级的版权防御方法（对比解码、DPO、激活干预），在LLAma-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B-v0.3上评估字面泄漏、非字面泄漏、效用和退化性。研究发现干预层级与合规-效用权衡存在关联：对比解码无退化但有字面抑制下限，DPO几乎消除字面泄漏但产生循环退化，激活干预通过广泛拒绝降低感知风险，同时识别出定向非字面抑制仍是开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的版权防御方法评估缺乏统一标准，各方法在不同协议下难以公平比较，无法揭示不同干预层级（输出层、行为层、表征层）在实际版权保护中的真实效能与权衡。

Method: 构建CopyShield受控基准，使用五本公有领域书籍生成受控记忆数据，在共享协议下测量字面泄漏（NV-Recall）、校准的非字面泄漏、效用和退化性指标，对三种有代表性的防御方法（对比解码、DPO、激活干预）在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B-v0.3两个模型系列上进行系统评估，并结合人工评估验证感知质量与版权风险。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B上：对比解码退化率仅0-2%但字面抑制存在下限（NV-Recall 0.192-0.203）；DPO将字面泄漏从0.263降至0.002但导致58%的QA输出出现释义循环退化且无效用增益；激活干预通过预生成阶段阻断84%的非字面查询，非字面标记率低至1/200。在Mistral-7B-v0.3上，输出层和表征层模式持续存在，而DPO退化率降至10-14%，表明其严重程度依赖模型。

Conclusion: CopyShield为版权防御提供了跨干预层级的参考基线，揭示了不同层级方法在合规与效用之间的权衡规律。输出级干预（对比解码）可保持文本自然性但有字面抑制瓶颈；行为级干预（DPO）字面抑制最强但可能严重损害输出质量且代价因模型而异；表征级干预（激活干预）通过广泛拒绝有效降低版权感知风险，但牺牲了合理的非字面使用。定向非字面版权抑制是尚待解决的开放性挑战。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce memorized text verbatim, yet copyright defenses are usually evaluated under incompatible protocols. We introduce CopyShield, a controlled benchmark comparing three representative defenses at distinct intervention levels: contrastive decoding (output), Direct Preference Optimization (behavioral), and activation intervention (representation). We evaluate CopyShield on two model families, LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, using controlled memorization over five public-domain books and a shared protocol measuring literal leakage, calibrated non-literal leakage, utility, and degeneracy. Across these methods, intervention level is associated with distinct compliance-utility trade-offs. On LLaMA-3.1-8B, contrastive decoding remains near-degeneracy-free (0-2%) but reaches a literal-suppression floor at NV-Recall 0.192-0.203. DPO nearly eliminates literal leakage (0.263 to 0.002) but induces paraphrase-loop degeneracy in 58% of QA outputs, with no utility gain over the SFT baseline. Activation intervention attains the lowest non-literal flagging rate (1/200) by blocking 84% of non-literal queries before generation. Human evaluation confirms that DPO has low coherence, whereas activation lowers perceived copyright risk through broad refusal. On Mistral-7B-v0.3, the output- and representation-level patterns persist, while DPO degeneracy falls to 10-14%, showing that its severity is model-dependent. Together, CopyShield provides cross-level reference baselines and identifies targeted non-literal suppression as an open challenge. The code is available at https://github.com/spotai-mbzuai/CopyShield.git.

</details>


### [196] [Pre-carved Niches: The Formation Dynamics of Modular Task Partitions in Early LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01170)
*Guangqi Li,Yongxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过从零训练Pythia-410M模型（bf16和fp32两条轨迹），逐步追踪大语言模型模块化内部组织的形成过程，发现模块化地图在训练前就已预刻好，分区通过两次急剧跳跃锁定，且偏差仅出现在正在学习的领域中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅描述训练完成后的模型，对大语言模型模块化组织如何在训练过程中逐步形成缺乏理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，追踪模块化内部组织（类似人脑功能网络）的形成步骤。

Method: 从零训练Pythia-410M模型（两条轨迹：bf16和fp32），在每一步进行属性修补（attribution patching），同时用探针测量梯度范数、有效更新、权重范数以及跨14个任务、4个认知领域的一阶损失分解。

Result: 三项发现：（1）模块化地图预先刻好——学习前主导任务对即已重叠约3.6倍于基线，其第0层浓度是该模型家族的架构级常数；（2）分区通过两次急剧跳跃锁定，振幅不随学习率调度变化（第二次达20.4 sigma安静窗口/6.2 sigma全局），伴随梯度级相对剥夺（胜者获得2.25-2.73倍于败者的梯度供应，低于随机对照9.5-11.5个标准差），但不传播到更新或权重；（3）偏差仅在正在学习的领域出现，支持模块化追踪学习的假说。

Conclusion: 作者区分了可辩护的特征级解释与无法回答的机制性问题，并预先注册了正在进行的2.8B实验背后的尺度阈值假说。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a modular internal organization that mirrors well-studied functional networks of the human brain, but how this organization forms during training is unknown: prior work has characterized finished models, not the formation process. We track formation step by step: we train a Pythia-410M model from scratch (two trajectories, bf16 and fp32) and run attribution patching at every step, alongside probes for gradient norms, effective updates, weight norms, and first-order loss decomposition across 14 tasks in four cognitive domains. Three findings. First, the modular map is pre-carved: before any learning, the dominant task pair already overlaps at ~3.6x the attribution substrate (a task-independent baseline), and its layer-0 concentration is an architecture-level constant on this model family. Second, the partition locks in through two sharp jumps whose amplitudes do not track the learning-rate schedule (the second reaching 20.4 sigma quiet-window / 6.2 sigma global), accompanied by gradient-level relative deprivation--winners receive 2.25->2.73x the loser's gradient supply, 9.5-11.5 standard deviations below a random control--that does not propagate to updates or weights. Third, deviation from the substrate appears only in the domain being learned, consistent with the hypothesis that modularity tracks learning. We close by separating the feature-level account we can defend from the mechanistic questions we cannot, and we pre-register the scale-threshold hypothesis behind our ongoing 2.8B experiments.

</details>


### [197] [Births are difficult to predict even with rich survey and full-population register data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01194)
*Elizaveta Sivak,Emily M. Cantrell,Thomas Emery,Javier Garcia-Bernardo,Flavio Hafner,Kasia Karpinska,Malte Lüken,Adrienne Mendrik,Joris Mulder,Hanzhang Ren,Varun Satish,Mark Verhagen,Angelica M. Maineri,Paulina Pankowska,Jasmin Abdel Ghany,Bruno Arpino,Giovanni Cassani,Julia Hellstrand,Katya Ivanova,Sanni Kuikka,Ana Macanovic,Charles Rahal,Felix C. Tropf,Roland J. Veen,Nicole Walasek,Daniël van Wijk,Kelsey Q. Wright,Emilio Zagheni,Henry Abbink,Emanuele Aliverti,Matteo Amestoy,Tilbe Atav,Nicola Barban,Sunnee Billingsley,Goan J. Booij,Louis Boucherie,Yael Broos,Li Ya Chang,Jamie C. Chiu,Chiara Ludovica Comolli,Boris Cule,Qixiang Fang,Dennis M. Feehan,Rachel Ganly,Erwin Gielens,Rolando M. Gonzales Martinez,Andrea Gradassi,Rosember Guerra-Urzola,Mario Guerra-Urzola,Stéphane Guerrier,Enamul Hassan,Vincent A. Haverhoek,Andrew T. Hendrickson,Amber Howard,Yuxuan Jin,Sayash Kapoor,Erik-Jan van Kesteren,Iris ten Klooster,Marie Labussiere,Lydia T. Liu,Tiffany Liu,Adam Maghout,Simone Meneghello,Lasse Mohr,Clara H. Mulder,Saul J. Newman,Jessica Nisén,Janis Norden,Mikkel Odgaard,Riccardo Omenti,Ozancan Ozdemir,Christina Pao,Paige Park,Gaia Penta,Juan C. Perdomo,Tanzir Pial,Alessio Piraccini,Federica Querin,Ziwei Rao,Christian Rellama,Adrien Remund,Frederieke Richert,Arnout van de Rijt,Mojtaba Rostami Kandroodi,Stijn J. Rotman,Lucas Sage,Germans Savcisens,Katrin Schwanitz,Steven Skiena,Alessandro Spata,Yannick Stadtfeld,Benedikt Stroebl,Gaetano Tedesco,Mathilde Theelen,Gianluca Tori,Abigail Tun-Mendicuti,Rishabh Tyagi,Keyon Vafa,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Linda Vecgaile,Willem R. J. Vermeulen,Maria-Pia Victoria Feser,Lionel A. Voirol,Thom B. Volker,Xinran Wang,Jiani Yan,Xinyi Zhao,Flora Zhou,Zuzana Zilincikova,Malvina Nissim,Matthew J. Salganik,Gert Stulp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过一项包含147名研究者的数据挑战赛，利用调查数据和全人口登记数据预测荷兰18-45岁居民三年内生育的事件，发现预测精度中等（最佳F1：登记数据0.59，调查数据0.76），先进模型并未超越传统方法，更大规模的登记数据也未优于调查数据。通过模拟受孕与怀孕的随机生物学过程，估计了预测上限（调查F1约0.86-0.94，登记数据0.88-0.96），实际表现与上限存在差距，表明数据、方法的不完善及未被建模的随机性共同导致预测局限，且生育过程中的随机性本身对个体人生预测构成了不容忽视的天然壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大人生事件难以预测是一个普遍现象，但学界对其原因存在分歧——究竟是理论、数据和算法的局限，还是事件本身随机性（运气）占据主导？作者希望在一个接近理想的预测环境中阐明这个问题。

Method: 组织一项大规模数据挑战赛，邀请147名研究者使用荷兰全人口登记数据与调查数据预测居民三年内生育事件，方法涵盖逻辑回归到大型语言模型和Transformer等先进算法；同时构建受孕与怀孕过程的随机生物学模拟，评估理论上可达到的预测性能上限。

Result: 预测准确度中等：最佳F1值在登记数据为0.59、调查数据为0.76；先进模型未超越经典模型；更大规模的登记数据未优于调查数据；模拟出的预测上限为调查F1约0.86-0.94、登记数据0.88-0.96，实际表现低于此上限。

Conclusion: 预测性能低于理论上限说明，数据不完善、方法论局限及未被建模的随机性共同造成了实际预测差距；但即便数据和方法达到近乎理想状态，生殖过程固有的随机性也为个体生活事件的预测设置了实质性且不可忽视的天花板。

Abstract: Major life events have proven difficult to predict. Does this reflect limits of theory, data, and algorithms, or the large role of chance? We examine one outcome - having a child within three years - through a near-ideal setting for prediction: a data challenge where 147 researchers predicted births for Dutch residents aged 18-45, using survey data and full-population registers. Methods ranged from logistic regression to a large language model and transformers. Predictions were moderately accurate (best F1: register 0.59, survey 0.76); advanced models did not outperform classical ones; and the larger registers did not beat the survey. Simulating the stochastic biology of conception and pregnancy, we estimated a predictive ceiling (survey F1 ~ 0.86-0.94, register 0.88-0.96). Observed performance falls short of this ceiling, implicating imperfect data, methods, and unmodelled chance, while the ceiling itself shows that chance in reproduction alone sets a non-trivial limit on predicting individual lives.

</details>


### [198] [Recent Developments in Transformer Inference Deployment on FPGA Platforms: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01212)
*Arjan Blankestijn,Uraz Odyurt,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Transformer模型在FPGA平台上进行推理的最新进展进行了系统性文献综述，探讨了实现技术与优化策略，并提出了主题分类法，旨在为学术界和工业界的研究人员提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构的机器学习模型应用快速增长，需要具备高吞吐量、低延迟和低能耗的高效部署方案。FPGA作为CPU和GPU之外的替代硬件加速器，具有实现灵活、能效高、延迟低和适合现场部署等优势，因此有必要系统梳理其在Transformer推理方面的最新进展。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对Transformer推理在FPGA平台上的最新研究成果进行提取和深入分析，梳理实现与优化的首选技术，并构建涵盖各主题的分类学框架。

Result: 总结了Transformer在FPGA上推理的最新技术趋势、设计选择和偏好实现方案，并产出了一个可用于指导研究工作的主题分类体系。

Conclusion: 本综述及所提出的分类法可作为学术与工业研究人员在FPGA上部署Transformer推理任务时的实用指南，帮助其做出明智的设计与优化决策。

Abstract: With the rapid and continuous growth in the incorporation of machine learning models based on the Transformer architecture, capable deployment is in high demand. In this context, capable deployment refers to operational performance aspects, e.g., throughput and latency, as well as efficiency aspects, e.g., energy consumption. When it comes to the task of inference using such models, purpose-built hardware accelerators provide a lucrative alternative to common deployment choices, such as Central Processing Units (CPUs) and Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). The Field Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) platforms category is an example of such alternative accelerators, promising implementation flexibility, energy efficiency, improved latency and suitability for on-site deployment. We investigate the most recent advances, trends, and design choices for Transformer inference on FPGA platforms. We perform a systematic literature review, extracting and delving into preferred techniques for implementation and optimisation. This study and the provided taxonomy of topics could act as a guide for researchers from the academia and industry alike.

</details>


### [199] [Multi-Head Self Attention is a Parameter Identification Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01231)
*W. Ross Morrow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明多头缩放点积注意力可被视为一种参数识别策略。未识别参数与总参数之比随头数增加从1/2下降到1/(2H)，即多头模型在结构上更可识别。同时揭示了注意力永远无法被完全识别，某些偏置项在softmax注意力层中无效，并从识别角度讨论了RoPE和GQA等现代改进如何提升"有意义"参数的占比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在从数学/统计的"参数可识别性"角度，为Transformer特定架构选择（如多头注意力、RoPE、GQA）为何能提升性能提供一种纯粹的理论解释，而非仅停留在实证观察层面。

Method: 作者将多头缩放点积注意力形式化为参数识别问题的数学框架，推导未识别参数比例与头数H的关系（1/2→1/(2H)）；分析单头和多头设置下softmax注意力层中无效偏置项；从识别角度分析RoPE和GQA的改进效果；通过数值实验（包括一种"再平衡"方法来修正与未识别子空间重叠的更新）验证理论结果。

Result: 多头注意力显著降低未识别参数比例；注意力始终存在固有不可识别性；某些偏置项对softmax注意力无影响；RoPE和GQA也能改善"有意义"参数与总参数的比值；数值实验证实训练更新确实会落入因缺乏识别导致的模型不变子空间。

Conclusion: 该文章为Transformer架构改进提供了纯粹数学/统计层面的"参数识别"解释框架，说明增加头数、RoPE和GQA等设计选择在结构上提升了模型的可识别性，从而可能对性能产生积极影响。作者提醒勿将"再平衡"方法视为实际建议，仅作为验证理论的手段。

Abstract: We prove that a multi-head scaled dot product attention can be viewed as a parameter identification strategy. The ratio of unidentified parameters to the total number of parameters scales like the reciprocal of the number of heads ($1/2 \to 1/(2H)$), meaning models with more heads are structurally more identified. A subtle side effect of the mathematics observation that attention can never be fully identified. Similarly we also show that some bias terms can have no effect on softmax-based attention layers in both the single- and multiple-head settings, though this is mostly a curiosity that should have a marginal effect on model size and model training/prediction efficiency. We also touch on modern improvements to transformers including RoPE and GQA from this perspective, illustrating how those as well can improve the ratio of ``meaningful'' parameters to all parameters. Simple numerical examples demonstrate that training can indeed involve updates that overlap model-invariant subspaces that arise from a lack of identification. As part of our experiments we use a ``rebalancing'' approach that can ``fix'' updates that overlap unindentified subspaces but do not try to present evidence this should actually be adopted. Instead we simply view our numerical results as exploring and confirming the theoretical results. As a whole we discuss a purely mathematical/statistical explanation, identification, for why specific architectural choices in transformers may have improved performance.

</details>


### [200] [Post-Training Science for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01244)
*Charles O'Neill,Mudith Jayasekara,Harry Partridge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化的单变量扫描实验，在Qwen3和Llama两个模型家族（涵盖稠密和MoE架构，最高至235B参数）、四个真实客户SFT数据集上，系统测量了监督微调中各项超参数决策（学习率、批大小、LoRA vs 全量微调、epoch数、优化器等）的优化规则及其跨模型和数据集的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次监督微调都需要重新摸索学习率、批大小、LoRA或全量微调等决策，这些经验缺乏系统性测量和跨场景验证，导致大量重复劳动和不确定性。

Method: 采用"一次只改变一个变量"的系统扫描方法，在两个模型家族（Qwen3和Llama）上涵盖稠密与MoE架构，在四个由客户构建的带评估的SFT数据集上同时进行LoRA和全量微调实验。数据集经迭代式生成以保证监督目标与评估标准内部一致。

Result: 刻画了最优学习率和批大小随模型规模、家族和数据的变化规律及其选择规则的迁移性；量化了LoRA与全量微调的权衡及rank和alpha对可学习能力的影响；检验了验证损失和损失景观平坦度对下游质量的排序能力；追踪了性能随规模和数据的扩展规律（至235B MoE）；发现了通用指令跟随能力受侵蚀的epoch阈值；评估了几何感知优化器相对AdamW的改进，并为每条建议提供了不确定性度量。

Conclusion: 为监督微调超参数选择提供了系统化的经验法则和不确定性量化，使各项决策不再需要从零摸索，可跨模型和数据集迁移。

Abstract: Every supervised fine-tuning run forces the same chain of decisions, such as learning rate, batch size, LoRA or full fine-tuning, how many epochs, which optimiser, and what data to feed the model. Each of these is typically rediscovered from scratch for every new model and dataset. Here we measure them under one instrument: a sweep that varies one lever at a time, and spans dense and mixture-of-experts models in two families (Qwen3 and Llama), on four real-world customer SFT datasets, for both LoRA and full fine-tuning. These datasets give a controlled testbed: each task carries an evaluation built with the customer, and its training data is produced by iterative supervised fine-tuning that refines model outputs until they pass that evaluation, so the supervised target is internally consistent and the task judge we report against is the criterion the data was built to satisfy. We ask how the optimal learning rate and batch size move with model scale, family, and data, and whether one selection rule transfers across them; what LoRA trades against full fine-tuning, and how its rank and alpha set what the adapter can learn; whether validation loss (or other metrics, such as loss landscape flatness) faithfully ranks downstream quality; whether post-training gains scale with model size and data volume, on a model ladder extended through mixtures-of-experts to 235B parameters; how many epochs to train before general instruction-following erodes; and whether a geometry-aware optimiser improves on AdamW. Each recommendation is paired with a measure of its uncertainty.

</details>


### [201] [Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01245)
*Liming Pu,Xiaoxia Li,Yifu Liu,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CANOPY（Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL），一种针对小型开放模型在长程交互任务上做结果导向RL的极简训练协议。CANOPY通过扩大同任务探索规模使稀疏奖励信号重新显现，并采用on-policy、KL锚定且仅约束agent自身动作token的更新策略来防止策略漂移。在AppWorld基准上，Qwen3-14B仅通过环境交互训练即登顶公开排行榜（Test-Normal TGC 86.9），并在SWE-bench Verified上使Qwen3.5-9B提升16.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为仅靠结果奖励的RL在小规模开放模型上很快触及天花板，因此现有工作普遍依赖稠密奖励、SFT先验、技能库等外部补偿。作者认为这个天花板源于两个常见实践缺陷：信号饥饿（sparse outcome-only reward在组间对比RL下，只有成功与失败混合时才产生梯度，探索不足会让最难的任务沉默）和策略漂移（小型任务池上过量更新导致无锚定目标使采样分布崩溃）。

Method: CANOPY协议包含三点：1）扩大同任务探索规模直到自然奖励信号重新出现；2）保持每次更新为on-policy并做KL锚定，且仅约束agent自身的动作token（不约束环境/观察token）；3）在测试时利用扩展的交互预算。整个训练仅依赖环境交互，无需任务特定监督、辅助信用信号或复杂agent脚手架。

Result: 在AppWorld长程交互编码基准上，Qwen3-14B模型经CANOPY训练后以Test-Normal TGC 86.9、Test-Challenge 67.6的成绩登顶2026年2月公共排行榜。在SWE-bench Verified上，相同设计原则使Qwen3.5-9B提升16.6个百分点。训练代码将开源至GitHub。

Conclusion: 仅靠agentic RL就能将长程交互能力直接内化到小型开放模型中，挑战了"结果导向RL需要外部补偿信号才能在小模型上工作"的普遍信念，表明问题不在于RL范式本身，而在于训练协议的工程细节。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. Signal starvation: group-relative RL with sparse outcome-only rewards yields a gradient only when a task's rollout group mixes successes and failures, so under-scaled exploration silences exactly the hardest, most instructive tasks. Policy drift: squeezing many updates out of a small task pool degrades the policy itself, as an unanchored objective lets the sampling distribution collapse exactly when saturation has already made informative groups rare. We present CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL), a minimalist protocol attacking both directly: scale same-task exploration until the natural signal reappears, keep every update on-policy, KL-anchored, and confined to the agent's own action tokens, then cash in an enlarged interaction budget at test time. On AppWorld, a long-horizon interactive coding benchmark, a Qwen3-14B policy trained with CANOPY through environment interaction alone--without task-specific supervision, auxiliary credit signals, or elaborate agent scaffolding--topped the public leaderboard (Feb. 2026; Test-Normal TGC 86.9, Test-Challenge 67.6), and the same design principles lift Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified by 16.6 points. Agentic RL alone thus internalizes long-horizon capability directly into a small open model; we plan to release the complete training stack at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/SignalCoverageRL.

</details>


### [202] [Solving In-Table Prediction Problems by Deep Neural Networks with Performance Evaluation Using Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01262)
*Xiao Zhao,Daniela Oelke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"表内预测"(In-Table Prediction, ITB)问题，即利用深度神经网络根据表中已知列预测任意选定列的数值，区别于传统监督学习和表填充方法。采用自监督学习策略，随机掩码部分列作为学习目标，并提出一种新颖的神经层来处理连续特征中的缺失值。在基于预定义列关系合成的数据上，评估了MLP、ResNet和Transformer三种架构，发现当训练样本充足且嵌入长度较大时，基于注意力机制的Transformer表现最佳。但作者强调这是在受控合成条件下的初步、窄范围研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格深度学习方法依赖显式给定的目标特征进行监督学习，而作者希望探索一种不同的范式——让神经网络学习表格中各列之间的内在关系，从而能够基于已知列预测任意列的值。这一方向在文献中尚未被广泛研究。

Method: 采用自监督学习方法，随机选择列进行掩码作为学习目标。针对仅含连续特征的表格数据集，提出一种新型神经层以同时嵌入数值和空值。基于预定义列关系合成训练数据，通过两种不同机制插入缺失值，并采用适配的掩码策略生成测试数据。在生成数据上评估MLP、ResNet和Transformer三种神经网络架构的性能。

Result: 当训练样本数量充足且选择较大的嵌入长度时，基于注意力机制的Transformer架构优于MLP和ResNet两种网络。

Conclusion: 该研究属于在受控合成条件下、少量列范围内的初步探索性工作，发现注意力结构在特定条件下表现更优，但不应被视为对真实世界表格数据上表内预测问题的普适性结论，后续仍需在更广泛和实际的场景中验证。

Abstract: Tabular deep learning (TDL) leverages neural networks (NN) to extract patterns from tabular data. Traditional TDL methods follow a supervised learning paradigm, where a target feature is explicitly given. In this work, however, we explore a different approach by employing deep NNs to learn relationships among individual columns within a given table. We investigate whether NNs can predict the values of arbitrarily selected columns in a given table based on the remaining known columns. We call this problem In-Table Prediction (ITB), which is slightly different from table imputation methods and the pretraining task of TDL. Three potential usage scenarios are identified, which, to our best knowledge, have not been extensively studied in the literature. A self-supervised learning approach is applied to address this problem by randomly selecting columns to be masked out and used as learning targets. This work focuses on tabular datasets containing only continuous features. To handle missing values in continuous features, a novel neural layer is proposed to embed both numerical and empty values. Synthetic data is generated based on predefined column relationships, with empty values inserted using two distinct mechanisms. Additionally, an adapted masking strategy is employed to create test data. Performances of three NN architectures, namely MLP, Resnet and Transformer, are evaluated using the generated synthetic data. We conclude that, the attention-based structure outperforms the other two networks, when a sufficiently large number of training examples is available and a relatively large embedding length is chosen. We stress that these findings are obtained under controlled, synthetic conditions with a small number of columns and it should therefore be regarded as an initial, narrowly-scoped investigation rather than a general characterization of ITP on real-world tabular data.

</details>


### [203] [Position: Privacy Is a Claim, Not a Property of Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01273)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，指出机器学习研究中合成数据的使用已从"明确的统计推断风险声明"悄然转变为"基于数据生成本身的外观属性"来推断隐私保护。作者通过实证分析发现，在许多隐私敏感场景下合成数据的使用缺乏对威胁模型、推断风险和可证伪隐私声明的明确阐述，导致隐私保证不明确、难以验证且分布不均，尤其对稀有和少数群体记录风险更高。文章主张将隐私视为明确、基于证据的科学主张，并建议ML会议采纳要求隐私相关断言具有清晰范围、可测试性和可争议性的规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的隐私声称存在根本性转变：从在明确假设下的残余推断风险声明，转变为一种从数据生成过程本身推断出的表面化属性。这一转变并非对既有隐私原则的误解，而是社区标准在"何为充分的隐私证据"上的隐性变化。这种隐性的标准转换可能导致隐私保证缺乏科学严谨性。

Method: 作者采用实证分析方法，对近期主要ML会议和期刊中发表的论文进行系统调查，分析这些研究中合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用方式，特别关注其是否明确阐述了威胁模型、推断风险和可证伪的隐私声明。

Result: 实证分析显示，大量论文在隐私敏感场景中使用合成数据，但没有明确说明威胁模型、推断风险或可证伪的隐私承诺。研究进一步揭示了隐私保证常处于隐性状态，难以验证，且风险分布不均衡——稀有和少数群体记录面临的暴露风险更高。

Conclusion: 作者主张应将隐私视为一种明确、基于证据的科学主张，并建议机器学习会议采纳相关规范，要求任何涉及隐私的断言必须被清晰界定范围、可被测试验证，且允许被质疑和争论，以恢复隐私声明的科学严谨性。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a common component of machine learning research. While widely adopted, its use in privacy-sensitive contexts has quietly shifted from a claim of residual inference risk under stated assumptions to an appearance-based property inferred from data generation itself. In this position paper, we argue that this shift reflects an implicit change in community standards for what counts as sufficient privacy evidence, rather than a misunderstanding of well-established privacy principles. Drawing on an empirical analysis of recent publications across major ML venues, we show that synthetic data is frequently used in privacy-sensitive settings without explicit articulation of threat models, inference risks, or falsifiable privacy claims. As a result, privacy assurance often remains implicit, difficult to verify, and unevenly distributed, with heightened exposure for rare and minority records. We argue for treating privacy as an explicit, evidence-based scientific claim and recommend that ML venues adopt norms requiring privacy-relevant assertions to be clearly scoped, testable, and contestable.

</details>


### [204] [The Constitutional Coverage Trilemma in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01275)
*Natalija Mitic,Soona Sedahmed A. O.,Mamadou Selly Ly,Moustapha Cisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对23个前沿大语言模型默认"宪法"（隐含价值排序）的审计与1,649名美国参与者的偏好配对权衡研究，发现：用户需求广泛覆盖五种价值（安全、有用、诚实、自主、公平），但供给侧宪法类型狭窄且偏移——23个模型仅覆盖约2%的需求空间；37%的用户在现有模型中没有对应宪法类型；且随版本迭代，自主性持续下降。解决方案非常稀疏：仅需两个顶点（诚实+自主）构成的菜单，其平均遗憾即优于全部23个模型的前沿，仅加三个顶点可将最差群体遗憾降低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统作为"宪法性机构"内嵌了安全、有用、诚实、自主与公平之间的隐含排序，但供需是否匹配从未被系统检验。作者提出核心问题：前沿AI的宪法类型供给是否能覆盖人类多样化的价值需求？

Method: 采用双管齐下的实证方法：(1) 对23个前沿LLM原型机的出厂默认宪法进行释义控制的审计；(2) 用同一工具对1,649名美国参与者进行两两权衡研究。此外构建了预算化多元主义三难困境形式化模型，并进行距离加权福利与降级路由的稳健性检验。

Result: 三个核心事实：(1) 需求广泛——五种价值均有群体支持，最大群体不足三分之一；(2) 供给狭窄且偏移——23个原型机仅覆盖约2%的需求凸包（完全审计精度下0.10%），无任何模型将有用或自主排在首位（37%用户"无家可归"），且6个模型家族中自主性在5/6中下降、公平性在5/6中上升、安全性在4/6中上升，版本内趋势呈单调性；(3) 修复极稀疏——2顶点菜单优于全23模型前沿47%（平均遗憾），增加3个顶点可将最差群体遗憾降低64%。

Conclusion: 前沿AI的宪法供给与人类价值需求存在系统性错配，且偏移方向恶化：离价值已覆盖不足的维度更远。但这并非无解——极少量精心选择的宪法顶点即可大幅改善福利底线；作者将发现形式化为预算化多元主义三难困境并证明其约束区域在现实中成立。
