This survey offers a comprehensive overview of AI voice generation and detection methods, encompassing both the technical foundations and the latest state-of-the-art advances. This study also identifies key open challenges, benchmark resources and future directions to make this survey useful for future researchers.

</details>


### [343] [Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction (NeuSOGA): From Observations to Symbolic Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01408)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuSOGA（神经符号几何抽象）框架，将几何观测逐步转化为拓扑抽象、几何抽象，最终形成符号数学表示，实现从感知到符号的可解释通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过大规模统计学习获得强大感知能力，但知识编码在难以检查或操作的潜在参数中，缺乏可解释性和可编辑性。受神经符号AI和人类抽象理论启发，本文旨在解决从观测到显式符号表示转换这一基础挑战。

Method: NeuSOGA框架包含四个核心组件：（1）基于欧几里得距离变换的拓扑引导结构发现；（2）基于Segment Anything的基础模型感知；（3）自适应多尺度几何抽象；（4）通过隐式面积样条进行符号综合。最终生成支持任意阶平滑性、加性组合和闭式求值的解析隐式模型。

Result: 在ModelNet40点云、任意视角投影和分割光学观测上的实验表明，NeuSOGA能将多样观测转换为紧凑符号表示，同时跨传感模态和视角保留基本几何与拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 与神经潜在编码不同，NeuSOGA生成的表示具有可解释性、可编辑性和数学显式性，为从观测到符号提供了可解释可解释的通路。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence is the transformation of observations into explicit symbolic representations suitable for abstraction, interpretation, and reasoning. While modern AI systems achieve remarkable perceptual capabilities through large-scale statistical learning, the resulting knowledge is typically encoded within latent parameters that are difficult to inspect or manipulate analytically. Inspired by Neuro-Symbolic AI and theories of human abstraction, this paper investigates the formation of symbolic mathematical representations from geometric observations.
  We propose NeuSOGA (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction), a framework that progressively transforms observations into topological abstractions, geometric abstractions, and ultimately symbolic mathematical representations. The architecture combines topology-guided structural discovery using Euclidean Distance Transforms, foundation-model perception using Segment Anything, adaptive multi-scale geometric abstraction, and symbolic synthesis through Implicit Area Splines.
  The resulting representation is an analytical implicit model supporting arbitrary-order smoothness, additive composition, and closed-form evaluation. Unlike neural latent encodings, the generated representation remains interpretable, editable, and mathematically explicit. Experiments on ModelNet40 point clouds, arbitrary-view projections, and segmented optical observations demonstrate that NeuSOGA transforms diverse observations into compact symbolic representations while preserving essential geometric and topological structure across sensing modalities and viewing directions.
  NeuSOGA provides an interpretable and explainable pathway from observation to symbol and establishes

</details>


### [344] [EdiTikZ: Scientific Figure Editing from Revision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01409)
*Christian Greisinger,Zhixue Zhao,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DaEdiTikZ，首个大规模基于自然科研修订轨迹的科学图表编辑数据集，通过挖掘arXiv、GitHub和TeX SE中的391K对TikZ编辑对并推断781K条编辑指令构建。同时提出DaEdiTikZ-Bench基准，训练了紧凑的EdiTikZ模型（4B/9B），通过联合学习重建与编辑及强化学习优化，在自动和人工评估中超越多个强基线，9B模型接近或超越GPT-5.6-Sol。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表编辑是重要但未被充分探索的任务。现有方法依赖昂贵的专有代理系统，或仅关注评估，或使用合成编辑构造训练数据。作者希望利用自然发生的科研修订轨迹作为可扩展监督来源，避免上述局限。

Method: 1) 从arXiv、GitHub和TeX SE中挖掘391K对plausible TikZ编辑对，推断781K条有向编辑指令（基于VLM，条件为渲染图和TikZ代码）；2) 构建DaEdiTikZ-Bench人工精炼基准（790个实例）；3) 训练Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ模型（4B和9B），联合学习重建和编辑，再用互补奖励（渲染保真度和编辑应用）进行强化学习。

Result: 自动评估中9B模型超越所有测试基线；人工评估（9名标注者、4320条评分）中超越GPT-5.6-Sol，与Gemini-3.1-Pro持平。在严重分布外偏移下，在2K训练序列长度范围内仍与GPT-5.6-Sol具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 利用自然科研修订轨迹作为监督来源是训练科学图表编辑模型的可扩展有效途径。紧凑的开源模型可以达到甚至超越大型专有模型的表现，且模型和数据集将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in generating scientific figures from text or images. However, producing publication-ready figures requires iterative refinement, making scientific figure editing an important yet largely unexplored task. Existing approaches rely on costly proprietary agentic systems, focus primarily on evaluation, or construct training supervision from synthetically generated edits. Instead, we leverage naturally occurring scientific revision and development trajectories as a scalable source of supervision. To this end, we introduce DaEdiTikZ, the first large-scale dataset of revision-derived scientific figure edits, constructed by mining 391K plausible TikZ edit pairs from arXiv, GitHub, and TeX SE and inferring 781K directed edit instructions with a VLM conditioned on rendered figures and TikZ code. We further introduce DaEdiTikZ-Bench, a human-refined benchmark with 790 instances, and train two compact Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ models (4B and 9B) by jointly learning reconstruction and editing, followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with complementary rewards for rendered fidelity and edit application. Automatic evaluation places our 9B model above all tested baselines, while human evaluation with 9 annotators and 4,320 ratings places it above GPT-5.6-Sol and on par with Gemini-3.1-Pro. Under severe out-of-distribution shifts, it remains competitive with GPT-5.6-Sol near its 2K training sequence-length regime. Models and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [345] [Parsing the Stream: A Live Trace Model for Long-Horizon Agents and Their Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01466)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"实时轨迹折叠"模型：将代理运行的追加式事件账本增量折叠为类型化的运行状态，并编译为面向不同消费者（人类观察者与代理自身）的视图。实验表明，该方案在观察者端大幅降低token消耗（约14-15倍）和成本（5-7倍）且准确性更高；在代理端解决了长程顺序依赖任务中全上下文提示失效的问题。作者还从失败中推导出11条轨迹折叠候选需求，并识别了该方法失效的任务边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程代理的运行轨迹规模会超出两类消费者的处理能力：人类观察者难以监视完整轨迹，而代理自身的有限上下文也难以容纳全部轨迹。需要一个结构化的轨迹表示方法，使两类消费者都能高效利用运行信息。

Method: 提出"实时轨迹模型"：采用追加式事件账本（append-only event ledger），将其增量折叠（fold）为类型化运行状态，并据此编译成面向不同消费者的视图。在观察者侧用LLM作为读者代理进行评估；在代理侧与全上下文提示、提示级草稿本等方法对比，并进行两臂分解分析。

Result: 观察者侧：编译视图相比原始轨迹的单次预算封顶读取，token减少约14-15倍，成本降低5-7倍，准确率更高（0.85-0.87 vs 0.48）。代理侧：在120链路顺序依赖任务中，维护运行统计量的折叠机制成功30/30，而全上下文提示仅8/30。提示级草稿本能以更低成本匹配折叠的准确率，但折叠的独特价值在于确定性的可审计性以及从同一状态服务观察者。

Conclusion: 轨迹折叠方法在长程代理场景下对两类消费者均有显著价值，其主要优势来自确定性聚合与紧凑表示；但其相对优势会随任务特性变化而消失（存在有序敏感的任务族作为边界）。研究发布了代码、基准、可再生的合成语料库及全部工作台轨迹。

Abstract: A long-horizon agent's trace outgrows both of its consumers: the human observer monitoring the run, and the agent itself, whose bounded context the trace must be folded back into. We present a live trace model, an append-only event ledger folded incrementally into typed run state and compiled into per-consumer views, and evaluate it for both consumers against deterministic ground truth. For the observer side, evaluated with an LLM reader as proxy, the compiled view answers monitoring questions using approximately 14x and 15x fewer input tokens (by reader) and at 5-7x lower cost than a budget-capped single-call reading of the raw trace, with higher accuracy (0.85-0.87 versus 0.48). Because the questions were co-designed with the view schema, we treat the token and cost reduction, conditional on schema coverage, as the transferable result. For the agent, on 120-link sequential-dependency tasks, mechanisms that maintain the task's running statistic in per-step state succeed where full-context prompting fails (30/30 versus 8/30 under a clean protocol, n=30, labeled descriptive owing to benchmark-system co-development); a prompt-level scratchpad matches the fold's accuracy at lower cost, and a two-arm decomposition attributes the fold's accuracy to its deterministic aggregate and its cost advantage to its compactness. The fold's remaining value over cheaper alternatives is deterministic auditability and serving the observer from the same state. We derive eleven candidate requirements for trace folding from observed failures and delimit them with an order-sensitive task family on which the fold ceases to help. Code, benchmarks, a regenerable synthetic corpus, and all workbench traces are released.

</details>


### [346] [Harness-of-Harness: Multi-Day Autonomous Software Development with Continual Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01481)
*Haoyang Yan,Min-le Su,Hangfan Zhang,Zhanhao Li,Chen Zhang,Shao Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-of-Harness（HoH）框架，旨在使基于LLM的编码智能体在无需人工干预的情况下，从高层需求持续迭代地开发出完整、功能可用的软件系统，在多个基准上平均相对提升52.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件开发中，现有编码智能体（基于LLM）缺乏持续改进能力，难以在无人工干预下将高层需求转化为完整可用的软件系统，缺少有效的迭代优化机制。

Method: HoH框架作用于现有编码智能体harness之上，将其执行组织为迭代的"规划-编码-测试"循环。核心机制包括：平衡修复与能力增长、将开发细分为小且可验证的增量、将实现期测试与独立评估分离、约束可验证输出而非规定智能体工作流；逐步暴露交付物、角色专用工具和技能，鼓励复用而非重新创建，并维护版本化项目历史。

Result: 在GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE和ProgramBench三个基准上，使用三组harness-模型对（Codex与GPT-5.5、OpenCode与DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Pi与MiniMax-M3），HoH一致优于各独立harness，三次迭代后平均相对提升52.25%，最高相对提升82.86%。超过70次迭代的多日部署中，HoH自主开发出一款第一人称射击游戏，具备连贯剧情、完整核心机制、可游玩体验、精美视觉与集成音频。

Conclusion: HoH通过设计合理的迭代循环和关键策略约束，能够显著增强现有编码智能体在自主软件开发中的持续改进能力，验证了该方法在长时间、多轮迭代场景下的有效性，并发布开源代码与项目页面。

Abstract: This paper studies autonomous software development, in which LLM-based coding agents transform high-level requirements into complete, functional, and usable software systems without human intervention. We introduce Harness-of-Harness (HoH), a framework that enables coding agents to continually improve software during autonomous development. HoH operates on existing coding-agent harnesses, and organizes their executions into iterative planning-coding-testing loops. To sustain improvement across loops, HoH balances repair with capability growth, scopes development into small and verifiable increments, separates implementation-time testing from independent evaluation, and constrains verifiable outputs rather than prescribing agent workflows. It progressively exposes deliverables, role-specific tools, and skills, encourages reuse rather than recreation, and maintains versioned project histories. On GameCraft-Bench, FrontierSWE, and ProgramBench, three harness-model pairs (Codex with GPT-5.5, OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Pi with MiniMax-M3), HoH consistently outperforms the corresponding standalone harnesses, achieving an average relative gain of 52.25 percent and a maximum gain of 82.86 percent after three iterations. In a multi-day deployment with more than 70 iterations, HoH autonomously develops a first-person-shooter game, featuring a coherent storyline, fully implemented core mechanics, human-playable experience, polished visuals and integrated audio. Github: https://github.com/Flesymeb/HarnessOfHarness Project Page: https://flesymeb.github.io/HarnessOfHarness/

</details>


### [347] [When Guardrails Look Effective: Construct Validity Failures in LLM Agent Commerce Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01519)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了交互式模拟市场中使用语言模型代理评估政策时面临的构建效度风险。通过一个多轮买方-卖方酒店交易测试平台，发现初始报告的市场护栏福利收益（+87.4, +35.0, +28.8）在控制协议变量后大幅改变（+7.2, -13.9, +23.8），且生成随机性占结果变异的49.9%。研究提出构建效度契约（区分激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性和福利核算），原始估计在协议隔离下判定为INVALID，受控研究在激励效度和随机稳定性下仍为INCONCLUSIVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型代理越来越多地被用于模拟市场中的政策评估，但这些模拟的输出看起来具有经济意义（价格、利润、福利），却可能并未实际体现声称的行为。研究者希望审计并识别这种构建效度风险，避免基于不可靠模拟做出实质性政策判断。

Method: 构建了一个可配置的多轮买方-卖方酒店交易测试平台，通过系统性地控制与操作以下因素来审计：提供模式（schema）、买家选择程序、生成次数、卖方激励提示，并使用脚本化阳性对照进行验证。提出了一个构建效度契约，包含激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性和福利核算四个维度，对政策主张输出INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE判定。

Result: 初始报告在Qwen2.5 1.5B-14B模型阶梯上声称的护栏福利收益（+87.4, +35.0, +28.8）在保持协议一致后变为+7.2, -13.9, +23.8；14B单次生成效应从平均+229降至三次生成后的+37.6（95%置信区间含零），生成残差占变异49.9%。卖方激励检查呈非单调性：施加更大利润压力反而产出更少利润。利润最大化卖方已实现一阶最优福利，护栏主要起再分配和降低福利的作用。

Conclusion: 原估算在协议隔离维度下无效（INVALID），受控研究在激励效度和随机稳定性维度下仍不确定（INCONCLUSIVE）。该案例并不证明护栏无效，而是表明在模拟代理和协议通过构建效度检查之前，其表观价值是未被识别的。研究贡献在于提供了一个可复用的构建效度契约框架。

Abstract: Interactive simulations increasingly evaluate policies in markets populated by language-model agents. Their outputs can look economic---prices, profits, consumer surplus, and welfare---without instantiating the behavior named in the claim. We audit this risk in a multi-turn buyer--seller testbed for configurable hotel transactions. An initial implementation reported welfare gains from two marketplace guardrails of +87.4, +35.0, and +28.8 across a Qwen2.5 1.5B--14B ladder. It also gave guarded and unguarded agents different offer schemas and choice procedures. Holding the schema and buyer chooser fixed changes the paired contrasts to +7.2, -13.9, and +23.8. The four largest 14B single-generation effects averaged +229; after three generations per profile-condition, they averaged +37.6 (95% bootstrap interval [-34.2, 109.3]), while generation residuals account for 49.9% of variation in this post-hoc probe. A seller-incentive check is non-monotone: increasing profit pressure produces less profit than the default seller prompt. Scripted positive controls show why this matters. A profit-maximizing seller already attains first-best welfare, so guardrails mostly redistribute and reduce welfare; they create welfare only when the seller is explicitly programmed to force inefficient bundles. We contribute a construct-validity contract separating incentive validity, protocol isolation, stochastic stability, and welfare accounting, and returning INVALID or INCONCLUSIVE before substantive policy claims. In our case, the original estimate is INVALID under protocol isolation, while the controlled study remains INCONCLUSIVE under incentive validity and stochastic stability. The case does not show that guardrails are ineffective; it shows their apparent value is unidentified until the simulated agents and protocol pass these checks.

</details>


### [348] [EvoSCM: Scientific Belief Revision Through Causal Model Evolution and Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01526)
*Qing Zhao,Haowei Li,Weijian Deng,Pengxu Wei,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSCM，为科学智能体配备显式结构因果模型（SCM），并通过假设种群演化、判别性干预设计和可证伪预测等机制，使智能体在不断收集实验证据的过程中迭代修正对环境的因果信念，从而提升科学发现的准确性和实验效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的科学智能体通常以自由文本形式表达科学假设，导致信念隐含、难以检验或修正。作者认为科学智能体不仅需要学会推理，更需明确"相信什么"，因此需要显式可演化的因果表示。

Method: EvoSCM维护一个竞争的SCM假设种群，每个假设编码环境的一种候选因果解释。每轮实验循环中：(1)从累积证据中溯因潜在机制；(2)设计判别性干预实验；(3)生成可证伪预测并实际验证；(4)将预测与观测的差异归纳蒸馏为修正规则以更新因果结构及机制；(5)演绎验证修订后的种群的一致性，指导下一轮实验。

Result: 在DiscoverPhysics基准（要求智能体通过实验揭示非标准物理世界的隐藏动力学）上，EvoSCM相较于基线方法持续提升了科学发现性能，生成的解释和预测更准确，且对实验交互的利用更高效。

Conclusion: 将科学假设表示为可演化的显式因果模型能够显著提升LLM智能体的科学发现能力，为智能体提供了可检验、可修订的信念框架，超越了自由文本假设的局限。

Abstract: Scientific agents must learn not only how to reason, but also what to believe. However, existing LLM agents typically express scientific hypotheses in free-form text, leaving their beliefs implicit and difficult to test or revise. We introduce EvoSCM, which equips scientific agents with explicit structural causal models that evolve as new experimental evidence is collected. EvoSCM maintains a population of competing SCM hypotheses, each encoding a candidate causal explanation of the environment, and evolves them through a closed discovery loop. In each round, the agent abduces latent mechanisms from accumulated evidence, designs discriminative interventions, and commits to falsifiable predictions that it tests through experimentation. Discrepancies between prediction and observation are inductively distilled into correction rules that revise the causal structures and mechanisms of each hypothesis, and the agent then deductively validates the revised population against accumulated evidence and structural consistency to guide the next round. We evaluate EvoSCM on DiscoverPhysics, a benchmark requiring agents to uncover the hidden dynamics of noncanonical physical worlds through experimentation. EvoSCM consistently improves scientific discovery over baselines, yielding more accurate explanations and predictions while making more effective use of experimental interactions.

</details>


### [349] [Can LLMs Discover Scientific Laws in Real and Parallel Worlds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01552)
*Yiming Huang,Ziche Liu,Zhuohang Wu,Yiqian Wang,Junxia Cui,Xinkai Zou,Linjun Mao,Nan Huang,Naicheng Yu,Kaijie Zhu,Yue Ma,Kun Zhou,Letian Peng,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCILAWS-BENCH基准测试，用于评估LLM在科学定律发现方面的能力，该基准基于381篇论文构建了118个问题、291个候选定律和约800万真实数据点，覆盖六个科学学科，并设计了REAL和PARALLEL两种互补任务设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学定律发现的评估方法存在两个问题：一是通过合成环境简化了发现过程，二是直接复用已发表的目标可能已被LLM在训练中见过，导致"记忆"而非真正"发现"。

Method: 从已发表研究和真实科学数据出发构建基准，涵盖118个问题、291个候选定律及约800万数据点；每个问题设置两种场景：SCILAWS-REAL（基于固定真实观测提出定律，评估预测拟合与科学有效性）和SCILAWS-PARALLEL（主动查询残差校准世界并恢复依据已发表形式综合出的隐藏定律）。

Result: 预测拟合度与科学有效性可能出现偏离；记忆效应会影响模型是复现已发表公式还是超越它们；best-of-N研究表明存在选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个基于文献的基准和新的实证视角，用于评估AI在科学发现方面的能力。

Abstract: Scientific equation discovery has long been central to scientific progress, proceeding through iterative cycles of hypothesis generation, observational testing, and refinement under scientific constraints. As LLM capabilities advance and their role in AI for Science expands, it remains an open problem whether they can genuinely discover scientific laws and how this ability should be evaluated. Existing evaluations, however, often either simplify discovery through synthetic settings or reuse published targets that may already be familiar to LLMs. We therefore introduce SCILAWS-BENCH, a benchmark for scientific law discovery built from published research and real scientific data. It comprises 118 problems drawn from 381 scientific papers, covering 291 candidate laws and roughly 8M real data points across six scientific disciplines. Each problem is instantiated in two complementary settings: (1) SCILAWS-REAL asks models to propose laws from fixed real observations and evaluates held-out predictive fit and scientific validity derived from the source literature, and (2) SCILAWS-PARALLEL asks models to actively query residual-calibrated worlds and recover synthesized hidden laws derived from published forms. This two-setting task design preserves each problem's scientific context while separately evaluating fixed-record law discovery and active recovery of a newly synthesized hidden law. We find that predictive fit can diverge from scientific validity, memorization shapes whether models reproduce or move beyond published formulas, and our best-of-N study reveals a selection bottleneck. Our work provides a paper-grounded benchmark and new empirical perspectives for evaluating AI for scientific discovery. Project page: https://yiyihum.github.io/SciLaws-Bench

</details>


### [350] [Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy: Learning Autonomous Policies from Imperfect VLM Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01567)
*Matteo Merler,Giovanni Bonetta,Davide Zago,Rossella Cancelliere,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，在强化学习训练过程中，仅在学习者不确定时选择性查询VLM教师，并通过环境优势加权蒸馏，将VLM的临时指导内化到轻量级RL策略中，实现部署时无需VLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM直接作为策略使用成本高昂且脆弱：每步都需要查询、无法从环境交互中改进、会重复系统性错误。需要一个能利用VLM先验知识但克服其缺陷的框架。

Method: SAGE（Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy）框架：(1) 仅在学习者不确定性高时查询VLM教师；(2) 训练期间执行教师建议的动作；(3) 利用基于环境回报的优势函数对教师动作蒸馏进行加权，而非平等对待所有建议，从而处理VLM建议不可靠的问题。

Result: 在稀疏奖励的视觉推理和导航任务中，SAGE学到的策略在评估时无需VLM指导即可行动，且在多个环境中优于无引导的RL，某些场景下甚至超过VLM教师本身。选择性引导在VLM能帮助发现高奖励轨迹时最有效，VLM调用次数也大幅减少。

Conclusion: VLM不必作为固定策略使用，可以作为临时的、不完美的指导来源，其价值通过环境交互被测试和内化，从而转化为廉价的自主策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide useful priors for interactive decision-making, but using them directly as policies is expensive and brittle: they must be queried at every step, do not improve from environment interaction, and can repeat systematic errors. We study how to learn a cheap autonomous policy from an online, expensive, and imperfect but informative VLM teacher. We propose SAGE (Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy), a framework that queries a VLM only when the learner is uncertain, executes the suggested action during training, and distills guidance into a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy. Because VLM advice is not always reliable, SAGE can weight teacher-action distillation using environment-derived advantages rather than treating all suggestions as equally useful. Across sparse-reward visual reasoning and navigation tasks, SAGE learns policies that act without VLM guidance at evaluation time and improves over unguided RL in several environments, including settings where the learned policy exceeds its VLM teacher. The results show that selective guidance is most beneficial when the VLM can help the agent discover high-reward trajectories, and less useful when unguided exploration already succeeds or teacher actions do not lead to informative experience. SAGE also reduces VLM usage by prompting the teacher only on a fraction of training steps and requiring no VLM calls at deployment. Overall, our results suggest that VLMs don't need to be used as fixed policies to be useful; they can instead act as temporary, imperfect sources of guidance whose value is tested and internalized through interaction.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [351] [Inverse kinematic solution for generic 3R positional robots using Conformal Geometric Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00311)
*Abhilash Nayak,Durgesh Haribhau Salunkhe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文利用共形几何代数（CGA）研究通用3R机器人的逆运动学问题，将问题重构为两个圆的交点，直接得到关于θ₂的一元多项式，无需通过代数消元来消除θ₁和θ₃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的3R机器人逆运动学求解主要依赖代数方法，需要求解特定的方程组；已有的几何解释将解表征为一对圆锥曲线的交点，但仅限于关节空间域，缺乏更深层的几何洞察。

Method: 利用共形几何代数（CGA）的理论优势，将3R机器人的逆运动学模型（IKM）重新表述为两个圆的交点问题，直接从几何框架中导出关于θ₂的一元多项式，无需显式消去θ₁和θ₃。

Result: 该方法成功获得了一个关于θ₂的一元多项式解，避免了繁琐的代数消元过程，并通过将问题简化为两个基本几何元素（圆）的交点，获得了更清晰的运动学性质洞察。

Conclusion: 通过CGA的几何框架，3R机器人逆运动学问题获得了更为直观和简洁的求解路径，为后续更复杂的机器人运动学研究提供了新的代数几何视角。

Abstract: The inverse kinematics of generic 3R robots has been investigated through multiple approaches, mainly algebraic methods involving the solution of certain equation sets. Previous geometric interpretations of the solution, characterized as the intersection of a pair of conics have been confined to the joint-space domain. In this article, we study the Inverse Kinematic Model (IKM) of 3R robots, using the advantages of Conformal Geometric Algebra (CGA) to provide further insights on its kinematic properties. Our approach directly yields a univariate polynomial in terms of theta_2 without the need to eliminate theta_1 and theta_3 by reframing the problem as the intersection of two circles, which are fundamental elements within this algebraic framework.

</details>


### [352] [Geometric analysis of generic 3R robots, and necessary and sufficient conditions for a class of orthogonal robots to have four IKS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00316)
*Durgesh Haribhau Salunkhe,Abhilash Nayak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文结合代数方法与几何方法（基于共形几何代数的逆运动学模型），对通用3R机器人的运动学进行分析，提出一个研究3R机器人具有四种逆解（IKS）条件的通用框架，并给出了正交类机器人的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往代数方法对3R机器人运动学分析虽结果确凿，但仅限于特殊类别或结构简化；几何方法虽可扩展到通用机器人并提供直观理解，但缺乏系统性结论。作者希望结合两者优势，对通用3R机器人进行更全面的运动学分析。

Method: 采用基于共形几何代数（CGA）的逆运动学模型作为基础，结合代数与几何方法各自的优势，提出一个通用框架来研究3R机器人具有四个逆运动学解（IKS）的条件，并分析IKS在机器人工作空间中的分布情况。

Result: 提出了一个通用框架用于研究3R机器人具有四个逆运动学解的条件，能够分析IKS在工作空间中的分布；针对正交类机器人给出了其具有四个IKS的必要充分条件。

Conclusion: 通过融合代数与几何方法的优势，所提出的框架成功将3R机器人的逆运动学解分析推广到通用情形，并量化了正交类机器人的解分布条件，为机器人运动学分析提供了新的途径。

Abstract: The kinematic analysis of a generic 3R robot has been investigated with multiple approaches in the past. The algebraic approaches have established concrete results but are unfortunately limited to special classes or architectural simplifications. Geometric approaches on the other hand have extended the analysis to generic robots while also providing an intuitive understanding of their kinematic properties. We use the best of both approaches to present the kinematic analysis of a generic 3R robot, using the inverse kinematic model inherited from a method based on conformal geometric algebra. The paper discusses a generic framework to study the conditions for a 3R robot to have four inverse kinematic solutions (IKS) and allows to study the distribution of IKS as seen in workspace. The necessary and sufficient conditions for a class of orthogonal robots are presented using the proposed approach.

</details>


### [353] [Scene Graph-based Driving Scenario Extraction for Automotive Egocentric Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00333)
*Stefan Ramdhan,Kyanna Dagenais,Vera Pantelic,Victor Bandur,Mark Lawford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从无标签的真实世界传感器数据流中提取驾驶场景的方法，利用场景图和线性时序逻辑（LTL）来形式化指定并自动提取感兴趣的驾驶场景，在Argoverse 2数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶系统开发中，从无标签的真实世界传感器数据流中提取场景是关键但具有挑战性的任务。自动从大规模数据集中筛选关键场景，能够实现基于场景的覆盖率分析，但目前缺乏有效的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 首先处理以自我为中心的传感器数据和HD地图，生成表示驾驶场景的场景图序列；然后使用LTL形式化指定感兴趣的驾驶场景；最后通过现成的模型检查器评估LTL公式与场景图序列，提取所有场景实例。

Result: 在Argoverse 2数据集的850条15秒真实驾驶日志以及多段行车记录仪视频上进行评估，通过与基于轨道标注和HD地图的规则基准对比，证明了该方法在场景提取和查询方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法可同时适用于仿真和真实世界数据集，为自动驾驶场景提取提供了一种形式化、可查询且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Extracting scenarios from unlabelled real-world sensor data streams is a critical but challenging task in the development process of automated driving systems (ADS). Automatically sifting through large datasets to spatially and temporally locate critical scenarios can enable scenario-based coverage analysis of ADS datasets. In this paper, we present a method for extracting scenarios from egocentric datasets using scene graphs and Linear Temporal Logic (LTL). We first process egocentric sensor data and HD maps to generate a sequence of scene graphs representing a driving scenario. Next, we use LTL to formally specify driving scenarios of interest, then extract all instances of the scenarios from the dataset using an off-the-shelf model checker, which evaluates the LTL formula against the sequence of scene graphs. Our approach can be used on both simulated and real world datasets. We evaluate the method on the training and validation datasets from Argoverse 2 consisting of 850 15-second real-world driving logs, and several videos of dashcam footage. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for extracting and querying scenarios by evaluating against a rule-based benchmark based on track annotations and HD maps.

</details>


### [354] [Risk-Aware Decision-Making for Autonomous Overtaking: A World Model-Based Mixture-of-Experts Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00385)
*Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Jinchang Xu,Jiawei Wang,Yushu Qiu,Chen Lv,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WM-RMoE框架，通过世界模型进行并行多步轨迹展开以提升安全评估从动作级到轨迹级的跨越，并结合层级门控机制动态协调多尺度专家模块以及高斯混合模型保留多模态机动分支，显著提升自动驾驶高速超车场景的安全合规性、决策稳定性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准安全强化学习方法依赖隐式基于价值的安全估计而非显式动力学建模，难以在多步时域内准确捕捉复杂风险传播。现有方法往往产生局部安全但长期存在重大潜在风险的驾驶行为，亟需一种能进行前瞻性轨迹级风险评估的决策框架。

Method: 首先构建学习型潜动力学模型支持并行多步轨迹展开，通过累积风险评估将安全评估从动作级提升至轨迹级；其次设计层级门控机制，动态协调长时域、短时域和基于规则的专家模块，增强不同交互强度下的鲁棒性；最后集成高斯混合模型保留多模态机动分支，缓解行为模式平均化问题。

Result: 实验表明WM-RMoE在安全合规性、决策稳定性和泛化能力上显著优于代表性基线方法。此外，得益于风险感知公式化设计，该框架能在不同交通密度下生成具有前瞻性和语义上截然不同的超车机动。

Conclusion: 通过显式潜动力学建模和风险感知的多专家协同机制，WM-RMoE为自动驾驶高速超车提供了同时具备前瞻性风险评估与多模态决策能力的有效解决方案，验证了轨迹级安全评估相对传统动作级评估的优越性。

Abstract: Autonomous highway overtaking demands foresighted decision-making to handle complex interactions, stochastic traffic evolution, and temporal risk accumulation. However, standard safe reinforcement learning approaches typically rely on implicit value-based risk estimations rather than explicit dynamics modeling, thereby struggling to accurately capture complex risk propagation over multi-step horizons. This limitation frequently results in behaviors that are locally safe but induce substantial latent risks in the long term. To address this, a World Model-based Risk-aware Mixture-of-Experts (WM-RMoE) framework is proposed. First, a learned latent dynamics model facilitates parallel multi-step rollouts, elevating safety assessment from the action level to the trajectory level via cumulative risk evaluation. Second, to enhance robustness under varying interaction intensities, a hierarchical gating mechanism dynamically coordinates experts across long-horizon, short-horizon, and rule-based safety modules. Furthermore, a Gaussian Mixture Model is integrated to preserve multimodal maneuvering branches, thereby mitigating the issue of behavioral mode averaging. Experimental results demonstrate that WM-RMoE significantly outperforms representative baselines in terms of safety compliance, decision stability, and generalization capability. Furthermore, benefiting from the risk-aware formulation, the proposed framework uniquely exhibits the ability to generate foresighted and semantically distinct overtaking maneuvers across diverse traffic densities.

</details>


### [355] [Beyond Object Selection:Markerless Gaze-based Robot Placement at Arbitrary Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00478)
*Yuzhi Lai,William Marx,Shenghai Yuan,Peizheng Li,Zhuoyu Ran,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于凝视的辅助操作中头显与机器人之间的跨设备对齐问题，从任务导向视角提出了无标记交互框架、跨设备数据集和凝视-表面交会误差（GSIE）指标，并通过实验证明传统位姿度量表现良好的对齐方法在任务级精度上并不总是最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝视辅助操作通常支持物体选择，但任意位置的放置需要头显与机器人之间的精确空间对齐。然而，位姿精度并不必然转化为任务精度，因为平移和旋转误差会共同影响变换后的凝视射线，且可能相互补偿。因此需要从任务导向的角度研究跨设备对齐。

Method: 提出一个无标记交互框架和专用的跨设备数据集；提出基于图的参考选择（Graph-based Reference Selection）来解决稀疏机器人参考问题；在统一协议下开发和基准测试多个任务特定的对齐流程；引入凝视-表面交会误差（GSIE）直接度量凝视指定目标的空间误差。

Result: 实验表明，按传统位姿度量排名较高的对齐方法在GSIE上并不总是最优，证明了在任务层面评估凝视操控的重要性。

Conclusion: 凝视操控的对齐评估应从任务级而非传统位姿级角度进行，GSIE为更真实地反映凝视操控任务效果提供了有效评估手段。

Abstract: Gaze-based assistive manipulation typically supports object selection, while arbitrary-position placement requires accurate spatial alignment between the headset and robot. However, for gaze-based manipulation, pose accuracy does not necessarily translate into task accuracy: translational and rotational errors jointly affect the transformed gaze ray and may compensate for each other. To study cross-device alignment from this task-oriented perspective, we present a markerless interaction framework and a dedicated cross-device dataset. We propose Graph-based Reference Selection to address sparse robot references. We further develop and benchmark multiple task-specific alignment pipelines under a unified protocol. Specifically, we introduce Gaze--Surface Intersection Error (GSIE), which directly measures the spatial error of the gaze-specified target. Experiments show that alignment methods ranked highly by conventional pose metrics are not always optimal in GSIE, demonstrating the importance of evaluating gaze-based manipulation at the task level.

</details>


### [356] [Exploring Nonlinear Body Oscillations for Natural Quadruped Gaits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00539)
*Annika Schmidt,Davide Calzolari,Florian Loeffl,Arne Sachtler,Daniel Seidel,Milan Herrmann,Robert Burger,Thomas Gumpert,Antonin Raffin,Tristan Ehlert,Maximilian Pries,David Wandinger,Florian Schmidt,Manuel Keppler,Jinoh Lee,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用非线性共振来生成多步态运动的框架，通过在高顺应性的四足机器人eBert上识别六种非线性法向模态（NNMs），使其能够利用身体力学（重力、惯性、弹性）自然涌现不同速度的步态，无需复杂的主动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数四足机器人并非专门设计来利用机械共振，因为非线性动力学复杂性高，需要专用的运动控制器。而动物通过调节姿态和肌肉刚度，利用其具身智能在不同速度下实现有效步态，这种差距促使研究者探索利用机械共振的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个概念验证框架，使机器人非线性动力学在设计过程中变得可预测。开发新的理论工具在高度顺应的四足机器人eBert上识别六种非线性法向模态（NNMs），并通过黑盒优化确定步长，使每个NNM自然发展成不同速度的独特步态。

Result: 仿真中验证了六种NNM的存在及其向不同步态的演化，每种NNM表现出不同速度；实验结果还表明这些步态在很大程度上迁移到了实际机器人硬件上，eBert能够利用其力学特性生成任务特定的运动。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了利用非线性共振让多步态运动从机器人自身的力学特性中涌现是可行的，为设计新一代敏捷高效的、利用具身智能的机器人提供了基础。

Abstract: Animals' body morphology shapes the gait patterns they can perform, where mechanical resonance reduces the need for active control. By tuning posture and muscle stiffness, they leverage their embodied intelligence to achieve effective gaits for different speeds. In contrast, most quadruped robots are not specifically designed to exploit mechanical resonance due to the complexity of nonlinear dynamics and require dedicated locomotion controllers. To provide an alternative, we present a proof of concept framework making the nonlinear dynamics of a robot predictable in the design process and show how this knowledge can be leveraged such that multi-gait locomotion can emerge from nonlinear resonances, shaped by gravity, inertia, and elasticity. We present the highly compliant quadruped robot eBert, on which we identify six nonlinear normal modes (NNMs) using our new theoretical tools and validate their existence in simulation and hardware. With black-box optimization to determine step length, simulations show how each NNM naturally develops into a distinct gait, manifesting different speeds, which also largely transfers to the robotic hardware. Our experiments show that eBert can exploit its mechanics to generate task-specific movements which may serve as foundation for designing a new generation of agile and efficient robots leveraging embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [357] [Potential-Guided Particle Steering for Negation-Constrained Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00555)
*Geonho Kim,SooGon Kim,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个推理时框架，用于解决语言驱动的灵巧抓取中"指令指明禁抓区域"的任务。该方法结合顺序蒙特卡洛与无分类器引导，无需否定类训练样本即可在采样时引导规避禁区。在构建的NegGrasp基准上，将违规率从57.9%降至17.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言驱动灵巧抓取模型（如DextER）在指令明确"抓哪里"时表现良好，但当指令同时指定"不要抓哪里"（如"抓把手但避开主体"）时系统性地失败。现有训练语料几乎不含规避类指令，且为每种可能的约束收集样例不切实际。此外，模型可能将禁忌部位误解为另一抓取目标而非应规避区域。

Method: 提出一个无需否定专项训练样本的推理时框架：将顺序蒙特卡洛（SMC）与无分类器引导（CFG）相结合，引导采样朝指定部位方向进行，同时剪除朝向禁区的候选抓取方案。使用冻结的3D部位接地模型从语言指令中定位禁区。

Result: 构建了NegGrasp基准，包含配对的正/负指令及约束感知度量（只有既完成任务又遵守约束的抓取才计为成功）。在该基准上，方法将最强基线的违规率从57.9%降至17.2%，同时提升了约束感知成功率和物理成功率。

Conclusion: 推理时框架能有效解决否约束下的灵巧抓取问题，证明无需重新训练或收集否定样本，仅通过推理时引导与剪枝即可显著降低违规率并提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Language-driven dexterous grasp models, such as DextER, perform well when instructions specify where to grasp, but we find they fail systematically when an instruction also specifies where not to grasp (e.g., "grasp the handle but avoid the body"). Existing training corpora, DexGYSNet among them, contain virtually no avoidance instructions, and collecting examples for every possible constraint is impractical. Moreover, because every part mentioned during training denotes a contact target, models may interpret a forbidden part as another region to grasp rather than one to avoid. We therefore introduce an inference-time framework for negation-constrained dexterous grasping that requires no negation-specific training examples. Combining Sequential Monte Carlo with classifier-free guidance, our method guides sampling toward the instructed part while pruning candidates headed for the forbidden region, without any negation examples during training. A frozen 3D part-grounding model localizes the forbidden region from the language instruction. To evaluate this setting, we construct NegGrasp, a benchmark of paired positive/negative instructions with constraint-aware metrics that credit a grasp only if it both accomplishes the task and respects the stated constraint. On NegGrasp, our method reduces the violation rate of the strongest baseline from 57.9% to 17.2% while improving both constraint-aware and physical success.

</details>


### [358] [A Wearable Pneumatic Device for Continuous, Closed-Loop, Bidirectional Tactile Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00612)
*Cosima du Pasquier,Aliyah Smith,Serin Huber,Joshua Phelps,Ilana A. Cohen,Ava Chen,Monroe Kennedy,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套由两个可穿戴气动触觉设备组成的系统，支持在感知相关力和时间尺度上的连续、闭环、双向触觉交互。每个设备包含多达12个压力传感通道，通道既可作传感器也可作致动器，甚至同时兼具两种功能，并通过分布式架构实现持续压力输出、快速动态响应和无线配对实时传输触觉信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有紧凑型气动触觉平台在力输出带宽、响应速度和压力保持能力方面存在局限，且缺乏统一的双向触觉传感与反馈框架，难以满足遥操作等场景对高效触觉交互的需求。

Method: 设计了一种基于纺织气动袋的多通道可穿戴气动设备，实现传感器与致动器的统一；通过局部闭环控制维持稳定的皮肤压痕，采用分布式架构支持实时无线配对与压力信号传输，并通过设备级表征及人类受试者实验评估系统性能。

Result: 设备力带宽超过30 Hz，阶跃响应快速且阻尼良好，压力保持能力优于先前紧凑平台。人类研究显示，基于压力的指尖反馈可区分力和刚度，在遥操作中将施加压力降低最多23.1%，任务时长缩短最多27.4%，主观脑力负荷降低18.8%，尤其在视觉受限条件下效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过将触觉感知与触觉反馈统一于单一气动模态，该系统为遥操作中的双向触觉交互提供了实用基础，展示了在力反馈精度和操作效率方面的显著优势。

Abstract: We present a system of two wearable pneumatic haptic devices that supports continuous, closed-loop, bidirectional tactile interaction at perceptually relevant force and temporal scales. A single device can contain up to twelve pressure sensing channels connected to textile-based pneumatic pouches. Each channel in a device can be used as a sensor, an actuator, or both. As an actuator with integrated sensing, the channel generates stable skin indentation through local closed-loop control. As a sensor, a channel can be mounted (or worn) on any surface, including on a robot gripper or on the human body, and used to measure touch interactions with the environment or a human user. A distributed architecture supports sustained pressure output, rapid dynamic response, and wireless pairing of identical devices in a system to transmit and reproduce tactile pressure signals in real time.
  Device-level characterization demonstrates force bandwidth exceeding 30 Hz, rapid and well-damped step responses, and extended pressure retention compared to prior compact pneumatic platforms. Human studies show that pressure-based fingertip feedback enables discrimination of force and stiffness, improves teleoperated manipulation by reducing applied pressures by up to 23.1% and task duration by up to 27.4%, and lowers subjective mental workload by 18.8%, particularly under visually constrained conditions. By unifying tactile sensing and haptic feedback within a single pneumatic modality, the device provides a practical foundation for bidirectional touch interaction in teleoperation.

</details>


### [359] [DSG: Dynamic 3D Scene Graph Construction for Embodied Agents in Changing Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00619)
*Ming Liao,Chao Ye,Jianing Fei,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSG，一个动态3D场景图构建框架，通过语义感知的3D高斯场景表示和双视角渲染进行物体变化检测，并结合多粒度视觉上下文的LLM空间关系推理，同时引入基于AI2-THOR的DynTHOR基准。实验表明DSG在物体节点构建和空间关系推理上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内环境中物体位置频繁变化（如人为活动或具身代理交互），导致已构建的场景图与当前场景不一致，现有方法缺乏对动态场景的有效处理。

Method: 1) 构建语义感知的3D高斯场景表示，提出双视角渲染的物体变化检测方法以支持场景图节点可靠更新；2) 提出融合多粒度视觉上下文的空间关系推理方法，利用大语言模型识别更丰富的物体间空间关系；3) 在AI2-THOR仿真平台上构建动态室内场景图基准DynTHOR。

Result: 在DynTHOR、3RScan和真实场景上的大量实验表明，DSG在物体节点构建和空间关系推理方面均持续优于现有方法，显著提升了动态场景图构建的准确性。

Conclusion: DSG框架有效解决了室内动态场景下场景图的更新与空间关系推理问题，验证了其在仿真与真实场景中的泛化能力，为动态场景理解提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In indoor environments, object positions frequently change due to human activities or embodied-agent interactions, causing previously constructed scene graphs to become inconsistent with the current scene. To address this issue, we propose DSG, a dynamic 3D scene graph construction framework that detects object changes and performs spatial relationship reasoning. First, we construct a semantic-aware 3D Gaussian scene representation and develop a dual-view rendering-based object change detection method to enable reliable scene graph node updates. Second, we propose a spatial relationship reasoning method that incorporates multi-granularity visual context, enabling a large language model to identify a richer set of interobject spatial relationships. Furthermore, we introduce DynTHOR, a dynamic indoor scene graph benchmark built on the AI2-THOR simulation platform for evaluating scene graph construction in dynamic environments. Extensive experiments on Dyn-THOR, 3RScan, and real-world scenes demonstrate that DSG consistently outperforms existing methods in both object node construction and spatial relationship reasoning, significantly improving the accuracy of dynamic scene graph construction.

</details>


### [360] [AM-Bench: A Modular Simulation Suite and Benchmark for Aerial Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00641)
*Yutong Wang,Dongjae Lee,Xiaofeng Guo,Yuanzhu Zhan,Yufei Jiang,Bavin Saravanan,Muqing Cao,Jia Xie,Chenyang Mao,Sebastian Scherer,Junyi Geng,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AM-Bench，一个面向多旋翼空中操纵（AM）策略学习的模块化仿真套件与基准，涵盖欠驱动、全驱动和过驱动三种代表性机体，12个接触、运输和受限交互任务，支持可配置的气动扰动和作动器饱和，旨在进行系统级评估而不只是端到端策略性能对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操纵学习基准大多聚焦于地面支撑系统，忽略了空中操纵这一动态关键领域。空中操纵面临环境扰动、操纵器与浮动机体之间的耦合动力学以及受限自由度等独特挑战，任务性能取决于机体设计、底层控制和高级策略的联合作用，但缺乏支持系统级评估的基准平台。

Method: AM-Bench包含模块化仿真套件和基准框架，提供：覆盖欠驱动/全驱动/过驱动系统的代表性机体；12个跨接触、运输和受限交互类别的任务；可配置的气动扰动与作动器饱和度；标准底层控制器；基线策略学习算法。支持对机体设计、控制、扰动与策略选择的交互效应进行系统级诊断评估。

Result: 通过三项仿真研究（高级策略、策略-控制接口、机体设计方案）展示了AM-Bench的诊断价值，并通过真实世界验证了所建模效应以及学习管线的硬件测试。

Conclusion: AM-Bench从系统级视角弥补了现有操纵基准对空中操纵支持不足的空白，能够揭示机体、控制、扰动与策略之间的复杂交互作用，为空中操纵策略学习研究提供了标准化的诊断和评估平台。

Abstract: Standardized benchmarks have played a central role in advancing robot manipulation learning, yet most focus on ground-supported manipulation systems, which limits their applicability to dynamics-critical domains such as aerial manipulation (AM). AM presents distinct system-level challenges, including environmental disturbances, coupled dynamics between the manipulator and floating base, and constrained degrees of freedom. Consequently, task performance depends jointly on robot embodiment, low-level control, and high-level policy design. We introduce AM-Bench, a modular simulation suite and benchmark for multirotor-based AM policy learning. AM-Bench includes representative embodiments spanning underactuated, fully actuated, and overactuated systems, 12 tasks across contact, transport, and constrained interaction, configurable aerodynamic disturbances and actuator saturation, standard low-level controllers, and baseline policy-learning algorithms. Unlike prior manipulation benchmarks that primarily emphasize end-to-end policy performance, AM-Bench enables system-level evaluation of how embodiment, control, disturbances, and policy choices interact. We demonstrate its diagnostic value through three simulation studies spanning high-level policies, policy--control interfaces, and embodiments, together with real-world validation of modeled effects and a hardware test of the learning pipeline.

</details>


### [361] [Human-robot conversation with multiple participants in noisy public spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00648)
*Divesh Lala,Yogeeswaran Muthukumaran,Vincent Fernandes,Kazushi Kato,Shota Fujiki,Zihao Chi,Masaya Iwasaki,Taiken Shintani,Megumi Kawata,Kazuki Sakai,Koji Inoue,Yuicihiro Yoshikawa,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于嘈杂真实环境（如开放公共空间）的音频系统，可在自主机器人（安卓ERICA）和远程操作者化身（移动Teleco机器人）两种场景中使用。系统通过单组多通道麦克风阵列实现多说话人语音增强和空间音频，提供更沉浸式的化身对话体验，并在2025年大阪世博会上进行了概念验证演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嘈杂的真实世界环境（如开放公共空间）中，自主机器人和化身的语音对话系统需要增强的语音信号，既用于语音识别，也用于向远程操作者传输清晰的语音。

Method: 提出一个统一音频系统，可同时服务于两种场景：一是与安卓ERICA配合的专注聆听系统，二是与移动Teleco机器人配合的对话支持系统（其中一个作为远程操作者的化身）。两个场景均支持多方对话，使用单组多通道麦克风阵列，实现多说话人语音增强和空间音频。

Result: 系统不仅能在嘈杂环境中增强多个说话人的语音，还能提供空间音频形式，增强基于化身的对话交互的沉浸感。该系统已在2025年大阪世博会作为概念验证进行演示。

Conclusion: 所提出的音频系统可同时满足机器人语音识别和化身远程语音传输两种需求，通过多通道阵列实现多维语音增强与空间音频，为真实嘈杂环境中的多方人机对话提供了可行方案。

Abstract: For noisy real-world environments such as those in open public spaces, spoken dialogue systems for both autonomous robots and avatars should be carefully designed to provide enhanced speech signals. These signals can be used either for speech recognition or, in the case of an avatar system, transmitted as clean speech to a remote operator. This work proposes an audio system that can be used for both these scenarios and was demonstrated as a proof-of-concept at the 2025 World Expo in Osaka. The first scenario is an attentive listening system with the android ERICA, and the second is a conversation support system with mobile Teleco robots, with one of them acting as an avatar for a remote operator. Both systems feature multi-party conversation and use a single multi-channel microphone array. We describe how our audio system not only enhances the speech of multiple speakers in a noisy environment, but provides a form of spatial audio which allows for more immersiveness in avatar-based conversational interactions.

</details>


### [362] [Fleets Need a Context Plane: Rethinking Cooperative Perception for Autonomous Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00659)
*Liangkai Liu,Xiaoxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文平面"（context plane）接口，用于无人机蜂群协同感知中的运行时自适应。该接口让每架无人机以极低开销（≤1KB@10Hz）发布场景描述符，并通过轻量级可替换策略决定各无人机的计算、共享与融合行为，而无需修改或重训练感知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机协同感知系统的特征共享策略要么在设计时固定，要么仅适应单一上下文信号，无法应对飞行中任务、带宽、编队几何和场景覆盖的动态变化。作者通过实验量化了上下文盲共享的代价：错误策略最高损失7.7 AP，同精度策略在任务区域内可差5.9 AP，表明需联合考虑多个上下文维度。

Method: 提出上下文平面（context plane）这一有界、结构化的运行时上下文接口。每架无人机以不超过1 KB、10 Hz的频率发布描述符；轻量级可替换的策略利用蜂群上下文决定每架无人机计算、共享和融合的内容。现有的共享方案可作为该接口内固定的策略。

Result: 任务感知共享仅使用5-10%的字节即达到与全共享相当的精度。在ROS 2 + Jetson AGX Orin原型中，上下文平面仅占用数据面约0.01%的带宽，每次策略决策耗时0.10 ms。

Conclusion: 显式上下文接口能够在无需修改或重训练感知模型的前提下，以极低开销支持运行时自适应，为无人机蜂群协同感知提供了可扩展的灵活性。

Abstract: Cooperative perception allows a drone fleet to combine observations from multiple viewpoints. However, existing systems typically fix their feature-sharing policies at design time or adapt to only one context signal. This is a poor fit for aerial fleets, whose missions, bandwidth, formation geometry, and scene coverage can change during flight. We quantify the cost of context-blind sharing on UAV3D by controlling feature exchange at evaluation time using a released DiscoNet checkpoint, without retraining. Mission-aware sharing matches full-sharing accuracy while using only 5-10% of the bytes. The best tested peer selection policy changes with the byte budget, and choosing the wrong policy loses up to 7.7 AP. Moreover, under a constrained budget, two policies with the same full-scene accuracy differ by 5.9 AP within the mission region, showing that multiple context axes must be considered jointly. We therefore propose the context plane, a bounded, structured interface for runtime context. Each drone publishes a descriptor of at most 1 KB at 10 Hz, and lightweight, replaceable policies use the fleet context to decide what each drone computes, shares, and fuses. Existing sharing schemes become fixed policies within this interface. In our ROS 2 prototype on a Jetson AGX Orin, the context plane uses approximately 0.01% of the data-plane bandwidth, and each policy decision takes 0.10 ms. These results show that an explicit context interface can support low-overhead runtime adaptation without modifying or retraining the perception model.

</details>


### [363] [Behavior--Realization Separation for Constrained Physical Human--Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00669)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将物理人机交互中的期望行为指定与约束实现分离为两层架构：行为层提供期望接触端口加速度，实现层将其转化为满足约束的机器人指令并显式报告期望-实际加速度误差。通过后退时域二次规划实现无记忆仿射行为，实验验证了阻抗/导纳行为的卸载切换和扭矩可行性约束的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理人机交互软件通常将期望行为指定与约束实现耦合在一起，导致饱和掩盖下的实现误差无法被显式识别，限制了对行为的灵活修改与约束的合理处理。

Method: 将系统分为行为层（提供期望接触端口加速度a_k^id）与实现层（将加速度转化为机器人命令并报告误差）。引入同目标无约束反事实来分离正则化与约束干预，通过后退时域二次规划实现无记忆仿射行为，改变行为只需修改目标系数(C_θ,G_θ)，机器人命令变量与可行集保持不变。

Result: 在MuJoCo中扭矩控制的7自由度Franka FR3上验证：持续20N推力下，松弛工作空间边界保持误差约0.1-0.2mm，相比瞬时裁剪方法的4.4-4.7cm超调。降额执行器预算下，全时域扭矩约束的冻结模型计划误差为2.1×10^-4 N·m，而仅第一步约束的消融实验超预算达11.329 N·m；真实非线性平台上差距缩小但仍优（0.161 vs 0.380 N·m）。

Conclusion: 行为-实现分离架构是可行且有效的：同一运行层可在无需重构的情况下接受阻抗-导纳-阻抗重分配，且全时域约束强制执行在误差报告和可行保持方面均优于瞬时裁剪，为物理人机交互的模块化设计提供了聚焦验证。

Abstract: Physical human--robot interaction software often couples desired-behavior specification with constrained realization; we treat these as separate layers. A \emph{behavior layer} supplies a desired contact-port acceleration $a_k^{\mathrm{id}}=f_θ(e_k,\dot e_k,F_{h,k})$. A \emph{realization layer} converts it into constrained robot commands and reports total desired-versus-realized acceleration error instead of hiding it in saturation. A same-objective unconstrained counterfactual separates regularization from constraint intervention, while plant data expose model error. This paper implements a receding-horizon quadratic program realizing memoryless affine behaviors. Changing the behavior modifies objective coefficients through $(C_θ,G_θ)$ while the robot-command variable and feasible set remain unchanged. A planar study instantiates impedance and admittance; the same running layer accepts an impedance--admittance--impedance reassignment without reconstruction, under its existing rate limit. On a torque-controlled 7-DOF Franka FR3 in MuJoCo, the runtime freezes task-space dynamics per solve and enforces torque feasibility across its horizon. Under a sustained 20~N push, it holds a slack-relaxed workspace boundary to within approximately 0.1--0.2~mm, versus 4.4~cm (impedance) and 4.7~cm (admittance) overshoot from instantaneous clipping. A derated actuator budget then activates the torque constraint: horizon-wide enforcement keeps its frozen-model plan feasible to $2.1\times10^{-4}$~N$\cdot$m, whereas a first-step-only ablation plans up to 11.329~N$\cdot$m beyond budget; on the executed nonlinear plant, where both share the same local-model error, the gap is smaller but still favors horizon-wide enforcement (0.161 vs.\ 0.380~N$\cdot$m). These results are a focused proof of behavior--realization separation.

</details>


### [364] [ADAPT: Agile Diffusion Action Priors for Robust and Steerable Online Text-Driven Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00677)
*Yan Wu,Chenhao Li,Kaifeng Zhao,Gen Li,Marco Hutter,Siyu Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADAPT是一个端到端框架，用于交互式、文本条件的人形全身控制。它通过扩散模型学习动作先验，并结合轻量级残差强化学习策略，实现语言命令驱动的多样化运动技能直接执行、长时间鲁棒性和平滑的提示切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到运动方法通常先生成运动学动作再由单独的跟踪器执行，这种分离式管线无法实现闭环控制，难以应对实时变化的语言指令以及保持平衡、自然运动和流畅转换的需求。

Method: 1) 从文本标注的人形状态-动作轨迹中学习基于扩散的动作先验，实现语言命令直接驱动的多样化运动技能执行；2) 在冻结的扩散控制器之上训练轻量级残差强化学习策略，提升长时间鲁棒性和平滑的命令切换；3) 同一扩散策略可复用为可引导的文本条件运动先验，用于下游任务适应。

Result: 实验表明该方法实现了稳健的语言接地技能执行、平滑的交互式转换以及保持风格的下游控制。

Conclusion: ADAPT展示了端到端闭环语言控制框架在人形全身控制中的可行性，验证了扩散动作先验与残差强化学习结合的方案能同时兼顾技能多样性、鲁棒性和平滑交互，且扩散先验具有良好的可复用性。

Abstract: We present ADAPT, an end-to-end framework for interactive, text-conditioned humanoid whole-body control. Unlike dominant text-to-motion pipelines that generate kinematic motions for a separate tracker, ADAPT solves language control with an end-to-end closed-loop control framework, where the robot must continuously respond to changing commands while maintaining balance, natural motion, and smooth transitions. ADAPT learns a diffusion-based action prior from text-labeled humanoid state-action trajectories, enabling diverse motion skills to be directly executed from language commands. To improve long-horizon robustness and smooth prompt switching, we train a lightweight residual reinforcement learning policy on top of the frozen diffusion controller. We further show that the same diffusion policy can be reused as a steerable text-conditioned motion prior for downstream task adaptation. Experiments demonstrate robust language-grounded skill execution, smooth interactive transitions, and style-preserving downstream control.

</details>


### [365] [Context-Aware Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00682)
*Liangkai Liu,Shuyao Shi,Mingke Wang,Noah T. Curran,Chuan Li,Fan Bai,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文主张将情境-场景因素（context-situational factors）作为下一代智能车辆系统的"一等公民"原则，统一为跨软件栈的共享状态，并系统综述了环境理解、规划控制、安全与网络连接四个领域的情境感知方法，识别出四大技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能车辆需要在精度、延迟、成本和可靠性等严格约束下运行自适应应用，但车辆运行环境复杂、不确定且快速变化，同时计算平台资源受限，传统方法难以满足这些需求，因此需要将情境因素作为系统设计的一等公民。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，对情境感知方法在四个维度（环境理解、规划与控制、安全与安全防护、网联车辆）进行梳理，并通过趋势分析识别关键技术挑战，提出构建统一情境引擎（contextual engine）的愿景。

Result: 识别出构建通用情境引擎的四大技术挑战：多模态情境融合、时序情境建模、罕见事件处理以及协作式情境共享。

Conclusion: 情境感知应作为智能车辆系统设计的第一性原理，通过统一、共享的情境状态来支撑学习、风险评估和闭环控制，从而推动鲁棒且高效的上下文感知车辆应用的发展。

Abstract: Intelligent vehicles increasingly support adaptive applications beyond driving themselves, ranging from context-aware ADAS and automated driving to in-cabin monitoring and fleet management, all under tight requirements on accuracy, latency, cost, and reliability. Meeting these requirements is challenging because vehicles operate in complex, uncertain, and rapidly changing environments while running on resource-constrained computing platforms. This paper argues that context-situational factors that give meaning to sensor signals and constrain decisions-should be treated as a first-class principle for next-generation vehicle systems, and operationalized as a unified, shared state for learning, risk assessment, and closed-loop control across the software stack. We systematically review state-of-the- art (SOTA) context-aware methods spanning (i) environment understanding, (ii) planning and control, (iii) safety and security, and (iv) connected vehicles. Based on a trend analysis of context-aware design, we identify four key technical challenges in building a general contextual engine for future intelligent vehicles: multi-modal context fusion, temporal context modeling, handling rare events, and collaborative context sharing. We hope this survey will motivate the development of robust and efficient context-aware vehicle applications.

</details>


### [366] [One Print, Many Moves: Monolithic Origami-inspired Folding Actuator for Composable Soft Multi-DoF Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00751)
*Jaehyung Jang,Zhenish Zhakypov,Jasmin Elena Palmer,Melissa Klein,Jee-Hwan Ryu,Allison Mariko Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MONORIGAMI，一种基于折纸启发的单片软体折叠执行器设计策略，通过空间编程的刚度各向异性，在保持材料柔顺性的同时实现精确、可重复、可组合的折叠运动。该设计可全3D打印（单材料、单流程、无需组装），每个执行器作为可扩展的运动基元，通过连接和定向多个执行器可机械编程多自由度轨迹，并在三种不同应用领域（VR可穿戴触觉、遥操作操纵杆、水下模块化抓取器）中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软体机器人执行器虽然具有出色的柔顺性，但其不可控的变形导致精度不足，限制了向多自由度系统的扩展。需要一种既能保持材料柔顺性又能实现精确可控运动的设计策略。

Method: 该方法基于空间编程的刚度各向异性设计，利用材料厚度分层的刚度等级，以折纸启发的几何结构（面与折痕）进行图案化，将无约束的软变形转化为精确、可重复、可组合的折叠运动。设计完全通过单材料、单流程3D打印实现，无需组装。每个执行器作为运动基元，通过机械连接和定向编程多自由度轨迹。

Result: 作者用同一基础模块演示了三种3D打印软体多自由度机器人系统：(1)紧凑型4-DoF可穿戴触觉设备用于VR高保真皮肤反馈；(2)3-DoF操纵杆用于遥操作运动觉反馈；(3)可在水下工作的模块化机器人抓取器，具有几何编码的抓取轨迹。这些系统验证了模块在紧凑多轴集成、受控物理交互和跨环境几何编程操作方面的能力。

Conclusion: MONORIGAMI提供了一个通用、可组合、可获取、可靠且可扩展的高精度软体多自由度机器人平台，解决了软体执行器设计和制造中长期存在的局限性。

Abstract: Conventional soft robot actuators excel in compliance, but their uncontrolled deformations compromise accuracy and hinder scaling to multi-degree-of-freedom (DoF) systems. We introduce a MONOlithic ORIGAMI-inspired soft folding actuator design (MONORIGAMI) that establishes a design strategy based on spatially programmed stiffness anisotropy to preserve material compliance along desired folding directions while selectively restricting deformation in unwanted directions. The actuator leverages stiffness tiers based on material thickness, patterned in an origami-inspired geometry with facets and creases, converting unconstrained soft deformation into accurate, repeatable, and composable folding motions without additional reinforcements. The design is fully 3D-printable through a single-material, single-print process that requires no assembly. Each actuator serves as a scalable motion primitive, and linking and orienting multiple actuators mechanically programs multi-DoF trajectories. Using the same fundamental module, we demonstrate three 3D-printed soft multi-DoF robotic systems spanning distinct application domains: (1) a compact 4-DoF wearable haptic device for high-fidelity cutaneous feedback in virtual reality (VR), (2) a 3-DoF joystick for kinesthetic feedback in teleoperation, and (3) a modular robotic gripper capable of underwater operation with geometry-encoded grasp trajectories. These systems demonstrate the module's capabilities for compact multi-axis integration, controlled physical interaction, and geometry-programmed operation across different environments. Together, these results show that MONORIGAMI provides a general, composable, accessible, reliable, and scalable platform for high-precision soft multi-DoF robotics, addressing long-standing limitations in both soft actuator design and fabrication.

</details>


### [367] [A Compact Robotic Finger with 2-DoF MCP Joint Embedding DoF-Selective Passive Continuously Variable Transmission for Wide Force-Speed Operating Range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00769)
*JaeHyung Jang,Jee-Hwan Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有屈曲选择性无源连续可变传动（CVT）的紧凑型二自由度机器人手指，灵感来自人类掌指关节的功能分化。该机构通过将无源CVT选择性分配给屈曲-伸展自由度，同时保留外展-内收自由度的直接传动，实现了力-速度操作范围的显著扩展。实验显示最大输出力放大4.19倍，平均放大3.6倍，并验证了在保持外展-内收功能的同时实现无源传动适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自由度机器人手部在紧凑尺寸下难以同时获得宽泛的力-速度操作范围，而人类掌指关节通过功能分化实现了不同自由度的高效传动差异化，这一仿生学思路可为紧凑型多自由度机器人手提供设计启发。

Method: 提出了一个二自由度机器人手指机构，将力响应型无源CVT嵌入屈曲通路，同时对外展-内收通路保留直接传动。采用输出侧无源CVT，其移动滑轮启发的线缆布线结构使得作用于滑轮的线缆张力产生的合力能根据负载被动增加屈曲力臂和传动比，无需额外执行器、传感器或控制器。

Result: 实验结果表明，在15度至75度的屈曲角度测试范围内，通过力臂适应实现了最大4.19倍的输出力放大和3.6倍的平均放大，显著扩展了可实现力-速度操作范围。灵巧的滚球实验验证了在保持外展-内收功能的同时可实现无源传动适应。

Conclusion: 研究证明了一种可扩展的传动设计策略，能够在不增加执行器、传感器或控制复杂性的前提下，实现紧凑型多自由度机器人手的宽泛力-速度操作范围与灵巧操作功能兼得。

Abstract: This letter presents a compact two-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic finger with a flexion-selective passive continuously variable transmission (CVT) to achieve a wide force-speed operating range. Inspired by the functional differentiation of the human metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint, the proposed mechanism realizes DoF-specific transmission differentiation by selectively assigning passive CVT to the flexion-extension DoF while preserving direct transmission for abduction-adduction. For a wide force-speed operating range, a force-responsive passive CVT is embedded in the flexion pathway, while direct transmission is preserved for the abduction-adduction pathway. To selectively realize transmission adaptation within a multi-DoF MCP mechanism, an output-side passive CVT employing a moving-pulley-inspired wire-routing structure is introduced. The resultant force generated by the wire tensions acting on the pulley that passively increases the flexion moment arm and transmission ratio according to the applied load without additional actuators, sensors, or control. Experimental results demonstrate a maximum output-force amplification of 4.19-fold and a mean amplification of 3.6-fold across the tested flexion angles ranging from 15 degrees to 75 degrees through moment-arm adaptation, thereby substantially expanding the achievable force-speed operating range. Furthermore, dexterous ball-rolling experiments verify that passive transmission adaptation can be achieved while preserving abduction-adduction functionality. These results demonstrate a scalable transmission design strategy for compact multi-DoF robotic hands.

</details>


### [368] [Non-Prehensile Throwing: A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00771)
*Abdullah Mustafa,Ryo Hanai,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Floris Erich,Ryoichi Nakajo,Yukiyasu Domae,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的机器人非抓取式投掷方法，利用滑动和滚动接触模式直接优化关节空间轨迹，无需解析接触模型或自定义参数化。在仿真中实现99%成功率，并零样本部署到UR5e真实机器人上，实现97%的真实世界成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非抓取式投掷方法依赖于基于模型的优化，使用简化接触模型（如动态抓取）和低维轨迹参数化，限制了解决方案质量和可达工作空间。需要一种能够处理大、重、易变形物体的更优投掷方法。

Method: 1) 将投掷问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），以目标、物体模型和初始配置为条件演化机器人关节状态；2) 离线以低控制率规划关节加加速度轨迹，再上采样为平滑的高速率速度指令；3) 通过最小加加速度系统辨识减小机器人动力学差距（sim-to-real）；4) 训练不确定性感知策略以缓解物体建模误差，特别是对动摩擦的敏感性。

Result: 仿真中策略在数千种配置下实现99%成功率并能泛化到未见物体；敏感性分析显示对质量不确定性鲁棒但对动摩擦高度敏感。真实部署在UR5e上（末端速度达5 m/s），能投掷重型（790g）、大型（20x20x28cm）物体，目标距离可达350cm或高度180cm，真实世界成功率为97%。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了强化学习结合滑动/滚动接触模式能够实现高质量的非抓取式投掷，显著扩展了投掷工作空间，并且通过适当的系统辨识和不确定性感知训练可以有效地从仿真迁移到真实世界。

Abstract: Robotic throwing enables fast object transport and extends a robot's reachable workspace beyond traditional pick-and-place. While prehensile (grasp-based) throwing works well for graspable items, non-prehensile (grasp-free) throwing is better suited for large, heavy, and/or deformable objects. Existing approaches rely on model-based optimization with simplified contact models (e.g., dynamic grasping) and low-dimensional trajectory parameterizations, which limit solution quality and reachable workspace. We propose a reinforcement learning approach that additionally leverages sliding and rolling contact modes and directly optimizes joint-space trajectories without analytical contact models or custom parameterizations. The Markov Decision Process (MDP) is formulated as a dynamical system that evolves the robot's joint state conditioned on the throwing target, object model, and initial configuration. Joint-jerk trajectories are planned offline at a low control rate and upsampled into smooth, high-rate velocity commands for deployment. For sim-to-real transfer, we minimize the robot-dynamics gap through minimum-jerk system identification and train uncertainty-aware policies to mitigate object-modeling errors, particularly sensitivity to dynamic friction. In simulation, the policy achieves 99% success across thousands of configurations and generalizes to unseen objects. Sensitivity analysis shows robustness to mass uncertainty but high sensitivity to dynamic friction, consistent with the sliding-based release mechanism. Deployed zero-shot on a UR5e operating near its physical limits (5 m/s end-effector velocity), our method throws diverse objects including heavy (790 g) and large (20x20x28 cm) items to targets up to 350 cm distance or 180 cm elevation, achieving a 97% real-world success rate.

</details>


### [369] [Connectivity-Aware Graph Extension for Decentralized Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00804)
*Béatrice Garcia Cegarra,Elena Vanneaux,Quentin Picard,David Filliat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前沿连通性的探索图扩展策略，用于多无人机在间歇性通信条件下的去中心化未知环境探索，通过维持区域划分的稳定性和鲁棒性，在低通信率下提升了探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机在未知环境探索中面临间歇性通信的挑战，去中心化操作是基本假设。如何在通信受限条件下协调多架无人机并保持区域划分稳定，是一个关键问题。

Method: 在去中心化框架下，提出一种基于前沿连通性（frontier connectivity）的新型探索图扩展策略，用于扩展探索计划并维持代理之间的区域划分，使其对断连和空间布局变化具有稳定性与鲁棒性。该方法应用于两种最先进的区域划分方法。

Result: 在仿真实验中，与现有图扩展方法相比，该方法在低通信率条件下表现出更高的探索效率，性能优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 基于前沿连通性的探索图扩展策略能够有效提升多无人机在间歇性通信环境下的去中心化探索性能，验证了该方法在低通信率下相对现有方法的优越性。

Abstract: Exploring unknown environments with multiple UAVs requires coordination under intermittent communication, making decentralized operation a baseline assumption. We propose, within a decentralized framework, a novel exploration graph extension strategy based on frontier connectivity to extend exploration plans and maintain area partitioning among agents stable and robust to disconnections and changes in spatial layout. The proposed extension method is applied to two state-of-the-art area partitioning methods and evaluated in simulation. Experiments show improved performance over existing graph extension approaches with higher exploration efficiency under low communication rate.

</details>


### [370] [Peg-in-Bench: A Modular Benchmark for High-Precision Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00906)
*Yosel Delgado,José G. Buenaventura-Carreón,Floris Erich,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个可重构的销孔插入基准测试平台，由3D可打印的模块化组件组成，支持多种销钉几何形状、公差等级和可配置基座结构，用于系统评估高精度插入操作的任务泛化能力，并提供场景生成工具以确保可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有销孔插入基准通常依赖固定任务配置，无法有效评估机器人插入操作在不同场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，缺乏系统化的泛化评测手段。

Method: 设计了一套完全3D可打印的模块化基准组件，包含多种销钉几何形状、公差等级和可配置基座结构，通过改变物体布局、方向和任务结构来生成多样化的插入与装配任务，同时提供场景生成工具和机器可读的任务描述以实现标准化和可复现。

Result: 该基准能够组合生成大量多样的插入和装配任务，在保持受控物理条件的同时支持对未见场景适应能力的系统评估；组件STL文件和场景生成工具已在项目仓库中公开。

Conclusion: 该可重构基准为高精度插入任务提供了系统化评估泛化能力的标准化平台，填补了现有固定配置基准在泛化评测方面的不足，并开源了全部工具与组件以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: High-precision insertion remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation due to the strict alignment requirements and contact-rich interactions involved. Although peg-in-hole tasks are widely used for evaluation, existing bench- marks often rely on fixed task configurations, limiting their ability to assess robustness and generalization across different insertion scenarios. This paper introduces a reconfigurable peg-in-hole benchmark designed to evaluate task generalization in high-precision insertion. The benchmark consists of a set of fully 3D-printable modular components, including multiple peg geometries, tolerance levels, and configurable base structures that can be combined to generate a large variety of insertion and assembly tasks. By varying object layouts, orientations, and task structures while maintaining controlled physical conditions, the benchmark enables systematic evaluation of adaptation to unseen scenarios. To support reproducibility, we additionally provide a scenario generation tool capable of producing standardized task configurations and machine-readable task descriptions. The scenario generation tool and the STL files of the benchmark pieces are available through the project repository: https://github.com/aistairc/peg-in-bench.

</details>


### [371] [Knowing When to Stop: Adaptive Action Chunking via Internal Cross-Attention Dynamics in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00908)
*Runze Xu,Xiaolong Shan,Shuang Dai,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于交叉注意力熵的自适应动作分块截断机制，通过检测预测视界中交叉注意力熵的持续高平台，在推理时动态选择执行步长，无需额外训练，在π0.5和X-VLA上超越固定与自适应分块基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA框架中固定动作分块长度在效率与精度之间存在两难：短分块需频繁推理且易产生振荡，长分块则可能与最新观测状态失准，缺乏自适应执行视界的机制。

Method: 观测到动作专家中动作到观测的交叉注意力熵随预测视界延长而升高并趋于平台，该模式与高预测误差相关。据此提出无需训练的截断机制，利用策略已计算的注意力权重，在推理中检测持续高熵平台并动态选择执行视界。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及三个真实世界操作任务上对π0.5和X-VLA进行评估，平均任务成功率优于固定视界和自适应分块基线，同时保持高效的闭环控制。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力动态可作为VLA中自适应动作执行的有效内部信号，为在线长度选择提供了直观且计算开销极小的解决方案。

Abstract: Action chunking is a standard execution strategy in modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks, but fixed execution horizons impose a trade-off between efficiency and accuracy. Short chunks require frequent inference and may cause oscillatory behavior, whereas long chunks can become misaligned with newly observed states. We address this limitation with an adaptive action chunking approach based on internal cross-attention dynamics in the action expert. We observe that, as the prediction horizon extends, action-to-observation cross-attention becomes increasingly dispersed and its entropy rises toward a plateau. This pattern is associated with higher action prediction error and provides an online signal that the current observation offers limited grounding for further open-loop execution. Based on this observation, we introduce a training-free truncation mechanism that detects sustained high-entropy plateaus and dynamically selects the execution horizon during inference. The method uses attention weights already computed by the policy and introduces negligible additional overhead. Evaluations on $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and three real-world manipulation tasks show improved average task success over fixed-horizon and adaptive chunking baselines, while preserving efficient closed-loop control. These results show that cross-attention dynamics can provide a practical internal signal for adaptive action execution in VLAs.

</details>


### [372] [VerNav: Verifier-First Low-Latency Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00920)
*Zhixin Wang,Chengzheyi Yao,Leyuan Liu,Xiaosong Zhang,Yongzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VerNav框架，通过验证器优先的方式降低LLM-based VLN的决策延迟，将逐步自回归生成替换为批量动作验证，并采用熵自适应生成器和两阶段对齐策略提升导航性能，在R2R基准上延迟降低超10倍且性能与代表性方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN任务需要智能体根据自然语言指令在3D环境中导航，显式推理虽能改善指令理解与语义接地，但逐步自回归生成导致多步导航中累积大量决策延迟，亟需低延迟解决方案。

Method: 提出VerNav验证器优先框架：(1)验证器替代逐步自回归生成，采用批量动作验证降低决策延迟；(2)熵驱动的自适应生成器仅在不确定决策时被调用以产生状态证据；(3)两阶段对齐方案：VPO用于静态验证器训练中局部动作偏好对齐，步骤级强化微调在动态任务执行中提供密集进度奖励。

Result: 在Room-to-Room基准上，VerNav的验证器决策路径在代表性LLM-based VLN智能体中取得有竞争力的导航性能，同时将每步平均决策阶段LLM延迟比自回归方法降低10倍以上。

Conclusion: 验证器优先的思路能够在保持导航性能的同时大幅降低LLM推理延迟，为LLM-based VLN的实时应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate through unseen 3D environments according to natural-language instructions. Explicit reasoning can improve instruction understanding and semantic grounding, but autoregressive generation at every step accumulates large decision-stage latency over multi-step navigation. We propose VerNav, a verifier-first framework for low-latency LLM-based VLN. The verifier reduces decision-stage latency by replacing per-step autoregressive generation with batched action verification, while an entropy-based adaptive generator is invoked only for uncertain decisions to produce compact state evidence. To further improve navigation performance with the verifier, we introduce a two-stage alignment scheme: (i) VPO improves local action-preference alignment in static verifier training, and (ii) step-level reinforcement fine-tuning provides dense progress rewards over multi-step navigation rollouts during dynamic task execution. Experiments on the Room-to-Room (R2R) benchmark show that the verifier-only decision path of VerNav achieves competitive navigation performance among representative LLM-based VLN agents while reducing average decision-stage LLM latency per step by more than $10\times$ compared with autoregressive methods.

</details>


### [373] [ProxPI: Proximal Prior Injection for Sampling-Based MPC under Learned-Prior Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00941)
*Euncheol Im,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Proximal Prior Injection (ProxPI)方法，在MPPI控制中保留名义中心采样，通过软邻近代价将学习策略融入优化器，解决策略引导MPPI在分布外任务中因将采样分布集中于策略输出而性能退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略引导MPPI方法采用策略中心的暖启动方式，将采样分布集中于策略输出。当策略先验不匹配（分布外任务）时，这种集中采样会限制解空间探索，阻碍优化器向任务最优解恢复，导致性能严重退化。

Method: ProxPI方法保留名义中心（nominal-centered）MPPI采样框架，不再将采样中心设为策略输出，而是通过一个软邻近代价（soft proximity cost）将策略信息引入优化目标中。理论上证明了重新居中于先验会丢弃优化器每次更新的修正量，而名义中心采样能保留该修正量并收敛到由任务代价和先验共同决定的解集。

Result: ProxPI在分布内任务中与现有先验注入方案性能相当，在分布外任务中能摆脱不准确的策略并恢复至vanilla MPPI级别的性能。理论分析表明重新居中于先验的问题不会通过增加rollout预算来解决。仿真和真实机器人实验均验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过保留名义中心采样并引入软邻近代价，既能享受学习策略带来的分布内性能提升，又能在策略失配时保持优化器的自我修正能力，实现分布内外的稳健表现。

Abstract: Combining learned policies with model predictive control can leverage learned task priors while retaining online adaptation to new objectives and constraints, but performance degrades when the policy is out of distribution. In policy-guided model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a policy-centered warm-start approach centers the sampling distribution on the policy output. When the prior is mismatched, centering the sampling distribution on the policy output restricts exploration around an unsuitable solution and prevents recovery toward the task optimum. We propose Proximal Prior Injection (ProxPI), which retains nominal-centered MPPI sampling and incorporates the policy through a soft proximity cost. This matches the in-distribution performance of existing prior-injection schemes while enabling the optimizer to escape an inaccurate policy and recover vanilla MPPI-level performance. We theoretically show that re-centering on the prior discards the optimizer's correction at every update, whereas nominal-centered sampling retains it and converges to a solution set by both the task cost and the prior, and that this failure is not removed by a larger rollout budget. Simulations and real-robot experiments demonstrate robust performance under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [374] [HitMem: Hierarchical Temporal 3D Memory with Multi-Modal Context-Aware Retrieval for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00950)
*Ruijie Tang,Chenye Zou,Guoquan Wu,Jun Wei,Wei Chen,Jiaxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HitMem是一个面向动态环境的层次化时序3D记忆框架，通过轻量拓扑图统一语义与空间信息，结合时间衰减机制和跨模态上下文感知检索，有效解决对象位移导致的记忆-观测冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D记忆框架大多假设静态世界，当对象因人类活动或未观测事件发生位移时，智能体会遭遇记忆与观测冲突，且常需昂贵的几何重算或低效的全局重探索，难以支撑动态环境中的长期任务执行。

Method: HitMem包含三个核心组件：1)轻量拓扑图统一语义与空间信息并捕获支撑关系；2)时间衰减机制动态调节记忆活跃度以缓解过时表征影响；3)多模态上下文感知检索机制，默认利用语义、空间和时间特征过滤候选，检测到位移时激活两阶段检索（结合外部智能体轨迹的空间约束与基于类别亲和性的语义常识）以高效定位高概率候选区域。

Result: 在构建的Dyna-THOR基准上，HitMem显著提升了对象重定位准确率，降低了探索成本，并提升了动态环境中的任务执行性能。

Conclusion: HitMem通过层次化时序记忆设计与跨模态检索机制，为具身智能体在动态环境中的长期任务执行提供了一种高效且鲁棒的3D场景记忆解决方案。

Abstract: Executing long-term tasks in dynamic environments requires embodied agents to maintain robust and adaptive 3D scene representations. However, most existing 3D memory frameworks rely on static world assumptions. When objects are displaced by human activities or unobserved events, agents encounter memory-observation conflicts and often require costly geometric recomputations or inefficient global re-exploration. To address this, we propose HitMem, a hierarchical temporal 3D memory framework with a multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism. Through continuous perception, HitMem unifies semantic and spatial information into a lightweight topological graph that captures support relationships, while a temporal decay mechanism dynamically regulates memory activeness to mitigate the impact of stale representations. In addition, the multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism defaults to filtering candidates using integrated semantic, spatial, and temporal memory features, and activates a specialized two-stage retrieval process when object displacement is detected. This process combines spatial constraints inferred from external agent trajectories with semantic common sense grounded in class affinities, efficiently identifying high-probability candidate regions. Extensive evaluations on our constructed Dyna-THOR benchmark demonstrate that HitMem significantly improves object relocation accuracy, reduces exploration costs, and enhances task execution performance in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [375] [Accelerating Reinforcement Learning via MPC Solver-Gradient Guidance for Weights-varying MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01061)
*Baha Zarrouki,Arslan Thobani,Jasper Hoffmann,Mattia Piccinini,Rudolf Reiter,Felix Jahncke,Sébastien Gros,Davide Scaramuzza,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-RL方法，将可微MPC求解器导数作为辅助引导信号注入强化学习，用于在线自适应MPC代价函数权重，在提高样本效率的同时保证闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC中固定代价函数权重在条件变化时表现次优，需要上下文相关的在线自适应。然而学习这种自适应策略存在偏差-方差权衡：RL基于环境样本优化闭环回报但样本效率低，而基于梯度的策略学习（GB-PL）利用可微MPC的求解器梯度但存在模型失配偏差。

Method: 提出SG-RL（Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning），在RL框架中保留采样闭环回报作为目标函数，同时使用有界的求解器派生梯度作为辅助引导信号。在PPO中实例化了四种模块化算法，分别将求解器梯度引导注入Actor更新缩放、策略损失、优势估计和值函数学习中。

Result: 在两个全尺寸自动驾驶赛车平台上，在存在有意模型失配的条件下，SG-RL以最多70.6%更少的样本达到PPO的最佳闭环回报，较GB-PL基线在闭环回报上至少提升54%，并能零样本泛化到未见过的环境。

Conclusion: SG-RL成功地将可微求解器梯度和RL各自的优点结合起来，在保持闭环return目标的鲁棒性（低偏差）的同时获得了求解器梯度的低方差特性（高样本效率），有效解决了MPC代价函数自适应学习中的偏差-方差困境。

Abstract: In Model Predictive Control (MPC), cost-function weights shape closed-loop behavior, yet changing conditions often make fixed parametrizations suboptimal and motivate context-dependent online adaptation. Learning such policies is difficult because behavior depends implicitly on numerical MPC solutions, producing nonlinear, potentially nonsmooth, long-horizon dependencies on policy parameters. This creates a bias-variance tradeoff: Reinforcement Learning (RL) optimizes realized closed-loop return from environment samples but is sample-inefficient, whereas Gradient-Based Policy Learning (GB-PL) uses low-variance solver gradients from differentiable MPC to optimize surrogate losses on predicted trajectories but can be biased under model mismatch. We propose Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning (SG-RL), a solver-sensitivity augmentation for RL-based online MPC cost-weight adaptation. SG-RL keeps sampled closed-loop return as the objective and uses bounded solver-derived gradients as auxiliary guidance to improve stability and sample efficiency. We instantiate SG-RL in Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with four modular algorithms that inject solver-gradient guidance into actor-update scaling, policy loss, advantage estimation, and value-function learning. On two full-scale autonomous racing platforms with intentional model mismatch, SG-RL reaches PPO's best closed-loop return with up to 70.6% fewer samples, outperforms GB-PL baselines by at least 54% in closed-loop return, and generalizes zero-shot to unseen environments.

</details>


### [376] [Adaptive Depth-Map-Guided Bundle Adjustment for Correspondence-Free Multi-View Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01089)
*Yiran Zhou,Yingyu Wang,Shoudong Huang,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应分层深度图引导的捆绑调整框架（ADM-BA），用于无特征对应的多视角点云配准。该方法以全局2.5-D网格表示场景，通过软最大分层分配处理多表面深度冲突，联合优化传感器位姿与分层深度图，适用于不规则钢废料切割场景中平滑金属表面的三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则钢废料加工需要密集3-D测量以替代人工目视评估，但现有基于特征提取和数据关联的多视角配准方法在面对平滑金属表面、重复结构、遮挡和部分重叠时容易产生错误对应关系，导致位姿估计不准确和重建失真，进而影响下游的尺寸估计、边界几何分析及切割路径规划。

Method: 提出自适应分层深度图引导的捆绑调整框架：(1) 场景由全局2.5-D网格表示，各单元自适应维护多个深度假设；(2) 原始深度观测直接投影到全局地图形成深度约束，无需显式特征对应；(3) 对多表面深度冲突的网格单元，采用softmax分层分配将观测关联到兼容的深度假设；(4) 通过非线性最小二乘公式联合优化传感器位姿与分层深度图，对应关系由深度图表示与投影模型隐式诱导。

Result: 在自采集工业数据集上的实验表明，该方法在具有挑战性的工业场景中实现了持续竞争力的重建精度，同时保持鲁棒性并具有较低的计算成本。

Conclusion: 该无对应多视角配准方法有效解决了光滑金属表面及复杂工业环境下传统特征匹配易失效的问题，并通过分层深度表示与联合优化兼顾了精度、鲁棒性和计算效率，且已开源代码供研究者使用。

Abstract: Robotic processing of irregular steel scrap requires dense 3-D measurement to replace manual visual assessment in hazardous cutting workcells. The reconstructed map is used to estimate piece dimensions, boundary geometry, feasible preheating and cutting regions, and collision-aware torch paths. The reconstruction errors therefore propagate directly to downstream measurement and planning. Existing multi-view registration methods commonly rely on feature extraction and data association to establish correspondences between views. In workcells with smooth metallic surfaces, repeated structures, occlusions, and partial overlaps, however, wrong correspondences may be established, leading to inaccurate pose estimation and distorted reconstruction. This paper presents an adaptive layered depth-map-guided bundle adjustment framework for correspondence-free multi-view point cloud registration. The scene is represented by a global 2.5-D grid, where each cell can adaptively maintain multiple depth hypotheses. Raw depth observations are directly projected into the global map to form depth constraints without explicit feature correspondences. At grid cells where multiple surfaces produce conflicting depths, a softmax-based layer assignment links each observation to compatible depth hypotheses. The resulting nonlinear least-squares formulation jointly refines sensor poses and the layered depth map, with correspondences implicitly induced by the depth-map representation and projection model. Experiments on self-collected industrial datasets show that the proposed method achieves consistently competitive reconstruction accuracy while maintaining robustness and low computational cost in challenging industrial scenarios. We release the open-source code implementation at: https://github.com/YiranZhou-Robotics/ADM-BA.git

</details>


### [377] [DNC-IMM: Early Lane-Change Intention Recognition via Neural Calibration Based on Driving Context Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01120)
*Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双神经校准交互多模型（DNC-IMM），通过神经网络同时校准转移概率矩阵和测量似然，在不破坏传统IMM概率结构和可解释性的前提下，实现更早、更可靠的换道意图识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶中，提前识别换道意图对主动决策至关重要，传统IMM方法虽具有概率结构和可解释性，但对驾驶情境的适应性不足，难以兼顾准确性与早期识别能力。

Method: 构建DNC-IMM框架，将驾驶情境信息（目标车辆运动、周围车辆间隙、相对速度等）输入神经网络，校准IMM的转移概率矩阵和测量似然；最终通过校准后的IMM模式后验概率确定意图类别，而非采用独立的直接分类器。

Result: 在highD数据集上的实验表明，所提方法能在车道穿越前可靠地识别换道意图，尤其在更早的2-3秒预测时域上表现出突出优势。

Conclusion: DNC-IMM通过双神经网络校准机制增强了模型对驾驶情境的适应性，在保持传统IMM可解释性的同时显著提升了早期换道意图识别的可靠性。

Abstract: Early recognition of lane-change intention is essential for proactive decision-making in autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems.
